# MCP Server (Roger 0.25.0 "I know kung fu") Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** A local agent (Claude Code or any MCP client) starts `roger-mcp` from inside Roger.app, sees exactly which log entries, meetings and file transcripts are new since its last visit, ingests them and confirms with `ack`, while Roger itself does not have to run. Roger sets this up from a new Agents settings tab: skill install in detected harnesses, MCP registration through their CLIs and a Claude Desktop extension (#7).

**Architecture:** A new static library `RogerKit` (Foundation only) holds everything the app and the helper share: the library layout and item reader (moved out of the app), an append-only change log (`<library>/.roger/changes.jsonl`, `flock` + `O_APPEND`), consumer bookmarks, a TOON encoder, the `get_changes` query and the six tool handlers. The app writes change events from its writers and from the library window. The command-line tool `roger-mcp` wraps the handlers in the official Swift MCP SDK (`StdioTransport`) and is embedded in `Roger.app/Contents/MacOS`. The Agents tab is thin SwiftUI over tested plain types (harness detection, skill installer, CLI runner, `.mcpb` builder).

**Tech Stack:** Swift 6 (strict concurrency), SwiftUI, AppKit, Foundation (`flock`, `open`, `Process`), CryptoKit (SHA-256), modelcontextprotocol/swift-sdk 0.12.1 (product `MCP`), XcodeGen 2.46.0, Xcode 27, Swift Testing, `/usr/bin/ditto`, macOS 15+ on Apple Silicon.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-13-roger-mcp-design.md` (binding). Background decisions: Crystalline engram `jordi/projects/roger/roger-mcp-support-planning`. Issue #7.

## Global Constraints

From the spec (verbatim values):

- Platform: "Local stdio only, macOS 15+ on Apple Silicon only. No HTTP, no OAuth, no Windows/Linux/x86_64 variants."
- Out of scope: "HTTP transport, OAuth, live actions (start recording, notifications), MCP resources and subscriptions, word-level diffs, tracking external file edits, log compaction."
- Log storage: `<library root>/.roger/changes.jsonl` (append-only, one event per line) and `<library root>/.roger/consumers/<name>.json` (`{"seq": 42, "updatedAt": "2026-10-13T18:02:11+02:00"}`).
- Event: `{"seq":42,"at":"2026-10-13T14:32:41+02:00","event":"created","kind":"log","key":"Logs/2026/10/2026-10-13 14-32-05","path":"Logs/2026/10/2026-10-13 14-32-05.md"}`. `seq` monotonic from 1, `at` RFC 3339 with local offset, `event` is `created`, `updated` or `deleted`, `kind` is `log`, `meeting` or `transcript`.
- "Writers call `append` only after their file is safely on disk. A failed append is logged as a warning. The item is caught up on the next start."
- "Reconcile does not detect edits or deletions done outside Roger (out of scope)."
- "All output is text. Lists are TOON. Documents are Markdown."
- Tools: `status`, `get_changes`, `ack`, `get_item`, `list_items`, `search`. Only `ack` is not readOnly (it is idempotent).
- "Consumer names: `^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{0,63}$`."
- "No tool takes a file path. No tool deletes anything."
- `get_changes` size: "about 30,000 characters, then cut at the item boundary", "With `summary: true` the limit is 300,000 characters."
- `list_items` limit default 50, max 500. `search` limit default 20, max 100.
- Library lookup: "1. `--library <path>` 2. `CFPreferencesCopyAppValue("libraryRoot", "com.jordiboehme.roger")` 3. `~/Documents/Roger/`".
- "stdout carries MCP only. Logs go to `os.Logger`, and to stderr with `--verbose`."
- "Server name `roger`, version from the enclosing Roger.app Info.plist. `roger-mcp --version` prints it."
- "Only writes: consumer bookmark files."
- Bundle: `Roger.app/Contents/MacOS/roger-mcp`, signed with hardened runtime and the same team. Cask: `binary "#{appdir}/Roger.app/Contents/MacOS/roger-mcp"`.
- "Roger never writes another app's config file."
- Skill source `skills/ingest-roger/SKILL.md`, bundled as a resource, "copy (never a link)", receipt `~/Library/Application Support/Roger/skill-installs.json` "treated as untrusted: Roger only ever touches `ingest-roger` inside the known skills folders". "Never updates on its own."
- Claude Desktop row "Shown when `/Applications/Claude.app` exists."

Project rules:

- Work on branch `feat/mcp-server` in `/Users/jordi/git/roger` (already checked out). Do not switch branches.
- macOS 15.0 deployment target, Swift 6 strict concurrency with no new warnings, `@MainActor` for `@Observable` types SwiftUI observes. arm64 only (release builds pass `ARCHS=arm64`; do not add x86_64 anywhere).
- Logging via `os.Logger` with subsystem `com.jordiboehme.roger`. `roger-mcp` never calls `print` while serving.
- Commits: plain conventional-commit messages. NEVER add `Co-Authored-By`, "Generated with Claude Code" or any AI attribution.
- User-facing strings (UI text, alerts, tool descriptions, server instructions, SKILL.md, README, script messages): no em-dash "—" or en-dash "–" (use "-"), no Oxford comma ("A, B and C"), short plain sentences. Author name is "Jordi Böhme".
- Debug builds: `cd /Users/jordi/git/roger/Roger && xcodebuild -project Roger.xcodeproj -scheme Roger -configuration Debug build 2>&1 | tail -20`. Never pass `CONFIGURATION_BUILD_DIR` for Debug.
- App tests (hosted in Roger.app): `cd /Users/jordi/git/roger/Roger && xcodebuild test -project Roger.xcodeproj -scheme Roger -destination 'platform=macOS' 2>&1 | tail -40`. Add `-only-testing:RogerTests/<SuiteName>` for one suite. This scheme also runs RogerKitTests (Task 1).
- RogerKit tests (no host app): `cd /Users/jordi/git/roger/Roger && xcodebuild test -project Roger.xcodeproj -scheme RogerKitTests -destination 'platform=macOS' 2>&1 | tail -40`. Add `-only-testing:RogerKitTests/<SuiteName>` for one suite.
- New files need the project regenerated. After every `xcodegen generate`: run `git -C /Users/jordi/git/roger checkout -- Roger/Resources/Roger.entitlements` (xcodegen empties it), check `git -C /Users/jordi/git/roger diff --stat Roger/Resources/Roger.entitlements` prints nothing, and commit the regenerated `Roger/Roger.xcodeproj` (tracked, including `xcshareddata/xcschemes/*.xcscheme`).
- Tests must NEVER construct `AppState` or `AppCoordinator`, never read or write `UserDefaults.standard`, never touch `~/Documents`, `~/.claude`, `~/.agents` or any real harness folder, never call `ExternalTranscriptRegistry.standard`, never run a real harness CLI, never move anything to the real Trash and never open Claude Desktop. Use temp folders (`TempFolder.make()` in RogerTests, `TempDir.make()` in RogerKitTests) and injected roots, homes, runners, clocks and closures.
- `AGENTS.md` is in `.gitignore`. Edit it in place (Task 11), never `git add` it.
- Do not bump `MARKETING_VERSION` and do not write release notes. The release is a separate step.
- Library files are the user's data. Nothing in this plan deletes or overwrites an item file. The change log is append-only. Roger writes only into `<root>/.roger/`, the skills folders' `ingest-roger/` and its own Application Support folder.
- Pinned dependency: `modelcontextprotocol/swift-sdk` `exactVersion: "0.12.1"`. FluidAudio 0.17.7 and KeyboardShortcuts 3.1.0 stay as they are (already the latest releases, see Verified facts).

## Review Focus

1. Two writers append to `changes.jsonl` at the same moment (a Debug and a Release Roger, or the app's launch reconcile racing a log entry) and two `roger-mcp` processes `ack` the same consumer at the same moment: seqs must stay unique and gap-free, and the bookmark must end at the highest `upTo`, never lower. (Task 3: `twoHandlesNeverShareASeq`, `concurrentAcksEndAtTheHighest`, `appendAfterAHalfLineStaysParseable`.)
2. The library root is changed in Roger while an agent has a bookmark and `roger-mcp` is already running: the next tool call must read the new library (not the old one), the new library must be filled on the change, and a consumer starts there at 0 instead of skipping items. (Task 5: `rootChangeFillsTheNewLibrary`; Task 8: `followsALibraryRootChangeWithoutRestart`.)
3. A harness CLI hangs, waits for input or asks a question (first-run login, a trust prompt): Install must come back within the timeout and show the manual command, never freeze the tab. (Task 13: `hangingCommandTimesOut`, `stdinIsClosed`, `timedOutRegistrationShowsTheCommand`.)
4. The user edits the installed `SKILL.md`, presses Update, edits again and presses Update again: both edits must survive as backups, no backup may be overwritten, and "changed by you" must win over "outdated". (Task 12: `secondUpdateKeepsTheFirstBackup`, `changedByYouWinsOverOutdated`.)
5. macOS denies `roger-mcp` access to `~/Documents` (TCC answer "Don't Allow" for the terminal or for Claude Desktop): every tool must say how to grant Files and Folders access, not "Start Roger once". (Task 8: `deniedAccessExplainsFilesAndFolders`.)

Covered by spec-mandated tests, listed so reviewers know they were checked: Roger.app moved after Add to Claude Desktop (Task 14 `movedAppShowsWarning`) and after a CLI registration (Task 13 `otherPathMeansReRegister`); a translocated app (Task 10 `translocatedBundleIsRefused`).

## Rulings

Where the spec is silent, unverifiable or wrong after research, these rulings apply. Reviewers: treat them as part of the spec.

1. **Re-export.** RogerKit is a static library. The app has one file with `@_exported import RogerKit`, so app files and `@testable import Roger` tests compile unchanged (checked in a probe project with Xcode 27.0 and XcodeGen 2.46.0). RogerTests does not link RogerKit itself (a second static copy would duplicate the types).
2. **What moves.** Declarations that move to RogerKit become `public` (`private` stays private). `LibraryItemReader` no longer reaches into meeting code: `SessionFolderName` (folder-name date, display date) and `MeetingMarkersSummary` (start date and marker count from `markers.json`) replace `LibraryMigration.sessionDate`, `MeetingTranscriptWriter.displayDate` and `MeetingCheckpointStore.load` there. `MeetingTrackSource` and `MeetingTrackEncodeOutcome` move to their own app file because they use `AudioSegmentConcatenator`.
3. **Handlers without MCP.** The six tool handlers live in RogerKit and take `[String: ToolValue]`, returning `ToolOutput(text:isError:)`. Only `roger-mcp` imports `MCP` and converts `Value` at the boundary. Roger.app never links swift-sdk or swift-nio.
4. **External keys.** Items outside the library root (transcripts next to their source and items in extra scan folders, for example `~/Documents/Roger Recordings/`) get `External/<name>-<hash8>`. `name` is the file name without `.md` (meetings: the session folder name), `hash8` the first 8 lowercase hex digits of the SHA-256 of the canonical absolute path (meetings: of the session folder). `path` is the canonical absolute path of the Markdown file. `roger-mcp` also reads Roger's `extraScanFolders` preference.
5. **Unfinished meetings** (session folder without `transcript.md`) are not change-log items and are not listed by `list_items` or `search`.
6. **One upsert for writers.** Writers record "this item was written": `created` when the key never appeared or its last event is `deleted`, else `updated`. A first meeting transcript is `created`, a retry `updated`.
7. **`deleted`** is recorded only for keys whose last event is not `deleted`. Moving extra files of a meeting to the Trash (`LibraryExtraFilesSection`) records nothing, because the meeting's Markdown did not change. A log's `.m4a` is not an item.
8. **Broken lines.** Readers skip every line that does not parse, not only the last one. Before appending, the writer adds `\n` when the file does not end with one, so a half line left by a crash stays one bad line and the new event parses.
9. **Whole-file read under the lock.** Every locked change-log operation reads the whole file (about 250 bytes per event). No tail index, no cache.
10. **`since` forms.** A seq means events with a seq greater than it (so "pass the previous `upTo` as `since`" works). A date (`YYYY-MM-DD`, local midnight), an RFC 3339 time or a duration (`30m`, `12h`, `3d`) means events whose `at` is at or after that time. `from` + `upTo` must satisfy `1 <= from <= upTo <= last seq`. Numbers are accepted as JSON integers or digit strings.
11. **`upTo` in answers.** When every entry fits, `upTo` is the end of the selected range (consumer and `since`: the last seq; range form: its `upTo`), so `ack` also moves past events that were folded into a later event of the same key or filtered by `kinds`. When the answer is cut, `upTo` is the seq of the last included entry and `more: true`. An empty range answers `count: 0`, `from` = start and `upTo` = start - 1, so `ack` is a no-op.
12. **`kinds`** filters after grouping. The ingest skill only acks the `upTo` of its unfiltered summary call (the tool description says so).
13. **Answer format.** `get_changes`: a TOON head (`from`, `upTo`, `more`, `count`), then per entry a line `==== roger entry <seq> ====`, its fields as TOON key-value lines, a blank line and the Markdown. `get_item`: the Markdown, then `==== roger paths ====` and `paths[N]: ...`. TOON tabular rows cannot hold arrays (TOON 4.4 §9.3), so in `list_items` the `paths` cell is one string joined by ` | `.
14. **Limits** above the maximum are clamped, values below 1 are an error.
15. **Library per call.** `roger-mcp` resolves the library on every tool call (`CFPreferencesAppSynchronize` first), so a root change in Roger is followed without restarting the client.
16. **Access check.** `open(2)` on `changes.jsonl`: `ENOENT` means "Start Roger once to set up the library", `EPERM`/`EACCES` means the Files and Folders message. Both are `isError` for every tool except `status`, which reports them as text.
17. **Empty library.** Reconcile creates an empty `changes.jsonl` even when there are no items, so `status` works after the first start.
18. **Shutdown.** On stdin EOF `roger-mcp` waits up to 2 s for running tool calls, then exits 0. `SIGPIPE` is ignored.
19. **Copilot and Kiro have MCP CLIs** (spec said "none"): `copilot mcp add roger -- <path>` and `kiro-cli mcp add --name roger --command <path> --scope global`. They are used like the others. Kiro's `--command` is one string, so a path with a space falls back to the snippet.
20. **Skills folders.** Each harness has the folders it reads and one folder Roger writes. Copilot writes `~/.agents/skills` (it reads it, verified). A harness is "covered by" another when a folder it reads is already written. Cursor reads `~/.agents/skills` and `~/.claude/skills`, so with Claude Code and Codex both installed it sees two copies; accepted.
21. **Server status** comes from reading the config files only, never from `<cli> mcp list` (`claude mcp list` starts every configured server; `gemini mcp list` depends on folder trust). Spec allowed both; reading is enough and never hangs.
22. **CLI runs.** stdin `/dev/null`, working folder = home, `PATH` = login shell `PATH` plus `/opt/homebrew/bin` and `/usr/local/bin` passed in the environment (gemini and codex are `#!/usr/bin/env node` scripts), timeout 20 s, then `terminate()` and `SIGKILL` after 1 s.
23. **Registered path.** The `roger-mcp` path handed to harnesses is a Homebrew symlink (`/opt/homebrew/bin/roger-mcp` or `/usr/local/bin/roger-mcp`) when it resolves into the running bundle, else the running bundle's `Contents/MacOS/roger-mcp`. Always absolute (GUI-launched harnesses may have a short `PATH`). Refused, with a message, when Roger runs translocated (`/AppTranslocation/`) or from `/Volumes/`.
24. **Registering again.** Same command already in the config: nothing to do. Another command: `mcp remove` then `mcp add`.
25. **Skill states** are content based. "Changed by you" (file hash differs from the receipt, or no receipt and the file differs from the bundled one) wins over everything. "Outdated" means the file matches the receipt but the bundled skill differs. No receipt and the file equals the bundled skill: "Up to date" (adopted; the receipt is written on the next action).
26. **Backups never overwrite:** `SKILL.md.bak`, then `SKILL.md.bak 1`, `SKILL.md.bak 2`.
27. **Uninstall** removes only `SKILL.md` (to the Trash after a confirmation when changed) and the `ingest-roger` folder only when it is then empty. Other files the user put there stay.
28. **Links.** If the skills folder entry `ingest-roger` or its `SKILL.md` is a symbolic link, install and uninstall refuse.
29. **Popover hint** shows while any installed skill is outdated. It has a close button that hides it until the next app version (`agentSkillHintDismissedVersion` in UserDefaults).
30. **MCPB.** `manifest_version` "0.3": the default of `@anthropic-ai/mcpb` 2.1.2 for new packages (0.4 only adds the `uv` server type) and what Crystalline ships. `server.entry_point` is `server/roger-mcp`, a tiny executable `#!/bin/sh` launcher that `exec`s the absolute path, because the mcpb validator requires an executable entry point inside the bundle. `mcp_config.command` is the absolute path itself. The manifest passed `npx -y @anthropic-ai/mcpb@2.1.2 validate` with an absolute `command` (checked 2026-10-14). Whether Claude Desktop installs it is a manual check (Task 14 step); if it refuses, switch `command` to `${__dirname}/server/roger-mcp` (the launcher) and keep everything else.
31. **Icon.** The `.mcpb` `icon.png` is the app icon rendered at 256 x 256 at build time. Zips are made with `/usr/bin/ditto -c -k --norsrc --noextattr --noqtn --noacl` (no `--keepParent` for `.mcpb`, `--keepParent` for the skill zip).
32. **Desktop record** (last added path and time, last skill save) lives in `~/Library/Application Support/Roger/claude-desktop.json`, not in UserDefaults, so it is testable.
33. **Script protocol.** The skill does at most 5 `get_changes` rounds per run and ends with `RESULT: ok` (nothing left), `RESULT: ok more` (stopped with work left) or `RESULT: failed: <reason>`. The script runs again on `RESULT: ok more`. Allowed tools: `mcp__<roger server>,mcp__<crystalline server>`, names from `ROGER_MCP_SERVER` (default `roger`) and `CRYSTALLINE_MCP_SERVER` (default `crystalline`), because the Crystalline server name differs between setups.
34. **CI.** The Roger scheme lists both test targets, so the existing CI test step runs RogerKitTests. `ci.yml` does not change.
35. **Status details.** `status` shows per consumer `name`, `seq`, `updatedAt`, `age` (`45m`, `3h`, `2d`) and `open` (events after the bookmark, not grouped). Dates in all outputs are RFC 3339 with the local offset.
36. **Search snippet** comes from the file text (case kept), around the first match, at most 160 characters, newlines folded to spaces.

### Task 1: RogerKit static library and RogerKitTests

Extract the Foundation-only library code into a static library that both Roger.app and (Task 9) `roger-mcp` link, and add a test bundle that runs without a host app.

**Files:**
- Modify: `Roger/project.yml`
- Move (`git mv`) and make public: `Roger/Models/LibraryLayout.swift` → `Roger/RogerKit/LibraryLayout.swift`, `Roger/Models/LibraryItem.swift` → `Roger/RogerKit/LibraryItem.swift`, `Roger/Services/Library/LibraryFrontmatterParser.swift` → `Roger/RogerKit/LibraryFrontmatterParser.swift`, `Roger/Services/Library/LibraryItemReader.swift` → `Roger/RogerKit/LibraryItemReader.swift`, `Roger/Services/Library/LibraryPath.swift` → `Roger/RogerKit/LibraryPath.swift`, `Roger/Services/MeetingRecording/MeetingSessionScanner.swift` → `Roger/RogerKit/MeetingSessionScanner.swift`, `Roger/Services/ExternalTranscriptRegistry.swift` → `Roger/RogerKit/ExternalTranscriptRegistry.swift`
- Create: `Roger/RogerKit/SessionFolderName.swift`, `Roger/RogerKit/MeetingMarkersSummary.swift`, `Roger/RogerKit/LibraryScanner.swift`
- Create: `Roger/Services/MeetingRecording/MeetingTrackSource.swift` (the two enums cut out of the scanner file)
- Create: `Roger/App/RogerKitExports.swift`
- Modify: `Roger/Services/Library/LibraryIndex.swift` (scope type and scanning move to `LibraryScanner`)
- Modify: `Roger/Services/LibraryMigration.swift:19-23` (`sessionDate` delegates)
- Modify: `Roger/Services/MeetingRecording/MeetingTranscriptWriter.swift:159-165` (`displayDate` delegates)
- Create: `Roger/RogerKitTests/TempDir.swift`, `Roger/RogerKitTests/KitFixtures.swift`
- Test: `Roger/RogerKitTests/SessionFolderNameTests.swift`, `Roger/RogerKitTests/MeetingMarkersSummaryTests.swift`, `Roger/RogerKitTests/LibraryScannerTests.swift`
- Regenerated: `Roger/Roger.xcodeproj` (new schemes `RogerKitTests`; `Roger` scheme gains the RogerKitTests test target)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (existing, all moved unchanged in behavior): `LibraryLayout`, `LibraryItemKind`, `LibraryItem`, `LibraryFrontmatter`, `LibraryFrontmatterParser`, `LibraryItemReader`, `LibraryPath`, `MeetingSessionScanner`, `ExternalTranscriptRegistry`, `LibraryScanScope`.
- Produces (all `public` in module `RogerKit`):
  - Everything listed under Consumes, with the same names and signatures, plus `public init` for `LibraryLayout(root:)`, `LibraryItem(url:folderURL:kind:title:date:durationSeconds:language:preset:speakerCount:screenshotCount:audioFiles:firstLine:searchText:isUnfinished:)`, `LibraryFrontmatter(scalars:tags:sources:)` (all defaulted), `LibraryFrontmatter.Source(id:resource:)`, `LibraryScanScope(layout:extraFolders:)`, `ExternalTranscriptRegistry(fileURL:)`. `LibraryItemKind` gains `Codable`.
  - `ExternalTranscriptRegistry.listedFiles() -> [URL]` (existing files, never writes).
  - `enum SessionFolderName { static func date(for folder: URL) -> Date?; static func displayDate(_ date: Date) -> String }`
  - `struct MeetingMarkersSummary: Equatable, Sendable { let sessionStartedAt: Date; let markerCount: Int; static func read(from folder: URL) -> MeetingMarkersSummary? }`
  - `enum LibraryScanner { static func scanAll(scope: LibraryScanScope, externalFiles: [URL]) -> [LibraryItem]; static func scanTree(_ path: String, kind: LibraryItemKind) -> [LibraryItem]; static func scanExtraFolder(_ path: String) -> [LibraryItem]; static func scanExternal(_ files: [URL]) -> [LibraryItem] }`
  - RogerKitTests helpers: `enum TempDir { static func make() throws -> URL; static func remove(_ url: URL) }`, `enum KitFixtures` (see Step 1).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the test helpers and the failing tests**

`Roger/RogerKitTests/TempDir.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

/// Unique `RogerKitTests-<UUID>` folders in the temporary directory. Pair
/// `make()` with `defer { TempDir.remove(url) }`.
enum TempDir {
    static func make() throws -> URL {
        let url = FileManager.default.temporaryDirectory
            .appendingPathComponent("RogerKitTests-\(UUID().uuidString)", isDirectory: true)
        try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: url, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        return url
    }

    static func remove(_ url: URL) {
        try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url)
    }
}
```

`Roger/RogerKitTests/KitFixtures.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation
@testable import RogerKit

/// Minimal files in the shape Roger writes them (the app's writers import
/// FluidAudio, so RogerKitTests cannot call them).
enum KitFixtures {
    static let base = Date(timeIntervalSince1970: 1_790_000_000)

    static func stem(_ date: Date) -> String {
        let f = DateFormatter()
        f.locale = Locale(identifier: "en_US_POSIX")
        f.timeZone = .current
        f.dateFormat = "yyyy-MM-dd HH-mm-ss"
        return f.string(from: date)
    }

    static func iso(_ date: Date) -> String {
        let f = ISO8601DateFormatter()
        f.timeZone = .current
        f.formatOptions = [.withInternetDateTime]
        return f.string(from: date)
    }

    /// `<root>/Logs/YYYY/MM/<stem>.md`, optionally with `<stem>.m4a`.
    @discardableResult
    static func log(in layout: LibraryLayout, at date: Date, text: String, audio: Bool = false) throws -> URL {
        let folder = layout.monthFolder(.logs, for: date)
        try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: folder, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        let name = stem(date)
        var sources = ""
        if audio {
            try Data("m4a".utf8).write(to: folder.appendingPathComponent(name + ".m4a"))
            sources = "sources:\n  - { id: audio, resource: \"\(name).m4a\" }\n"
        }
        let md = folder.appendingPathComponent(name + ".md")
        let body = "---\ntype: source\ntitle: \"Log \(name)\"\ntags: [log]\n\(sources)status: stable\nstarted_at: \(iso(date))\nduration_seconds: 12\n---\n# Log \(name)\n\n\(text)\n"
        try body.write(to: md, atomically: true, encoding: .utf8)
        return md
    }

    /// A finished meeting session `<parent>/<name>/` with `transcript.md`,
    /// `mic.m4a` and optionally one screenshot. Returns the session folder.
    @discardableResult
    static func meeting(in parent: URL, name: String, at date: Date, text: String, screenshot: Bool = false) throws -> URL {
        let folder = parent.appendingPathComponent(name, isDirectory: true)
        try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: folder, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        try Data("m4a".utf8).write(to: folder.appendingPathComponent("mic.m4a"))
        if screenshot {
            try Data([0x89, 0x50, 0x4E, 0x47]).write(to: folder.appendingPathComponent("\(stem(date)).png"))
        }
        let body = "---\ntype: source\ntitle: \"Meeting \(name)\"\ntags: [meeting, transcript]\nsources:\n  - { id: mic, resource: \"mic.m4a\" }\nstatus: stable\nstarted_at: \(iso(date))\nduration_seconds: 600\nspeaker_count: 2\n---\n# Meeting \(name)\n\n**Me** [00:00:01]\n\(text)\n"
        try body.write(to: folder.appendingPathComponent("transcript.md"), atomically: true, encoding: .utf8)
        return folder
    }

    /// A file transcript at `url` (any folder).
    @discardableResult
    static func transcript(at url: URL, at date: Date, title: String, text: String) throws -> URL {
        try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: url.deletingLastPathComponent(), withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        let body = "---\ntype: source\ntitle: \"\(title)\"\ntags: [transcript]\nstatus: stable\nstarted_at: \(iso(date))\nduration_seconds: 90\n---\n# \(title)\n\n\(text)\n"
        try body.write(to: url, atomically: true, encoding: .utf8)
        return url
    }
}
```

```swift
import Foundation
import Testing
@testable import RogerKit

struct SessionFolderNameTests {
    @Test func readsTheFolderNameDate() throws {
        let date = try #require(SessionFolderName.date(for: URL(fileURLWithPath: "/x/2026-10-13 09-05")))
        let parts = Calendar.current.dateComponents([.year, .month, .day, .hour, .minute], from: date)
        #expect(parts.year == 2026 && parts.month == 10 && parts.day == 13 && parts.hour == 9 && parts.minute == 5)
    }

    @Test func ignoresTheCounterSuffix() {
        let plain = SessionFolderName.date(for: URL(fileURLWithPath: "/x/2026-10-13 09-05"))
        let suffixed = SessionFolderName.date(for: URL(fileURLWithPath: "/x/2026-10-13 09-05 2"))
        #expect(plain != nil && plain == suffixed)
    }

    @Test func otherNamesHaveNoDate() {
        #expect(SessionFolderName.date(for: URL(fileURLWithPath: "/x/notes")) == nil)
        #expect(SessionFolderName.date(for: URL(fileURLWithPath: "/x/2026-10-13")) == nil)
    }

    @Test func displayDateIsMinutePrecise() throws {
        let date = try #require(SessionFolderName.date(for: URL(fileURLWithPath: "/x/2026-10-13 09-05")))
        #expect(SessionFolderName.displayDate(date) == "2026-10-13 09:05")
    }
}
```

```swift
import Foundation
import Testing
@testable import RogerKit

struct MeetingMarkersSummaryTests {
    @Test func readsStartAndCount() throws {
        let folder = try TempDir.make()
        defer { TempDir.remove(folder) }
        // The shape MeetingCheckpointStore writes (ISO 8601 dates, sorted keys).
        let json = """
        {
          "markers" : [
            { "capturedAt" : "2026-10-13T07:05:00Z", "id" : "6F1C4A43-2B8C-4C55-9C1E-1F6D2A7B3C11", "imageFile" : "a.png", "offsetSeconds" : 300, "stemDate" : "2026-10-13T07:05:00Z" },
            { "capturedAt" : "2026-10-13T07:10:00Z", "id" : "7A2D5B54-3C9D-4D66-8D2F-2E7E3B8C4D22", "imageFile" : "b.png", "offsetSeconds" : 600, "stemDate" : "2026-10-13T07:10:00Z" }
          ],
          "sessionStartedAt" : "2026-10-13T07:00:00Z",
          "version" : 1
        }
        """
        try Data(json.utf8).write(to: folder.appendingPathComponent("markers.json"))
        let summary = try #require(MeetingMarkersSummary.read(from: folder))
        #expect(summary.markerCount == 2)
        #expect(summary.sessionStartedAt == ISO8601DateFormatter().date(from: "2026-10-13T07:00:00Z"))
    }

    @Test func missingOrBrokenFileIsNil() throws {
        let folder = try TempDir.make()
        defer { TempDir.remove(folder) }
        #expect(MeetingMarkersSummary.read(from: folder) == nil)
        try Data("{ nope".utf8).write(to: folder.appendingPathComponent("markers.json"))
        #expect(MeetingMarkersSummary.read(from: folder) == nil)
    }
}
```

```swift
import Foundation
import Testing
@testable import RogerKit

struct LibraryScannerTests {
    private let date = KitFixtures.base

    @Test func scansEveryKindAndPlace() throws {
        let home = try TempDir.make()
        defer { TempDir.remove(home) }
        let layout = LibraryLayout(root: home.appendingPathComponent("Library", isDirectory: true))
        try KitFixtures.log(in: layout, at: date, text: "Called the supplier", audio: true)
        try KitFixtures.meeting(in: layout.monthFolder(.meetings, for: date), name: "2026-09-21 16-00", at: date, text: "Welcome")
        try KitFixtures.transcript(at: layout.monthFolder(.transcripts, for: date).appendingPathComponent("talk.mp4.md"), at: date, title: "Talk", text: "Hello")
        let extra = home.appendingPathComponent("Roger Recordings", isDirectory: true)
        try KitFixtures.meeting(in: extra, name: "2025-01-02 10-00", at: date, text: "Old")
        let external = try KitFixtures.transcript(at: home.appendingPathComponent("Media/clip.m4a.md"), at: date, title: "Clip", text: "Hi")

        let items = LibraryScanner.scanAll(
            scope: LibraryScanScope(layout: layout, extraFolders: [extra]),
            externalFiles: [external]
        )
        #expect(items.filter { $0.kind == .log }.count == 1)
        #expect(items.filter { $0.kind == .meeting }.count == 2)
        #expect(items.filter { $0.kind == .transcript }.count == 2)
        let log = try #require(items.first { $0.kind == .log })
        #expect(log.audioFiles.map(\.lastPathComponent) == [KitFixtures.stem(date) + ".m4a"])
    }

    @Test func hiddenFilesAndTheRogerFolderAreNoItems() throws {
        let root = try TempDir.make()
        defer { TempDir.remove(root) }
        let layout = LibraryLayout(root: root)
        let roger = root.appendingPathComponent(".roger", isDirectory: true)
        try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: roger, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        try Data("{}\n".utf8).write(to: roger.appendingPathComponent("changes.jsonl"))
        let folder = layout.monthFolder(.logs, for: date)
        try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: folder, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        try Data("---\ntags: [log]\n---\n".utf8).write(to: folder.appendingPathComponent(".draft.md"))
        #expect(LibraryScanner.scanAll(scope: LibraryScanScope(layout: layout, extraFolders: []), externalFiles: []).isEmpty)
    }

    @Test func anOverlappingExtraFolderListsEachItemOnce() throws {
        let root = try TempDir.make()
        defer { TempDir.remove(root) }
        let layout = LibraryLayout(root: root)
        let month = layout.monthFolder(.meetings, for: date)
        try KitFixtures.meeting(in: month, name: "2026-09-21 16-00", at: date, text: "Welcome")
        let items = LibraryScanner.scanAll(
            scope: LibraryScanScope(layout: layout, extraFolders: [month]),
            externalFiles: []
        )
        #expect(items.count == 1)
    }

    @Test func unfinishedSessionIsAnItemWithoutMarkdown() throws {
        let root = try TempDir.make()
        defer { TempDir.remove(root) }
        let layout = LibraryLayout(root: root)
        let session = layout.monthFolder(.meetings, for: date).appendingPathComponent("2026-09-21 16-00", isDirectory: true)
        try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: session, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        try Data().write(to: session.appendingPathComponent("mic-001.caf"))
        let item = try #require(LibraryScanner.scanAll(scope: LibraryScanScope(layout: layout, extraFolders: []), externalFiles: []).first)
        #expect(item.isUnfinished)
        #expect(!item.hasMarkdown)
        #expect(item.title == "Unfinished meeting 2026-09-21 16:00")
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Make the moved declarations public**

Rule for every moved file: each declaration that had no access modifier (internal) gets `public`; `private` and `fileprivate` stay as they are. Concretely:

`Roger/RogerKit/LibraryLayout.swift` becomes:

```swift
import Foundation

/// Folder layout of Roger's library: one root with `Logs/`, `Meetings/` and
/// `Transcripts/`, each split into `YYYY/MM/` by the local recording time.
/// The single place that knows where Roger's files go.
public struct LibraryLayout: Sendable, Equatable {
    public enum Kind: String, CaseIterable, Sendable {
        case logs = "Logs"
        case meetings = "Meetings"
        case transcripts = "Transcripts"
    }

    public let root: URL

    public init(root: URL) {
        self.root = root
    }

    /// `~/Documents/Roger/`.
    public static var defaultRoot: URL {
        documents.appendingPathComponent("Roger", isDirectory: true)
    }

    /// Where meetings went before the library existed: `~/Documents/Roger Recordings/`.
    public static var legacyRecordingsFolder: URL {
        documents.appendingPathComponent("Roger Recordings", isDirectory: true)
    }

    private static var documents: URL {
        FileManager.default.urls(for: .documentDirectory, in: .userDomainMask).first
            ?? URL(fileURLWithPath: NSHomeDirectory()).appendingPathComponent("Documents", isDirectory: true)
    }

    public func kindFolder(_ kind: Kind) -> URL {
        root.appendingPathComponent(kind.rawValue, isDirectory: true)
    }

    /// `<root>/<Kind>/YYYY/MM/` for the local month of `date`.
    public func monthFolder(_ kind: Kind, for date: Date, calendar: Calendar = .current) -> URL {
        let parts = calendar.dateComponents([.year, .month], from: date)
        let year = String(format: "%04d", parts.year ?? 0)
        let month = String(format: "%02d", parts.month ?? 0)
        return kindFolder(kind)
            .appendingPathComponent(year, isDirectory: true)
            .appendingPathComponent(month, isDirectory: true)
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write the new RogerKit files**

`Roger/RogerKit/SessionFolderName.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

/// Meeting session folders are named `yyyy-MM-dd HH-mm` in local time, with
/// ` 1`, ` 2` … when the name was taken.
public enum SessionFolderName {
    /// Start date from the first 16 characters of the folder name.
    public static func date(for folder: URL) -> Date? {
        let name = folder.lastPathComponent
        guard name.count >= 16 else { return nil }
        let f = DateFormatter()
        f.locale = Locale(identifier: "en_US_POSIX")
        f.dateFormat = "yyyy-MM-dd HH-mm"
        return f.date(from: String(name.prefix(16)))
    }

    /// `yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm` in local time, used in meeting titles.
    public static func displayDate(_ date: Date) -> String {
        let f = DateFormatter()
        f.locale = Locale(identifier: "en_US_POSIX")
        f.dateFormat = "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm"
        return f.string(from: date)
    }
}
```

`Roger/RogerKit/MeetingMarkersSummary.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

/// What the library needs from a session's `markers.json`: when the session
/// started and how many screenshots were dropped. Reads only these two
/// fields, so it does not depend on the app's checkpoint types.
public struct MeetingMarkersSummary: Equatable, Sendable {
    public let sessionStartedAt: Date
    public let markerCount: Int

    private struct File: Decodable {
        struct Marker: Decodable {}
        let sessionStartedAt: Date
        let markers: [Marker]
    }

    /// Nil when the file is missing or cannot be decoded.
    public static func read(from folder: URL) -> MeetingMarkersSummary? {
        guard let data = try? Data(contentsOf: folder.appendingPathComponent("markers.json")) else { return nil }
        let decoder = JSONDecoder()
        decoder.dateDecodingStrategy = .iso8601
        guard let file = try? decoder.decode(File.self, from: data) else { return nil }
        return MeetingMarkersSummary(sessionStartedAt: file.sessionStartedAt, markerCount: file.markers.count)
    }
}
```

`Roger/RogerKit/LibraryScanner.swift` (the scanning code moves here unchanged from `InMemoryLibraryIndex`):

```swift
import Foundation

/// The folders a scan covers: the library root and the extra scan folders.
public struct LibraryScanScope: Equatable, Sendable {
    public let layout: LibraryLayout
    public let extraFolders: [URL]

    public init(layout: LibraryLayout, extraFolders: [URL]) {
        self.layout = layout
        self.extraFolders = extraFolders
    }
}

/// Reads every item in a scope from disk. Used by the library index, the
/// change log's catch-up and `roger-mcp`. Paths are canonical. Hidden files
/// and folders (including `<root>/.roger/`) are skipped.
public enum LibraryScanner {
    /// Logs, transcripts and meetings below the root, sessions and `.md`
    /// files directly in each extra folder, and the external transcripts.
    /// One item per canonical path (first found wins).
    public static func scanAll(scope: LibraryScanScope, externalFiles: [URL]) -> [LibraryItem] {
        let layout = scope.layout
        var found = scanTree(LibraryPath.canonical(layout.kindFolder(.logs)), kind: .log)
        found += scanTree(LibraryPath.canonical(layout.kindFolder(.transcripts)), kind: .transcript)
        found += scanTree(LibraryPath.canonical(layout.kindFolder(.meetings)), kind: .meeting)
        for extra in scope.extraFolders {
            found += scanExtraFolder(LibraryPath.canonical(extra))
        }
        found += scanExternal(externalFiles)
        var seen = Set<String>()
        return found.filter { seen.insert($0.id).inserted }
    }

    /// Every item at or below `path`. Meetings: session folders up to three
    /// levels down (`YYYY/MM/<session>`). Logs and transcripts: every `.md`
    /// file. Synchronous on purpose: iterating a `DirectoryEnumerator` is
    /// not allowed in async functions under Swift 6.
    public static func scanTree(_ path: String, kind: LibraryItemKind) -> [LibraryItem] {
        var isDirectory: ObjCBool = false
        guard FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: path, isDirectory: &isDirectory), isDirectory.boolValue else { return [] }
        let url = URL(fileURLWithPath: path, isDirectory: true)
        if kind == .meeting {
            let sessions = MeetingSessionScanner.isSessionFolder(url)
                ? [url]
                : MeetingSessionScanner.sessionFolders(in: url, maxDepth: 3)
            return sessions.compactMap { LibraryItemReader.meetingItem(sessionFolder: $0) }
        }
        guard let enumerator = FileManager.default.enumerator(
            at: url,
            includingPropertiesForKeys: [.isRegularFileKey],
            options: [.skipsHiddenFiles, .skipsPackageDescendants]
        ) else { return [] }
        var items: [LibraryItem] = []
        for case let file as URL in enumerator where file.pathExtension.lowercased() == "md" {
            if let item = LibraryItemReader.item(markdownAt: file, fallbackKind: kind) { items.append(item) }
        }
        return items
    }

    /// Sessions stored directly in an extra folder (the pre-library meetings
    /// folder) and `.md` files directly inside it (the pre-library custom
    /// transcripts folder).
    public static func scanExtraFolder(_ path: String) -> [LibraryItem] {
        let url = URL(fileURLWithPath: path, isDirectory: true)
        var items = MeetingSessionScanner.sessionFolders(in: url, maxDepth: 1)
            .compactMap { LibraryItemReader.meetingItem(sessionFolder: $0) }
        let children = (try? FileManager.default.contentsOfDirectory(
            at: url, includingPropertiesForKeys: nil, options: [.skipsHiddenFiles]
        )) ?? []
        for file in children where file.pathExtension.lowercased() == "md" {
            if let item = LibraryItemReader.item(markdownAt: file, fallbackKind: .transcript) { items.append(item) }
        }
        return items
    }

    /// Transcripts written next to their source file.
    public static func scanExternal(_ files: [URL]) -> [LibraryItem] {
        files.compactMap { file in
            LibraryItemReader.item(markdownAt: LibraryPath.canonicalURL(file), fallbackKind: .transcript)
        }
    }
}
```

### Task 2: TOON encoder

All lists the tools return are TOON. Roger needs only the encoder, and only the forms it writes: key-value lines, nested objects, inline primitive arrays and tabular arrays with the comma delimiter.

**Files:**
- Create: `Roger/RogerKit/TOON.swift`
- Test: `Roger/RogerKitTests/TOONTests.swift`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing.
- Produces:
  - `public enum TOONValue: Equatable, Sendable { case string(String), int(Int), bool(Bool), null }`
  - `public enum TOON { static func encode(_ value: TOONValue) -> String; static func string(_ s: String) -> String; static func needsQuotes(_ s: String) -> Bool; static func quoted(_ s: String) -> String; static func key(_ k: String) -> String; static func field(_ name: String, _ value: TOONValue, depth: Int = 0) -> String; static func object(_ name: String, depth: Int = 0) -> String; static func inlineArray(_ name: String, _ values: [TOONValue], depth: Int = 0) -> String; static func table(_ name: String, fields: [String], rows: [[TOONValue]], depth: Int = 0) -> String }`

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the encoder**

`Roger/RogerKit/TOON.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

/// A primitive TOON value.
public enum TOONValue: Equatable, Sendable {
    case string(String)
    case int(Int)
    case bool(Bool)
    case null
}

/// The part of TOON 4.4 (github.com/toon-format/spec, 2026-10-08) that
/// Roger writes: key-value lines, nested objects, inline primitive arrays
/// and tabular arrays. Comma delimiter, two spaces per level, no trailing
/// spaces, no blank lines inside a block.
public enum TOON {
    public static let indentUnit = "  "

    public static func encode(_ value: TOONValue) -> String {
        switch value {
        case .string(let text): return string(text)
        case .int(let number): return String(number)
        case .bool(let flag): return flag ? "true" : "false"
        case .null: return "null"
        }
    }

    /// The string as is, or quoted when §7.2 requires it.
    public static func string(_ text: String) -> String {
        needsQuotes(text) ? quoted(text) : text
    }

    /// TOON 4.4 §7.2 with the comma as document delimiter.
    public static func needsQuotes(_ text: String) -> Bool {
        if text.isEmpty { return true }
        if let first = text.unicodeScalars.first, first == " " || first == "\t" { return true }
        if let last = text.unicodeScalars.last, last == " " || last == "\t" { return true }
        if text == "true" || text == "false" || text == "null" { return true }
        if text.range(of: #"^[+-]?[0-9]+(\.[0-9]+)?([eE][+-]?[0-9]+)?$"#, options: .regularExpression) != nil { return true }
        if text.hasPrefix("-") || text.hasPrefix("#") { return true }
        for scalar in text.unicodeScalars {
            switch scalar {
            case ":", "\"", "\\", "[", "]", "{", "}", ",":
                return true
            default:
                if scalar.value < 0x20 { return true }
            }
        }
        return false
    }

    /// Double-quoted with the escapes of §7.1: `\\`, `\"`, `\n`, `\r`,
    /// `\t`, other controls as `\uXXXX` with lowercase hex.
    public static func quoted(_ text: String) -> String {
        var out = "\""
        for scalar in text.unicodeScalars {
            switch scalar {
            case "\\": out += "\\\\"
            case "\"": out += "\\\""
            case "\n": out += "\\n"
            case "\r": out += "\\r"
            case "\t": out += "\\t"
            default:
                if scalar.value < 0x20 {
                    out += String(format: "\\u%04x", scalar.value)
                } else {
                    out.unicodeScalars.append(scalar)
                }
            }
        }
        return out + "\""
    }

    /// §7.3: unquoted when it matches `^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_.]*$`.
    public static func key(_ name: String) -> String {
        name.range(of: #"^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_.]*$"#, options: .regularExpression) != nil ? name : quoted(name)
    }

    /// `name: value`
    public static func field(_ name: String, _ value: TOONValue, depth: Int = 0) -> String {
        indent(depth) + key(name) + ": " + encode(value)
    }

    /// `name:` opening a nested object; its fields follow at `depth + 1`.
    public static func object(_ name: String, depth: Int = 0) -> String {
        indent(depth) + key(name) + ":"
    }

    /// `name[N]: a,b` or `name: []`.
    public static func inlineArray(_ name: String, _ values: [TOONValue], depth: Int = 0) -> String {
        guard !values.isEmpty else { return indent(depth) + key(name) + ": []" }
        return indent(depth) + key(name) + "[\(values.count)]: " + values.map(encode).joined(separator: ",")
    }

    /// `name[N]{f1,f2}:` and one row per record, or `name: []`.
    public static func table(_ name: String, fields: [String], rows: [[TOONValue]], depth: Int = 0) -> String {
        guard !rows.isEmpty else { return indent(depth) + key(name) + ": []" }
        var lines = [indent(depth) + key(name) + "[\(rows.count)]{" + fields.map(key).joined(separator: ",") + "}:"]
        for row in rows {
            precondition(row.count == fields.count, "A TOON row needs one cell per field")
            lines.append(indent(depth + 1) + row.map(encode).joined(separator: ","))
        }
        return lines.joined(separator: "\n")
    }

    private static func indent(_ depth: Int) -> String {
        String(repeating: indentUnit, count: depth)
    }
}
```

### Task 3: Change log and consumer bookmarks

The append-only log and the bookmarks, safe against concurrent writers in several processes.

**Files:**
- Create: `Roger/RogerKit/RFC3339.swift`
- Create: `Roger/RogerKit/ChangeEvent.swift`
- Create: `Roger/RogerKit/ChangeLog.swift`
- Create: `Roger/RogerKit/ConsumerBookmarks.swift`
- Test: `Roger/RogerKitTests/ChangeLogTests.swift`
- Test: `Roger/RogerKitTests/ConsumerBookmarksTests.swift`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `LibraryItemKind` (Task 1, `Codable`).
- Produces:
  - `public enum RFC3339 { static func string(_ date: Date, timeZone: TimeZone = .current) -> String; static func date(_ text: String) -> Date? }`
  - `public enum ChangeEventType: String, Codable, Sendable, CaseIterable { case created, updated, deleted }`
  - `public struct ChangeEvent: Equatable, Sendable { let seq: Int; let at: Date; let event: ChangeEventType; let kind: LibraryItemKind; let key: String; let path: String; init(seq:at:event:kind:key:path:); func jsonLine(timeZone: TimeZone = .current) -> String; static func parse(_ line: Data) -> ChangeEvent? }`
  - `public struct PendingChange: Equatable, Sendable { let event: ChangeEventType; let kind: LibraryItemKind; let key: String; let path: String; init(event:kind:key:path:) }`
  - `public enum ChangeLogFile { static func folder(root: URL) -> URL; static func url(root: URL) -> URL; static func parse(_ data: Data) -> [ChangeEvent]; static func readEvents(root: URL) throws -> [ChangeEvent] }`
  - `public actor ChangeLog { nonisolated let root: URL; nonisolated let fileURL: URL; init(root: URL, now: @escaping @Sendable () -> Date = { Date() }); @discardableResult func append(_ changes: [PendingChange]) throws -> [ChangeEvent]; func ensureFile() throws }` plus the module-internal `func locked<T: Sendable>(_ decide: ([ChangeEvent]) throws -> ([PendingChange], T)) throws -> (written: [ChangeEvent], result: T)` (Task 4 adds `record` and `reconcile` in an extension)
  - `public struct ConsumerBookmark: Equatable, Sendable { let seq: Int; let updatedAt: Date }`, `public struct NamedBookmark: Equatable, Sendable { let name: String; let bookmark: ConsumerBookmark }`, `public struct AckResult: Equatable, Sendable { let previous: Int; let current: Int }`, `public enum ConsumerBookmarkError: Error, Equatable, LocalizedError { case invalidName(String) }`
  - `public struct ConsumerBookmarks: Sendable { let root: URL; init(root: URL); var folder: URL; static func isValidName(_ name: String) -> Bool; func bookmark(_ name: String) throws -> ConsumerBookmark?; func all() -> [NamedBookmark]; @discardableResult func ack(_ name: String, upTo: Int, now: Date = Date()) throws -> AckResult; func reset(_ name: String, now: Date = Date()) throws; func forget(_ name: String) throws }`

```swift
import Foundation
import Testing
@testable import RogerKit

struct ChangeLogTests {
    private func change(_ n: Int, _ event: ChangeEventType = .created) -> PendingChange {
        PendingChange(event: event, kind: .log, key: "Logs/2026/10/entry \(n)", path: "Logs/2026/10/entry \(n).md")
    }

    @Test func lineHasTheSpecFieldOrder() throws {
        let at = try #require(RFC3339.date("2026-10-13T14:32:41+02:00"))
        let event = ChangeEvent(
            seq: 42, at: at, event: .created, kind: .log,
            key: "Logs/2026/10/2026-10-13 14-32-05", path: "Logs/2026/10/2026-10-13 14-32-05.md"
        )
        let line = event.jsonLine(timeZone: try #require(TimeZone(secondsFromGMT: 7200)))
        #expect(line == #"{"seq":42,"at":"2026-10-13T14:32:41+02:00","event":"created","kind":"log","key":"Logs/2026/10/2026-10-13 14-32-05","path":"Logs/2026/10/2026-10-13 14-32-05.md"}"#)
        #expect(ChangeEvent.parse(Data(line.utf8)) == event)
    }

    @Test func keysWithQuotesAndBackslashesRoundTrip() throws {
        let event = ChangeEvent(seq: 1, at: Date(timeIntervalSince1970: 1_790_000_000), event: .updated, kind: .transcript,
                                key: "External/a \"b\" \\ c-0123abcd", path: "/tmp/a \"b\" \\ c.md")
        #expect(ChangeEvent.parse(Data(event.jsonLine().utf8)) == event)
    }

    @Test func seqStartsAtOneAndIncreases() async throws {
        let root = try TempDir.make()
        defer { TempDir.remove(root) }
        let log = ChangeLog(root: root)
        let first = try await log.append([change(1)])
        let next = try await log.append([change(2), change(3)])
        #expect(first.map(\.seq) == [1])
        #expect(next.map(\.seq) == [2, 3])
        #expect(try ChangeLogFile.readEvents(root: root).map(\.seq) == [1, 2, 3])
        #expect(log.fileURL.path.hasSuffix("/.roger/changes.jsonl"))
    }

    @Test func ensureFileCreatesAnEmptyLog() async throws {
        let root = try TempDir.make()
        defer { TempDir.remove(root) }
        try await ChangeLog(root: root).ensureFile()
        #expect(try Data(contentsOf: ChangeLogFile.url(root: root)).isEmpty)
        #expect(try ChangeLogFile.readEvents(root: root).isEmpty)
    }

    @Test func twoHandlesNeverShareASeq() async throws {
        let root = try TempDir.make()
        defer { TempDir.remove(root) }
        let a = ChangeLog(root: root)
        let b = ChangeLog(root: root)
        try await withThrowingTaskGroup(of: Void.self) { group in
            for n in 0..<100 {
                let log = n.isMultiple(of: 2) ? a : b
                group.addTask { _ = try await log.append([PendingChange(event: .created, kind: .log, key: "k\(n)", path: "k\(n).md")]) }
            }
            try await group.waitForAll()
        }
        let events = try ChangeLogFile.readEvents(root: root)
        #expect(events.map(\.seq).sorted() == Array(1...100))
        #expect(Set(events.map(\.key)).count == 100)
    }

    @Test func halfWrittenLastLineIsSkipped() throws {
        let root = try TempDir.make()
        defer { TempDir.remove(root) }
        let url = ChangeLogFile.url(root: root)
        try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: url.deletingLastPathComponent(), withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        let good = ChangeEvent(seq: 1, at: Date(timeIntervalSince1970: 1_790_000_000), event: .created, kind: .log, key: "a", path: "a.md")
        try Data((good.jsonLine() + "\n" + #"{"seq":2,"at":"2026-10-1"#).utf8).write(to: url)
        #expect(try ChangeLogFile.readEvents(root: root) == [good])
    }

    @Test func appendAfterAHalfLineStaysParseable() async throws {
        let root = try TempDir.make()
        defer { TempDir.remove(root) }
        let url = ChangeLogFile.url(root: root)
        try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: url.deletingLastPathComponent(), withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        let good = ChangeEvent(seq: 1, at: Date(timeIntervalSince1970: 1_790_000_000), event: .created, kind: .log, key: "a", path: "a.md")
        try Data((good.jsonLine() + "\n" + #"{"seq":2,"at":"2026-10-1"#).utf8).write(to: url)
        let written = try await ChangeLog(root: root).append([change(9)])
        #expect(written.map(\.seq) == [2])
        let events = try ChangeLogFile.readEvents(root: root)
        #expect(events.map(\.seq) == [1, 2])
        let lines = try String(contentsOf: url, encoding: .utf8).split(separator: "\n")
        #expect(lines.count == 3)
    }

    @Test func badLinesInTheMiddleAreSkipped() throws {
        let root = try TempDir.make()
        defer { TempDir.remove(root) }
        let url = ChangeLogFile.url(root: root)
        try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: url.deletingLastPathComponent(), withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        let a = ChangeEvent(seq: 1, at: Date(timeIntervalSince1970: 1_790_000_000), event: .created, kind: .log, key: "a", path: "a.md")
        let b = ChangeEvent(seq: 2, at: Date(timeIntervalSince1970: 1_790_000_100), event: .deleted, kind: .log, key: "a", path: "a.md")
        var bytes = Data((a.jsonLine() + "\n").utf8)
        bytes.append(contentsOf: [0xFF, 0xFE, 0x0A])
        bytes.append(Data("not json\n\(b.jsonLine())\n".utf8))
        try bytes.write(to: url)
        #expect(try ChangeLogFile.readEvents(root: root) == [a, b])
    }

    @Test func eventsUseTheInjectedClock() async throws {
        let root = try TempDir.make()
        defer { TempDir.remove(root) }
        let fixed = Date(timeIntervalSince1970: 1_790_000_000)
        let written = try await ChangeLog(root: root, now: { fixed }).append([change(1)])
        #expect(written.first?.at == fixed)
    }
}
```

```swift
        defer { TempDir.remove(root) }
        let bookmarks = ConsumerBookmarks(root: root)
        try bookmarks.ack("crystalline", upTo: 42, now: now)
        let text = try String(contentsOf: bookmarks.folder.appendingPathComponent("crystalline.json"), encoding: .utf8)
        #expect(text == "{\"seq\":42,\"updatedAt\":\"\(RFC3339.string(now))\"}\n")
        let names = try FileManager.default.contentsOfDirectory(atPath: bookmarks.folder.path)
        #expect(names.filter { $0.hasSuffix(".tmp") }.isEmpty)
    }

    @Test func concurrentAcksEndAtTheHighest() async throws {
        let root = try TempDir.make()
        defer { TempDir.remove(root) }
        let now = self.now
        try await withThrowingTaskGroup(of: Void.self) { group in
            for upTo in (1...200).shuffled() {
                group.addTask {
                    // A fresh value per task: separate lock file handles, like two processes.
                    _ = try ConsumerBookmarks(root: root).ack("crystalline", upTo: upTo, now: now)
                }
            }
            try await group.waitForAll()
        }
        #expect(try ConsumerBookmarks(root: root).bookmark("crystalline")?.seq == 200)
    }

    @Test func listsValidBookmarksOnly() throws {
        let root = try TempDir.make()
        defer { TempDir.remove(root) }
        let bookmarks = ConsumerBookmarks(root: root)
        try bookmarks.ack("zeta", upTo: 3, now: now)
        try bookmarks.ack("alpha", upTo: 5, now: now)
        try Data("{".utf8).write(to: bookmarks.folder.appendingPathComponent("broken.json"))
        try Data("{\"seq\":1,\"updatedAt\":\"2026-10-13T18:02:11+02:00\"}".utf8).write(to: bookmarks.folder.appendingPathComponent("Bad_Name.json"))
        #expect(bookmarks.all().map(\.name) == ["alpha", "zeta"])
    }

    @Test func resetAndForget() throws {
        let root = try TempDir.make()
        defer { TempDir.remove(root) }
        let bookmarks = ConsumerBookmarks(root: root)
        try bookmarks.ack("crystalline", upTo: 9, now: now)
        try bookmarks.reset("crystalline", now: now)
        #expect(try bookmarks.bookmark("crystalline")?.seq == 0)
        try bookmarks.forget("crystalline")
        #expect(try bookmarks.bookmark("crystalline") == nil)
        try bookmarks.forget("crystalline")
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `RFC3339` and `ChangeEvent`**

`Roger/RogerKit/RFC3339.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

/// RFC 3339 timestamps with the local offset (`2026-10-13T14:32:41+02:00`).
public enum RFC3339 {
    public static func string(_ date: Date, timeZone: TimeZone = .current) -> String {
        let formatter = ISO8601DateFormatter()
        formatter.timeZone = timeZone
        formatter.formatOptions = [.withInternetDateTime]
        return formatter.string(from: date)
    }

    /// Accepts any offset or `Z`, with or without fractional seconds.
    public static func date(_ text: String) -> Date? {
        let plain = ISO8601DateFormatter()
        plain.formatOptions = [.withInternetDateTime]
        if let date = plain.date(from: text) { return date }
        let fractional = ISO8601DateFormatter()
        fractional.formatOptions = [.withInternetDateTime, .withFractionalSeconds]
        return fractional.date(from: text)
    }
}
```

`Roger/RogerKit/ChangeEvent.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

public enum ChangeEventType: String, Codable, Sendable, CaseIterable {
    case created
    case updated
    case deleted
}

/// One line of `.roger/changes.jsonl`.
public struct ChangeEvent: Equatable, Sendable {
    public let seq: Int
    public let at: Date
    public let event: ChangeEventType
    public let kind: LibraryItemKind
    /// Stable item id (see `ChangeKey`).
    public let key: String
    /// Main Markdown file: library-relative, or absolute for `External/` keys.
    public let path: String

    public init(seq: Int, at: Date, event: ChangeEventType, kind: LibraryItemKind, key: String, path: String) {
        self.seq = seq
        self.at = at
        self.event = event
        self.kind = kind
        self.key = key
        self.path = path
    }

    /// One JSON object in the spec's field order, without a newline.
    /// `at` has whole seconds, so `parse(jsonLine())` gives back an equal
    /// event only for whole-second dates (Roger's clock values are
    /// truncated by RFC 3339 anyway).
    public func jsonLine(timeZone: TimeZone = .current) -> String {
        "{\"seq\":\(seq),\"at\":\(Self.json(RFC3339.string(at, timeZone: timeZone))),\"event\":\(Self.json(event.rawValue)),\"kind\":\(Self.json(kind.rawValue)),\"key\":\(Self.json(key)),\"path\":\(Self.json(path))}"
    }

    /// Nil for anything that is not a complete, valid event.
    public static func parse(_ line: Data) -> ChangeEvent? {
        guard let raw = try? JSONDecoder().decode(Raw.self, from: line),
              raw.seq >= 1,
              !raw.key.isEmpty,
              let at = RFC3339.date(raw.at),
              let event = ChangeEventType(rawValue: raw.event),
              let kind = LibraryItemKind(rawValue: raw.kind)
        else { return nil }
        return ChangeEvent(seq: raw.seq, at: at, event: event, kind: kind, key: raw.key, path: raw.path)
    }

    private struct Raw: Decodable {
        let seq: Int
        let at: String
        let event: String
        let kind: String
        let key: String
        let path: String
    }

    private static func json(_ text: String) -> String {
        let encoder = JSONEncoder()
        encoder.outputFormatting = [.withoutEscapingSlashes]
        guard let data = try? encoder.encode(text), let quoted = String(data: data, encoding: .utf8) else { return "\"\"" }
        return quoted
    }
}

/// A change to append; the log assigns `seq` and `at`.
public struct PendingChange: Equatable, Sendable {
    public let event: ChangeEventType
    public let kind: LibraryItemKind
    public let key: String
    public let path: String

    public init(event: ChangeEventType, kind: LibraryItemKind, key: String, path: String) {
        self.event = event
        self.kind = kind
        self.key = key
        self.path = path
    }
}
```

The test `keysWithQuotesAndBackslashesRoundTrip` uses a whole-second date, so the round trip is exact.

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `ChangeLog`**

`Roger/RogerKit/ChangeLog.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

extension POSIXError {
    /// The error for the current `errno`.
    static var current: POSIXError {
        POSIXError(POSIXErrorCode(rawValue: errno) ?? .EIO)
    }
}

/// Where the log lives and how readers read it. Readers take no lock: a
/// line is written with one `write` on an `O_APPEND` descriptor, and a line
/// that is not complete yet (or was cut by a crash) does not parse and is
/// skipped.
public enum ChangeLogFile {
    public static func folder(root: URL) -> URL {
        root.appendingPathComponent(".roger", isDirectory: true)
    }

    public static func url(root: URL) -> URL {
        folder(root: root).appendingPathComponent("changes.jsonl")
    }

    /// Every line that parses, in file order. Lines are split on `\n` bytes
    /// first, so a broken UTF-8 sequence spoils only its own line.
    public static func parse(_ data: Data) -> [ChangeEvent] {
        data.split(separator: 0x0A, omittingEmptySubsequences: true)
            .compactMap { ChangeEvent.parse(Data($0)) }
    }

    public static func readEvents(root: URL) throws -> [ChangeEvent] {
        parse(try Data(contentsOf: url(root: root)))
    }
}

/// The app's writer for `<root>/.roger/changes.jsonl`. Every operation runs
/// under an exclusive `flock` on the log file: read all events, decide what
/// to append, append it with one `write`. That makes `seq` unique across
/// processes (a Debug and a Release Roger) and across actor instances.
public actor ChangeLog {
    public nonisolated let root: URL
    public nonisolated let fileURL: URL
    private let now: @Sendable () -> Date

    public init(root: URL, now: @escaping @Sendable () -> Date = { Date() }) {
        self.root = root
        self.fileURL = ChangeLogFile.url(root: root)
        self.now = now
    }

    @discardableResult
    public func append(_ changes: [PendingChange]) throws -> [ChangeEvent] {
        try locked { _ in (changes, ()) }.written
    }

    /// Creates `.roger/changes.jsonl` when it is missing.
    public func ensureFile() throws {
        _ = try locked { _ in ([], ()) }
    }

    /// Runs `decide` with all events under the lock and appends what it
    /// returns. `seq` continues after the highest valid seq in the file.
    /// When the file does not end with a newline (a crash cut the last
    /// line), one is written first so the new lines parse.
    func locked<T: Sendable>(
        _ decide: ([ChangeEvent]) throws -> ([PendingChange], T)
    ) throws -> (written: [ChangeEvent], result: T) {
        try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: fileURL.deletingLastPathComponent(), withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        let fd = open(fileURL.path, O_RDWR | O_APPEND | O_CREAT | O_CLOEXEC, 0o644)
        guard fd >= 0 else { throw POSIXError.current }
        defer { close(fd) }
        while flock(fd, LOCK_EX) != 0 {
            guard errno == EINTR else { throw POSIXError.current }
        }
        defer { flock(fd, LOCK_UN) }

        let existing = try Self.readAll(fd)
        let events = ChangeLogFile.parse(existing)
        let (pending, result) = try decide(events)
        guard !pending.isEmpty else { return ([], result) }

        var seq = events.map(\.seq).max() ?? 0
        let at = now()
        var text = (existing.isEmpty || existing.last == 0x0A) ? "" : "\n"
        var written: [ChangeEvent] = []
        for change in pending {
            seq += 1
            let event = ChangeEvent(seq: seq, at: at, event: change.event, kind: change.kind, key: change.key, path: change.path)
            written.append(event)
            text += event.jsonLine() + "\n"
        }
        try Self.writeAll(fd, Data(text.utf8))
        return (written, result)
    }

    private static func readAll(_ fd: Int32) throws -> Data {
        var data = Data()
        var offset: off_t = 0
        var buffer = [UInt8](repeating: 0, count: 64 * 1024)
        while true {
            let count = buffer.withUnsafeMutableBytes { pread(fd, $0.baseAddress, $0.count, offset) }
            if count < 0 {
                if errno == EINTR { continue }
                throw POSIXError.current
            }
            if count == 0 { return data }
            data.append(contentsOf: buffer[0..<count])
            offset += off_t(count)
        }
    }

    /// One `write` call; loops only if the kernel takes fewer bytes.
    private static func writeAll(_ fd: Int32, _ data: Data) throws {
        try data.withUnsafeBytes { raw in
            guard let base = raw.baseAddress else { return }
            var offset = 0
            while offset < raw.count {
                let count = write(fd, base + offset, raw.count - offset)
                if count < 0 {
                    if errno == EINTR { continue }
                    throw POSIXError.current
                }
                offset += count
            }
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write `ConsumerBookmarks`**

`Roger/RogerKit/ConsumerBookmarks.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

public struct ConsumerBookmark: Equatable, Sendable {
    public let seq: Int
    public let updatedAt: Date

    public init(seq: Int, updatedAt: Date) {
        self.seq = seq
        self.updatedAt = updatedAt
    }
}

public struct NamedBookmark: Equatable, Sendable {
    public let name: String
    public let bookmark: ConsumerBookmark
}

public struct AckResult: Equatable, Sendable {
    public let previous: Int
    public let current: Int

    public init(previous: Int, current: Int) {
        self.previous = previous
        self.current = current
    }
}

public enum ConsumerBookmarkError: Error, Equatable, LocalizedError {
    case invalidName(String)

    public var errorDescription: String? {
        switch self {
        case .invalidName(let name):
            return "\"\(name)\" is not a valid consumer name. Use 1 to 64 characters: a-z, 0-9 and \"-\", starting with a letter or digit."
        }
    }
}

/// `<root>/.roger/consumers/<name>.json`. Every change runs under an
/// exclusive `flock` on `consumers/.lock` and replaces the file with a
/// temp file plus `rename`, so two `roger-mcp` processes acking at once
/// end at the higher seq and a reader never sees half a file.
public struct ConsumerBookmarks: Sendable {
    public let root: URL

    public init(root: URL) {
        self.root = root
    }

    public var folder: URL {
        ChangeLogFile.folder(root: root).appendingPathComponent("consumers", isDirectory: true)
    }

    /// `^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{0,63}$`, checked by hand so a trailing newline
    /// cannot slip through a regex `$`.
    public static func isValidName(_ name: String) -> Bool {
        let scalars = Array(name.unicodeScalars)
        guard (1...64).contains(scalars.count) else { return false }
        func lowerOrDigit(_ s: Unicode.Scalar) -> Bool { ("a"..."z").contains(s) || ("0"..."9").contains(s) }
        return lowerOrDigit(scalars[0]) && scalars.allSatisfy { lowerOrDigit($0) || $0 == "-" }
    }

    public func bookmark(_ name: String) throws -> ConsumerBookmark? {
        try validate(name)
        return Self.read(fileURL(name))
    }

    /// Valid bookmarks, sorted by name. Files with other names or broken
    /// content are left out.
    public func all() -> [NamedBookmark] {
        let names = (try? FileManager.default.contentsOfDirectory(atPath: folder.path)) ?? []
        return names
            .filter { $0.hasSuffix(".json") && !$0.hasPrefix(".") }
            .map { String($0.dropLast(5)) }
            .filter(Self.isValidName)
            .sorted()
            .compactMap { name in Self.read(fileURL(name)).map { NamedBookmark(name: name, bookmark: $0) } }
    }

    /// Moves the bookmark to `upTo` when that is higher. Never backwards.
    @discardableResult
    public func ack(_ name: String, upTo: Int, now: Date = Date()) throws -> AckResult {
        try validate(name)
        return try withLock {
            let previous = Self.read(fileURL(name))?.seq ?? 0
            guard upTo > previous else { return AckResult(previous: previous, current: previous) }
            try write(ConsumerBookmark(seq: upTo, updatedAt: now), name: name)
            return AckResult(previous: previous, current: upTo)
        }
    }

    /// "Start over" in the Agents tab: the bookmark goes back to 0.
    public func reset(_ name: String, now: Date = Date()) throws {
        try validate(name)
        try withLock { try write(ConsumerBookmark(seq: 0, updatedAt: now), name: name) }
    }

    /// "Forget" in the Agents tab: the bookmark file goes away.
    public func forget(_ name: String) throws {
        try validate(name)
        try withLock {
            let url = fileURL(name)
            if FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: url.path) {
                try FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url)
            }
        }
    }

    // MARK: - Helpers

    private func fileURL(_ name: String) -> URL {
        folder.appendingPathComponent("\(name).json")
    }

    private func validate(_ name: String) throws {
        guard Self.isValidName(name) else { throw ConsumerBookmarkError.invalidName(name) }
    }

    private func withLock<T>(_ body: () throws -> T) throws -> T {
        try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: folder, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        let fd = open(folder.appendingPathComponent(".lock").path, O_RDWR | O_CREAT | O_CLOEXEC, 0o644)
        guard fd >= 0 else { throw POSIXError.current }
        defer { close(fd) }
        while flock(fd, LOCK_EX) != 0 {
            guard errno == EINTR else { throw POSIXError.current }
        }
        defer { flock(fd, LOCK_UN) }
        return try body()
    }

    private func write(_ bookmark: ConsumerBookmark, name: String) throws {
        let text = "{\"seq\":\(bookmark.seq),\"updatedAt\":\"\(RFC3339.string(bookmark.updatedAt))\"}\n"
        let temp = folder.appendingPathComponent(".\(name).json.\(UUID().uuidString).tmp")
        try Data(text.utf8).write(to: temp)
        guard rename(temp.path, fileURL(name).path) == 0 else {
            let error = POSIXError.current
            try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: temp)
            throw error
        }
    }

    private struct Raw: Decodable {
        let seq: Int
        let updatedAt: String
    }

    private static func read(_ url: URL) -> ConsumerBookmark? {
        guard let data = try? Data(contentsOf: url),
              let raw = try? JSONDecoder().decode(Raw.self, from: data),
              raw.seq >= 0,
              let date = RFC3339.date(raw.updatedAt)
        else { return nil }
        return ConsumerBookmark(seq: raw.seq, updatedAt: date)
    }
}
```

### Task 4: Item keys, upsert and first fill

How an item becomes a key and a path, how writers record changes, and the catch-up that fills the log on the first start and after every start.

**Files:**
- Create: `Roger/RogerKit/ChangeKey.swift`
- Modify: `Roger/RogerKit/ChangeLog.swift` (append the extension below)
- Test: `Roger/RogerKitTests/ChangeKeyTests.swift`
- Test: `Roger/RogerKitTests/ReconcileTests.swift`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ChangeLog.locked`, `ChangeLogFile`, `PendingChange`, `ChangeEventType` (Task 3); `LibraryItem`, `LibraryPath`, `LibraryScanner` (Task 1).
- Produces:
  - `public struct ChangeSubject: Equatable, Sendable { let kind: LibraryItemKind; let key: String; let path: String; var isExternal: Bool; init(kind:key:path:) }`
  - `public enum ChangeKey { static let externalPrefix = "External/"; static func subject(for item: LibraryItem, root: URL) -> ChangeSubject?; static func subject(markdownURL: URL, kind: LibraryItemKind, root: URL) -> ChangeSubject; static func externalKey(name: String, absolutePath: String) -> String }`
  - `public enum ChangeIntent: Sendable { case written, deleted }`
  - `extension ChangeLog { @discardableResult func record(_ intent: ChangeIntent, _ subject: ChangeSubject) throws -> ChangeEvent?; @discardableResult func reconcile(items: [LibraryItem]) throws -> [ChangeEvent]; static func lastEvents(_ events: [ChangeEvent]) -> [String: ChangeEvent] }`
  - `extension PendingChange { init(_ event: ChangeEventType, _ subject: ChangeSubject) }`

```swift
import CryptoKit

struct ChangeKeyTests {
```

```swift
import Foundation
import Testing
@testable import RogerKit

struct ReconcileTests {
    private let day: TimeInterval = 86_400

    private func items(_ layout: LibraryLayout, extra: [URL] = [], external: [URL] = []) -> [LibraryItem] {
        LibraryScanner.scanAll(scope: LibraryScanScope(layout: layout, extraFolders: extra), externalFiles: external)
    }

    @Test func firstFillIsInRecordingDateOrder() async throws {
        let root = try TempDir.make()
        defer { TempDir.remove(root) }
        let layout = LibraryLayout(root: root)
        let base = KitFixtures.base
        try KitFixtures.log(in: layout, at: base.addingTimeInterval(2 * day), text: "newest")
        try KitFixtures.meeting(in: layout.monthFolder(.meetings, for: base), name: "2026-09-21 16-00", at: base, text: "oldest")
        try KitFixtures.transcript(at: layout.monthFolder(.transcripts, for: base).appendingPathComponent("talk.mp4.md"), at: base.addingTimeInterval(day), title: "Talk", text: "middle")

        let written = try await ChangeLog(root: root).reconcile(items: items(layout))
        #expect(written.map(\.seq) == [1, 2, 3])
        #expect(written.map(\.kind) == [.meeting, .transcript, .log])
        #expect(written.allSatisfy { $0.event == .created })
        #expect(written[0].key.hasSuffix("/2026-09-21 16-00"))
        #expect(written[0].path.hasSuffix("/2026-09-21 16-00/transcript.md"))
        #expect(written[1].key.hasSuffix("/talk.mp4"))
    }

    @Test func catchUpAddsOnlyMissingItems() async throws {
        let root = try TempDir.make()
```
