- [ ] **Step 4: Write `ChangeKey`**

`Roger/RogerKit/ChangeKey.swift`:

```swift
import CryptoKit
import Foundation

/// What the change log says about one item.
public struct ChangeSubject: Equatable, Sendable {
    public let kind: LibraryItemKind
    public let key: String
    public let path: String

    public init(kind: LibraryItemKind, key: String, path: String) {
        self.kind = kind
        self.key = key
        self.path = path
    }

    public var isExternal: Bool { key.hasPrefix(ChangeKey.externalPrefix) }
}

/// Stable item ids. Inside the library: the library-relative path without
/// `.md` (meetings: the session folder), and `path` is the relative path of
/// the Markdown file. Outside the library (transcripts next to their
/// source, items in extra scan folders): `External/<name>-<hash8>` with the
/// canonical absolute path. All paths are canonical (`LibraryPath`), so
/// `/var/...` and `/private/var/...` give the same key.
public enum ChangeKey {
    public static let externalPrefix = "External/"

    /// Nil for items without Markdown (unfinished meetings).
    public static func subject(for item: LibraryItem, root: URL) -> ChangeSubject? {
        guard item.hasMarkdown else { return nil }
        return subject(markdownURL: item.url, kind: item.kind, root: root)
    }

    public static func subject(markdownURL: URL, kind: LibraryItemKind, root: URL) -> ChangeSubject {
        let rootPath = LibraryPath.canonical(root)
        let rogerPath = LibraryPath.canonical(ChangeLogFile.folder(root: root))
        let markdown = LibraryPath.canonical(markdownURL)
        let isSession = kind == .meeting && markdownURL.lastPathComponent == "transcript.md"
        let subjectPath = isSession ? (markdown as NSString).deletingLastPathComponent : markdown

        if subjectPath != rootPath,
           LibraryPath.isPath(subjectPath, inside: rootPath),
           !LibraryPath.isPath(subjectPath, inside: rogerPath) {
            let relativeMarkdown = LibraryPath.components(of: markdown, below: rootPath).joined(separator: "/")
            let relativeSubject = LibraryPath.components(of: subjectPath, below: rootPath).joined(separator: "/")
            return ChangeSubject(
                kind: kind,
                key: isSession ? relativeSubject : withoutMarkdownExtension(relativeSubject),
                path: relativeMarkdown
            )
        }
        let name = isSession
            ? (subjectPath as NSString).lastPathComponent
            : withoutMarkdownExtension((markdown as NSString).lastPathComponent)
        return ChangeSubject(kind: kind, key: externalKey(name: name, absolutePath: subjectPath), path: markdown)
    }

    /// `External/<name>-<first 8 hex of SHA-256(absolutePath)>`.
    public static func externalKey(name: String, absolutePath: String) -> String {
        let hex = SHA256.hash(data: Data(absolutePath.utf8)).map { String(format: "%02x", $0) }.joined()
        return externalPrefix + name + "-" + hex.prefix(8)
    }

    private static func withoutMarkdownExtension(_ path: String) -> String {
        path.lowercased().hasSuffix(".md") ? String(path.dropLast(3)) : path
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Add `record` and `reconcile`**

Append to `Roger/RogerKit/ChangeLog.swift`:

```swift
/// What a writer did to an item.
public enum ChangeIntent: Sendable {
    /// The item's Markdown was written (new, edited, renamed, rewritten).
    case written
    /// The item went to the Trash.
    case deleted
}

extension PendingChange {
    public init(_ event: ChangeEventType, _ subject: ChangeSubject) {
        self.init(event: event, kind: subject.kind, key: subject.key, path: subject.path)
    }
}

extension ChangeLog {
    /// `.written` appends `created` when the key never appeared or its last
    /// event is `deleted`, else `updated`. `.deleted` appends only when the
    /// key's last event exists and is not `deleted`. Nil when nothing was
    /// appended.
    @discardableResult
    public func record(_ intent: ChangeIntent, _ subject: ChangeSubject) throws -> ChangeEvent? {
        try locked { events in
            let last = Self.lastEvents(events)[subject.key]
            switch intent {
            case .written:
                let type: ChangeEventType = (last == nil || last?.event == .deleted) ? .created : .updated
                return ([PendingChange(type, subject)], ())
            case .deleted:
                guard let last, last.event != .deleted else { return ([], ()) }
                return ([PendingChange(.deleted, subject)], ())
            }
        }.written.first
    }

    /// First fill and catch-up: `created` for every item whose key never
    /// appeared or whose last event is `deleted` (the file exists again),
    /// oldest recording first. Items without Markdown are skipped. Creates
    /// an empty log when there is nothing to add. Does not notice edits or
    /// deletions made outside Roger.
    @discardableResult
    public func reconcile(items: [LibraryItem]) throws -> [ChangeEvent] {
        let root = self.root
        let subjects = items
            .sorted { ($0.date, $0.url.path) < ($1.date, $1.url.path) }
            .compactMap { ChangeKey.subject(for: $0, root: root) }
        return try locked { events in
            let last = Self.lastEvents(events)
            var seen = Set<String>()
            var pending: [PendingChange] = []
            for subject in subjects where seen.insert(subject.key).inserted {
                if let event = last[subject.key], event.event != .deleted { continue }
                pending.append(PendingChange(.created, subject))
            }
            return (pending, ())
        }.written
    }

    /// The event with the highest seq per key.
    public static func lastEvents(_ events: [ChangeEvent]) -> [String: ChangeEvent] {
        var result: [String: ChangeEvent] = [:]
        for event in events where (result[event.key]?.seq ?? 0) < event.seq {
            result[event.key] = event
        }
        return result
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the recorder**

`Roger/Services/Library/LibraryChangeRecorder.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation
import os

private let logger = Logger(subsystem: "com.jordiboehme.roger", category: "LibraryChanges")

/// Where the recorder writes and what a catch-up scans.
struct LibraryChangeEnvironment: Sendable {
    let layout: LibraryLayout
    let extraFolders: [URL]
    let externalRegistry: ExternalTranscriptRegistry
}

/// The app's side of the change log (`<root>/.roger/changes.jsonl`).
/// Calls come from the main actor and run one after the other off the main
/// actor, in call order. After the first operation on a root (at launch,
/// after the library folder changed) that root is caught up: every item
/// that is not in its log yet gets `created`. A failed write is logged as a
/// warning; the next catch-up adds the item.
@MainActor
final class LibraryChangeRecorder {
    /// Nil until the coordinator sets it. Calls before that do nothing.
    var environment: (@MainActor () -> LibraryChangeEnvironment)?
    private var tail: Task<Void, Never>?
    private var logs: [String: ChangeLog] = [:]
    private var caughtUpRoot: String?

    init(environment: (@MainActor () -> LibraryChangeEnvironment)? = nil) {
        self.environment = environment
    }

    /// The item's Markdown is on disk (new, edited, renamed or rewritten).
    func recordWritten(markdownURL: URL, kind: LibraryItemKind) {
        guard let env = environment?() else { return }
        let subject = ChangeKey.subject(markdownURL: markdownURL, kind: kind, root: env.layout.root)
        enqueue(env) { log in
            try await log.record(.written, subject)
        }
    }

    /// These items went to the Trash.
    func recordDeleted(_ items: [LibraryItem]) {
        guard let env = environment?(), !items.isEmpty else { return }
        let subjects = items.compactMap { ChangeKey.subject(for: $0, root: env.layout.root) }
        enqueue(env) { log in
            for subject in subjects {
                try await log.record(.deleted, subject)
            }
        }
    }

    /// Catches up the current root now. Called at launch and when the
    /// library folder changes.
    func reconcile() {
        guard let env = environment?() else { return }
        caughtUpRoot = nil
        enqueue(env) { _ in }
    }

    /// Waits until every queued operation ran. For tests.
    func waitUntilIdle() async {
        await tail?.value
    }

    private func enqueue(_ env: LibraryChangeEnvironment, _ operation: @escaping @Sendable (ChangeLog) async throws -> Void) {
        let rootKey = env.layout.root.standardizedFileURL.path
        let log = logs[rootKey] ?? ChangeLog(root: env.layout.root)
        logs[rootKey] = log
        let needsCatchUp = caughtUpRoot != rootKey
        caughtUpRoot = rootKey
        let previous = tail
        tail = Task.detached(priority: .utility) {
            await previous?.value
            // The operation first: an item written a moment ago is then
            // `created` by its own record, not by the catch-up (which would
            // make the record an `updated`).
            do {
                try await operation(log)
            } catch {
                logger.warning("Change log not updated: \(error.localizedDescription, privacy: .public)")
            }
            guard needsCatchUp else { return }
            do {
                let items = LibraryScanner.scanAll(
                    scope: LibraryScanScope(layout: env.layout, extraFolders: env.extraFolders),
                    externalFiles: env.externalRegistry.listedFiles()
                )
                let added = try await log.reconcile(items: items).count
                logger.notice("Change log caught up, \(added) items added")
            } catch {
                logger.warning("Change log not caught up: \(error.localizedDescription, privacy: .public)")
            }
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Add `trashedItems`**

Append to `Roger/Services/Library/LibraryChangeRecorder.swift`:

```swift
extension LibraryChangeRecorder {
    /// The items whose own file really went to the Trash: a meeting's session
    /// folder (or its `transcript.md`), otherwise the item's Markdown file.
    /// A log's `.m4a` is not an item; failed and skipped URLs do not count.
    /// Paths are compared canonically; call this right after the move, while
    /// the parent folders still exist.
    nonisolated static func trashedItems(_ items: [LibraryItem], plan: LibraryActions.TrashPlan, failed: [URL]) -> [LibraryItem] {
        let failedPaths = Set(failed.map { LibraryPath.canonical($0) })
        let moved = Set(plan.urls.map { LibraryPath.canonical($0) }).subtracting(failedPaths)
        return items.filter { item in
            moved.contains(LibraryPath.canonical(item.url))
                || (item.kind == .meeting && moved.contains(LibraryPath.canonical(item.folderURL)))
        }
    }
}
```

`Roger/UI/Library/LibraryListView.swift`: `runTrash` takes the items the plan was made for:

```swift
    private func runTrash(_ plan: LibraryActions.TrashPlan, items: [LibraryItem]) {
        // Editing may have started while the question was open.
        guard !model.isEditing else { return }
        let failed = LibraryActions.moveToTrash(plan.urls)
        coordinator.libraryChanges.recordDeleted(
            LibraryChangeRecorder.trashedItems(items, plan: plan, failed: failed)
        )
        model.selection = []
        if plan.meetingFolderCount > 0 {
            // A trashed unfinished meeting must leave the popover banner and
            // the Unfinished list now, not after the next recording.
            coordinator.refreshPendingMeetingSessions()
        }
        message = LibraryActions.trashFailureMessage(failed) ?? LibraryActions.trashSkippedMessage(plan)
    }
```

The confirmation button calls `runTrash(commands.trashPlan(for: pendingTrashItems).limited(to: confirmed), items: pendingTrashItems)` (before `pendingTrashItems = []`), and `requestTrash` calls `runTrash(plan, items: items)`. `LibraryExtraFilesSection` keeps calling `LibraryActions.moveToTrash` directly and records nothing (Ruling 7).

  - `public struct SearchHit: Equatable, Sendable { let item: LibraryItem; let snippet: String }`

```swift
import Foundation
import Testing
@testable import RogerKit

struct ChangeQueryTests {
    private let base = KitFixtures.base

    private func event(_ seq: Int, _ type: ChangeEventType, _ subject: ChangeSubject, at: Date? = nil) -> ChangeEvent {
        ChangeEvent(seq: seq, at: at ?? base.addingTimeInterval(Double(seq)), event: type, kind: subject.kind, key: subject.key, path: subject.path)
    }

    private func log(_ root: URL, minute: Int, text: String) throws -> ChangeSubject {
        let md = try KitFixtures.log(in: LibraryLayout(root: root), at: base.addingTimeInterval(Double(minute) * 60), text: text)
        return ChangeKey.subject(markdownURL: md, kind: .log, root: root)
    }

    @Test func oneEntryPerKeyAtItsLatestSeq() throws {
        let root = try TempDir.make()
        defer { TempDir.remove(root) }
        let a = try log(root, minute: 0, text: "alpha")
        let b = try log(root, minute: 1, text: "bravo")
        let events = [event(1, .created, a), event(2, .created, b), event(3, .updated, a)]
        let answer = ChangeQuery.run(events: events, start: .afterSeq(0), kinds: nil, summary: false, root: root)
        #expect(answer.entries.map(\.seq) == [2, 3])
        #expect(answer.entries.map(\.event) == [.created, .updated])
        #expect(answer.from == 1 && answer.upTo == 3 && !answer.more)
        #expect(answer.entries[1].markdown?.contains("alpha") == true)
        #expect(answer.entries[1].paths.first?.hasSuffix(KitFixtures.stem(base) + ".md") == true)
    }

    @Test func deletedEntryHasNoTextAndNoPaths() throws {
        let root = try TempDir.make()
        defer { TempDir.remove(root) }
        let a = try log(root, minute: 0, text: "alpha")
        let answer = ChangeQuery.run(events: [event(1, .created, a), event(2, .deleted, a)], start: .afterSeq(0), kinds: nil, summary: false, root: root)
        let entry = try #require(answer.entries.first)
        #expect(entry.event == .deleted && entry.markdown == nil && entry.paths.isEmpty && entry.title == nil)
        #expect(!entry.render().contains("paths"))
    }

    @Test func missingFileIsMarked() throws {
        let root = try TempDir.make()
        defer { TempDir.remove(root) }
        let a = try log(root, minute: 0, text: "alpha")
        try FileManager.default.removeItem(at: root.appendingPathComponent(a.path))

        #expect(entry.missing && entry.markdown == nil && entry.paths.isEmpty)
        #expect(entry.render().contains("missing: true"))

        #expect(first.entries.count == 2)
        #expect(first.more)
        #expect(first.upTo == 2)

        #expect(second.entries.map(\.seq) == [3, 4])
        let last = ChangeQuery.run(events: events, start: .afterSeq(second.upTo), kinds: nil, summary: false, root: root)
        #expect(last.entries.map(\.seq) == [5])
        #expect(!last.more && last.upTo == 5)
    }

    @Test func aSingleOversizedEntryComesWhole() throws {
        let root = try TempDir.make()
        defer { TempDir.remove(root) }
        let big = try log(root, minute: 0, text: String(repeating: "x", count: 45_000))

        let meeting = ChangeKey.subject(markdownURL: root.appendingPathComponent("Meetings/2026/10/2026-10-13 09-00/transcript.md"), kind: .meeting, root: root)
        let answer = ChangeQuery.run(events: [event(1, .created, meeting), event(2, .created, a)], start: .afterSeq(0), kinds: [.meeting], summary: false, root: root)

        let subjects = try (0..<5).map { try log(root, minute: $0, text: text) }

        let a = try log(root, minute: 0, text: "alpha")
        let answer = ChangeQuery.run(events: [event(1, .created, a)], start: .afterSeq(1), kinds: nil, summary: false, root: root)
        #expect(answer.entries.isEmpty && answer.from == 2 && answer.upTo == 1 && !answer.more)
        #expect(answer.render() == "from: 2\nupTo: 1\nmore: false\ncount: 0")

    @Test func rangeFormIsFixed() throws {

        defer { TempDir.remove(root) }
        let subjects = try (0..<5).map { try log(root, minute: $0, text: "n\($0)") }
        let events = subjects.enumerated().map { event($0.offset + 1, .created, $0.element) }
        let answer = ChangeQuery.run(events: events, start: .range(from: 2, upTo: 3), kinds: nil, summary: false, root: root)
        #expect(answer.entries.map(\.seq) == [2, 3])
        #expect(answer.from == 2 && answer.upTo == 3)
    }

    @Test func timeStartUsesEventTimes() throws {
        let root = try TempDir.make()
        defer { TempDir.remove(root) }
        let a = try log(root, minute: 0, text: "alpha")
        let b = try log(root, minute: 1, text: "bravo")
        let events = [event(1, .created, a, at: base), event(2, .created, b, at: base.addingTimeInterval(3_600))]
        let answer = ChangeQuery.run(events: events, start: .atOrAfter(base.addingTimeInterval(60)), kinds: nil, summary: false, root: root)
        #expect(answer.entries.map(\.seq) == [2])
        #expect(answer.from == 2)
    }

    @Test func renderedAnswerHasHeadThenEntries() throws {
        let root = try TempDir.make()
        defer { TempDir.remove(root) }
        let a = try log(root, minute: 0, text: "alpha")
        let text = ChangeQuery.run(events: [event(1, .created, a)], start: .afterSeq(0), kinds: nil, summary: false, root: root).render()
        #expect(text.hasPrefix("from: 1\nupTo: 1\nmore: false\ncount: 1\n\n==== roger entry 1 ====\nseq: 1\nevent: created\nkind: log\nkey: "))
        #expect(text.contains("\npaths[1]: /"))
        #expect(text.contains("\n\n---\ntype: source"))
    }
}

struct SinceParserTests {
    private let now = Date(timeIntervalSince1970: 1_790_000_000)

    @Test func seq() {
        #expect(SinceParser.parse("12", now: now) == .afterSeq(12))
        #expect(SinceParser.parse("0", now: now) == .afterSeq(0))
    }

    @Test func dayIsLocalMidnight() throws {
        var calendar = Calendar(identifier: .gregorian)
        calendar.timeZone = try #require(TimeZone(identifier: "Europe/Berlin"))
        let expected = try #require(RFC3339.date("2026-10-13T00:00:00+02:00"))
        #expect(SinceParser.parse("2026-10-13", now: now, calendar: calendar) == .atOrAfter(expected))
    }

    @Test func rfc3339() throws {
        #expect(SinceParser.parse("2026-10-13T14:32:41+02:00", now: now) == .atOrAfter(try #require(RFC3339.date("2026-10-13T14:32:41+02:00"))))
    }

    @Test func durations() {
        #expect(SinceParser.parse("30m", now: now) == .atOrAfter(now.addingTimeInterval(-1_800)))
        #expect(SinceParser.parse("12h", now: now) == .atOrAfter(now.addingTimeInterval(-43_200)))
        #expect(SinceParser.parse("3d", now: now) == .atOrAfter(now.addingTimeInterval(-259_200)))
    }

    @Test(arguments: ["", "yesterday", "-1", "3w", "h", "2026-13-45", "12 h"])
    func rejects(raw: String) {
        #expect(SinceParser.parse(raw, now: now) == nil)
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `ItemResolver`**

`Roger/RogerKit/ItemResolver.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

public enum ItemKeyError: Error, Equatable, LocalizedError {
    case invalid(String)
    case notFound(String)

    public var errorDescription: String? {
        switch self {
        case .invalid(let key):
            return "\"\(key)\" is not a valid key. Use a key from get_changes, list_items or search."
        case .notFound(let key):
            return "No item with the key \"\(key)\"."
        }
    }
}

/// Turns change-log paths and tool keys into items on disk. Keys never
/// leave the library: they are relative, have no `.`, `..` or empty parts,
/// no hidden parts and resolve (after symlinks) inside the root. `External/`
/// keys are only valid when the change log knows them.
public enum ItemResolver {
    public static let imageExtensions: Set<String> = ["png", "jpg", "jpeg", "heic", "tiff", "gif"]

    /// `path` from an event: relative to the root, or absolute for `External/` keys.
    public static func markdownURL(path: String, root: URL) -> URL {
        path.hasPrefix("/") ? URL(fileURLWithPath: path) : root.appendingPathComponent(path)
    }

    /// The item as it is on disk now. Nil when the Markdown file is gone.
    public static func item(path: String, kind: LibraryItemKind, root: URL) -> LibraryItem? {
        let url = markdownURL(path: path, root: root)
        guard FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: url.path) else { return nil }
        let folder = (kind == .meeting && url.lastPathComponent == "transcript.md") ? url.deletingLastPathComponent() : nil
        guard var item = LibraryItemReader.item(markdownAt: url, fallbackKind: kind, folder: folder) else { return nil }
        item.kind = kind
        return item
    }

    /// Absolute paths: the Markdown file, its audio (a file transcript's
    /// source media included) and, for meetings, the screenshots in the
    /// session folder, sorted by name.
    public static func paths(of item: LibraryItem) -> [String] {
        var result = [LibraryPath.canonical(item.url)]
        result += item.audioFiles.map { LibraryPath.canonical($0) }
        if item.kind == .meeting {
            let names = (try? FileManager.default.contentsOfDirectory(atPath: item.folderURL.path)) ?? []
            result += names
                .filter { !$0.hasPrefix(".") && imageExtensions.contains(($0 as NSString).pathExtension.lowercased()) }
                .sorted()
                .map { LibraryPath.canonical(item.folderURL.appendingPathComponent($0)) }
        }
        var seen = Set<String>()
        return result.filter { seen.insert($0).inserted }
    }

    /// The whole file as text; nil when it cannot be read as UTF-8.
    public static func markdown(of item: LibraryItem) -> String? {
        (try? Data(contentsOf: item.url)).flatMap { String(data: $0, encoding: .utf8) }
    }

    /// The item behind a key passed to a tool.
    public static func subject(forKey key: String, root: URL, events: [ChangeEvent]) throws -> ChangeSubject {
        let parts = key.split(separator: "/", omittingEmptySubsequences: false)
        guard !key.isEmpty,
              !key.hasPrefix("/"),
              !key.hasPrefix("~"),
              !key.contains("\\"),
              !key.contains("\0"),
              !parts.contains(where: { $0.isEmpty || $0 == "." || $0 == ".." || $0.hasPrefix(".") })
        else { throw ItemKeyError.invalid(key) }

        if key.hasPrefix(ChangeKey.externalPrefix) {
            guard let last = ChangeLog.lastEvents(events)[key], last.event != .deleted else { throw ItemKeyError.notFound(key) }
            return ChangeSubject(kind: last.kind, key: key, path: last.path)
        }

        let rootPath = LibraryPath.canonical(root)
        let base = URL(fileURLWithPath: rootPath, isDirectory: true)
        let candidates: [(URL, LibraryItemKind)] = [
            (base.appendingPathComponent(key + ".md"), key.hasPrefix("Logs/") ? .log : .transcript),
            (base.appendingPathComponent(key, isDirectory: true).appendingPathComponent("transcript.md"), .meeting),
        ]
        for (url, fallback) in candidates where FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: url.path) {
            guard LibraryPath.isPath(LibraryPath.canonical(url), inside: rootPath) else { throw ItemKeyError.invalid(key) }
            guard let item = LibraryItemReader.item(markdownAt: url, fallbackKind: fallback) else { continue }
            let kind: LibraryItemKind = url.lastPathComponent == "transcript.md" && fallback == .meeting ? .meeting : item.kind
            return ChangeKey.subject(markdownURL: url, kind: kind, root: root)
        }
        throw ItemKeyError.notFound(key)
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write `SinceParser` and `ChangeQuery`**

`Roger/RogerKit/SinceParser.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

/// Where a `get_changes` range starts.
public enum ChangeStart: Equatable, Sendable {
    /// Events with a seq greater than this (a consumer's bookmark, `since: <seq>`).
    case afterSeq(Int)
    /// Events whose `at` is at or after this time.
    case atOrAfter(Date)
    /// The fixed inclusive range.
    case range(from: Int, upTo: Int)
}

/// The four `since` forms: a seq, a day (`YYYY-MM-DD`, local midnight), an
/// RFC 3339 time or a duration (`30m`, `12h`, `3d`).
public enum SinceParser {
    public static func parse(_ raw: String, now: Date, calendar: Calendar = .current) -> ChangeStart? {
        let text = raw.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
        guard !text.isEmpty else { return nil }
        if text.allSatisfy(\.isASCIIDigit), let seq = Int(text) {
            return .afterSeq(seq)
        }
        if text.range(of: #"^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}$"#, options: .regularExpression) != nil {
            let formatter = DateFormatter()
            formatter.locale = Locale(identifier: "en_US_POSIX")
            formatter.calendar = calendar
            formatter.timeZone = calendar.timeZone
            formatter.dateFormat = "yyyy-MM-dd"
            formatter.isLenient = false
            return formatter.date(from: text).map { .atOrAfter(calendar.startOfDay(for: $0)) }
        }
        if let unit = text.last, "mhd".contains(unit) {
            let number = text.dropLast()
            guard !number.isEmpty, number.allSatisfy(\.isASCIIDigit), let count = Int(number) else { return nil }
            let seconds: Double = unit == "m" ? 60 : unit == "h" ? 3_600 : 86_400
            return .atOrAfter(now.addingTimeInterval(-Double(count) * seconds))
        }
        return RFC3339.date(text).map { .atOrAfter($0) }
    }
}

private extension Character {
    var isASCIIDigit: Bool { ("0"..."9").contains(self) }
}
```

`Roger/RogerKit/ChangeQuery.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

/// One item in a `get_changes` answer, at its latest event in the range.
public struct ChangeEntry: Equatable, Sendable {
    public let seq: Int
    public let event: ChangeEventType
    public let kind: LibraryItemKind
    public let key: String
    public let title: String?
    public let date: Date?
    public let duration: Int?
    public let paths: [String]
    /// The event says the item exists, but its Markdown file is gone.
    public let missing: Bool
    /// The whole file; nil for `deleted`, `missing` and summaries.
    public let markdown: String?

    /// `==== roger entry <seq> ====`, TOON fields, then the Markdown after
    /// a blank line.
    public func render() -> String {
        var lines = [
            "==== roger entry \(seq) ====",
            TOON.field("seq", .int(seq)),
            TOON.field("event", .string(event.rawValue)),
            TOON.field("kind", .string(kind.rawValue)),
            TOON.field("key", .string(key)),
        ]
        if missing { lines.append(TOON.field("missing", .bool(true))) }
        if let title { lines.append(TOON.field("title", .string(title))) }
        if let date { lines.append(TOON.field("date", .string(RFC3339.string(date)))) }
        if event != .deleted, !missing { lines.append(TOON.field("duration", duration.map { .int($0) } ?? .null)) }
        if !paths.isEmpty { lines.append(TOON.inlineArray("paths", paths.map { .string($0) })) }
        var text = lines.joined(separator: "\n")
        if let markdown {
            var body = markdown
            while body.hasSuffix("\n") { body.removeLast() }
            text += "\n\n" + body
        }
        return text
    }
}

public struct ChangeAnswer: Equatable, Sendable {
    public let from: Int
    public let upTo: Int
    public let more: Bool
    public let entries: [ChangeEntry]

    /// TOON head (`from`, `upTo`, `more`, `count`), then the entries.
    public func render() -> String {
        let head = [
            TOON.field("from", .int(from)),
            TOON.field("upTo", .int(upTo)),
            TOON.field("more", .bool(more)),
            TOON.field("count", .int(entries.count)),
        ].joined(separator: "\n")
        return ([head] + entries.map { $0.render() }).joined(separator: "\n\n")
    }
}

/// `get_changes`: select the range, keep the latest event per key at that
/// event's position, filter by kind, read each item and cut at an item
/// boundary when the text passes the limit (a single entry above the limit
/// still comes whole).
public enum ChangeQuery {
    public static let textLimit = 30_000
    public static let summaryLimit = 300_000

    public static func run(
        events: [ChangeEvent],
        start: ChangeStart,
        kinds: Set<LibraryItemKind>?,
        summary: Bool,
        root: URL,
        limit: Int? = nil
    ) -> ChangeAnswer {
        let sorted = events.sorted { $0.seq < $1.seq }
        let lastSeq = sorted.last?.seq ?? 0
        let from: Int
        let end: Int
        switch start {
        case .afterSeq(let seq):
            from = seq + 1
            end = lastSeq
        case .atOrAfter(let date):
            from = sorted.first { $0.at >= date }?.seq ?? lastSeq + 1
            end = lastSeq
        case .range(let first, let last):
            from = first
            end = last
        }

        var latest: [String: ChangeEvent] = [:]
        for event in sorted where event.seq >= from && event.seq <= end {
            latest[event.key] = event
        }
        var grouped = latest.values.sorted { $0.seq < $1.seq }
        if let kinds { grouped = grouped.filter { kinds.contains($0.kind) } }

        let budget = limit ?? (summary ? summaryLimit : textLimit)
        var entries: [ChangeEntry] = []
        var used = 0
        var more = false
        for event in grouped {
            let entry = makeEntry(event, root: root, includeText: !summary)
            let size = entry.render().count
            if !entries.isEmpty, used + size > budget {
                more = true
                break
            }
            entries.append(entry)
            used += size
        }
        let upTo = more ? (entries.last?.seq ?? end) : end
        return ChangeAnswer(from: from, upTo: upTo, more: more, entries: entries)
    }

    private static func makeEntry(_ event: ChangeEvent, root: URL, includeText: Bool) -> ChangeEntry {
        guard event.event != .deleted else {
            return ChangeEntry(seq: event.seq, event: event.event, kind: event.kind, key: event.key, title: nil, date: nil,
                               duration: nil, paths: [], missing: false, markdown: nil)
        }
        guard let item = ItemResolver.item(path: event.path, kind: event.kind, root: root) else {
            return ChangeEntry(seq: event.seq, event: event.event, kind: event.kind, key: event.key, title: nil, date: nil,
                               duration: nil, paths: [], missing: true, markdown: nil)
        }
        return ChangeEntry(
            seq: event.seq,
            event: event.event,
            kind: event.kind,
            key: event.key,
            title: item.title,
            date: item.date,
            duration: item.durationSeconds,
            paths: ItemResolver.paths(of: item),
            missing: false,
            markdown: includeText ? ItemResolver.markdown(of: item) : nil
        )
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write `ItemSearch`**

`Roger/RogerKit/ItemSearch.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

public struct SearchHit: Equatable, Sendable {
    public let item: LibraryItem
    public let snippet: String
}

/// `search`: a case-insensitive scan over title and body of every item with
/// Markdown. Every word must match. Newest first. No index (same reasoning
/// as the library window).
public enum ItemSearch {
    public static let snippetLength = 160

    public static func search(_ query: String, in items: [LibraryItem], kind: LibraryItemKind?, limit: Int) -> [SearchHit] {
        let words = query.lowercased().split(whereSeparator: \.isWhitespace).map(String.init)
        guard !words.isEmpty, limit > 0 else { return [] }
        return items
            .filter { item in
                item.hasMarkdown
                    && (kind == nil || item.kind == kind)
                    && words.allSatisfy { item.searchText.contains($0) }
            }
            .sorted { $0.date > $1.date }
            .prefix(limit)
            .map { SearchHit(item: $0, snippet: snippet(for: $0, words: words)) }
    }

    /// Up to `snippetLength` characters of the body around the first match,
    /// whitespace folded to single spaces, with "..." where text was cut.
    public static func snippet(for item: LibraryItem, words: [String]) -> String {
        let body = ItemResolver.markdown(of: item).map { String(LibraryFrontmatterParser.split($0).body) } ?? item.firstLine
        let flat = body.split(whereSeparator: \.isWhitespace).joined(separator: " ")
        let first = words
            .compactMap { flat.range(of: $0, options: .caseInsensitive) }
            .min { $0.lowerBound < $1.lowerBound }
        guard let first else { return String(flat.prefix(snippetLength)) }
        let start = flat.index(first.lowerBound, offsetBy: -60, limitedBy: flat.startIndex) ?? flat.startIndex
        let end = flat.index(start, offsetBy: snippetLength, limitedBy: flat.endIndex) ?? flat.endIndex
        var snippet = String(flat[start..<end])
        if start > flat.startIndex { snippet = "..." + snippet }
        if end < flat.endIndex { snippet += "..." }
        return snippet
    }
}
```

```swift
import Foundation
import Testing
@testable import RogerKit

/// Preferences that a test can change between calls, like Roger's settings.
final class PreferencesBox: @unchecked Sendable {
    private let lock = NSLock()
    private var value: RogerPreferences
    init(_ value: RogerPreferences) { self.value = value }
    func get() -> RogerPreferences { lock.withLock { value } }
    func set(_ newValue: RogerPreferences) { lock.withLock { value = newValue } }
}

struct RogerToolsTests {
    private let now = Date(timeIntervalSince1970: 1_790_000_000)

    private func tools(_ root: URL) -> RogerTools {
        let now = self.now
        return RogerTools(
            locator: LibraryLocator(argument: root.path, preferences: { RogerPreferences(libraryRoot: nil, extraScanFolders: []) }),
            registry: ExternalTranscriptRegistry(fileURL: root.appendingPathComponent("none.json")),
            now: { now }
        )
    }

    /// Two logs and a meeting, caught up into the change log (seq 1 to 3).
    private func library() async throws -> URL {
        let root = try TempDir.make()
        let layout = LibraryLayout(root: root)
        try KitFixtures.log(in: layout, at: KitFixtures.base, text: "Called the supplier", audio: true)
        try KitFixtures.log(in: layout, at: KitFixtures.base.addingTimeInterval(60), text: "Invoice arrived")
        try KitFixtures.meeting(in: layout.monthFolder(.meetings, for: KitFixtures.base), name: "2026-09-21 16-00",
                                at: KitFixtures.base.addingTimeInterval(120), text: "Supplier meeting", screenshot: true)
        let items = LibraryScanner.scanAll(scope: LibraryScanScope(layout: layout, extraFolders: []), externalFiles: [])
        try await ChangeLog(root: root).reconcile(items: items)
        return root
    }

    @Test func statusBeforeSetUp() throws {
        let root = try TempDir.make()
        defer { TempDir.remove(root) }
        let status = tools(root).call("status", arguments: [:])
        #expect(!status.isError)
        #expect(status.text.contains("Start Roger once to set up the library"))
        let changes = tools(root).call("get_changes", arguments: ["consumer": .string("crystalline")])
        #expect(changes.isError)
        #expect(changes.text.contains("Start Roger once to set up the library"))

    @Test func statusReportsCountsAndConsumers() async throws {

        _ = tools(root).call("ack", arguments: ["consumer": .string("crystalline"), "upTo": .int(1)])
        let text = tools(root).call("status", arguments: [:]).text

        #expect(text.contains("lastSeq: 3"))

        #expect(text.contains("consumers[1]{name,seq,updatedAt,age,open}:\n  crystalline,1,"))
        #expect(text.hasSuffix(",0m,2"))

    @Test(arguments: [
        [String: ToolValue](),
        ["consumer": .string("crystalline"), "since": .int(1)],
        ["consumer": .string("crystalline"), "from": .int(1), "upTo": .int(2)],
        ["from": .int(1)],
        ["upTo": .int(2)],

    func getChangesNeedsExactlyOneStart(arguments: [String: ToolValue]) async throws {
        let root = try await library()
        defer { TempDir.remove(root) }
        let output = tools(root).call("get_changes", arguments: arguments)
        #expect(output.isError)
        #expect(output.text.contains("exactly one start") || output.text.contains("together"))
    }

    @Test func consumerLoopEndsEmpty() async throws {
        let root = try await library()
        defer { TempDir.remove(root) }
        let first = tools(root).call("get_changes", arguments: ["consumer": .string("crystalline")])
        #expect(!first.isError)
        #expect(first.text.hasPrefix("from: 1\nupTo: 3\nmore: false\ncount: 3"))
        let ack = tools(root).call("ack", arguments: ["consumer": .string("crystalline"), "upTo": .int(3)])
        #expect(ack.text == "Bookmark crystalline is now 3 (was 0).")
        let again = tools(root).call("get_changes", arguments: ["consumer": .string("crystalline")])
        #expect(again.text == "from: 4\nupTo: 3\nmore: false\ncount: 0")
    }

    @Test func sinceTakesNumbersAsIntegersOrStrings() async throws {
        let root = try await library()
        defer { TempDir.remove(root) }
        for value in [ToolValue.int(2), .string("2"), .double(2)] {
            let text = tools(root).call("get_changes", arguments: ["since": value]).text
            #expect(text.hasPrefix("from: 3\nupTo: 3\nmore: false\ncount: 1"))
        }
        let bad = tools(root).call("get_changes", arguments: ["since": .string("last tuesday")])
        #expect(bad.isError)
        #expect(bad.text.contains("30m, 12h or 3d"))
    }

    @Test func rangeMustBeInsideTheLog() async throws {
        let root = try await library()
        defer { TempDir.remove(root) }
        #expect(tools(root).call("get_changes", arguments: ["from": .int(2), "upTo": .int(9)]).isError)
        #expect(tools(root).call("get_changes", arguments: ["from": .int(0), "upTo": .int(2)]).isError)
        #expect(tools(root).call("get_changes", arguments: ["from": .int(3), "upTo": .int(2)]).isError)
        let ok = tools(root).call("get_changes", arguments: ["from": .int(2), "upTo": .int(3), "summary": .bool(true)])
        #expect(ok.text.hasPrefix("from: 2\nupTo: 3\nmore: false\ncount: 2"))
    }

    @Test func kindsAreChecked() async throws {
        let root = try await library()
        defer { TempDir.remove(root) }
        let bad = tools(root).call("get_changes", arguments: ["consumer": .string("x"), "kinds": .array([.string("log"), .string("podcast")])])
        #expect(bad.isError)
        let meetings = tools(root).call("get_changes", arguments: ["consumer": .string("x"), "kinds": .array([.string("meeting")])])
        #expect(meetings.text.contains("count: 1"))
        #expect(meetings.text.contains("kind: meeting"))
    }

    @Test func ackRules() async throws {
        let root = try await library()
        defer { TempDir.remove(root) }
        #expect(tools(root).call("ack", arguments: ["consumer": .string("crystalline"), "upTo": .int(4)]).isError)
        #expect(tools(root).call("ack", arguments: ["consumer": .string("Crystalline"), "upTo": .int(1)]).text.contains("not a valid consumer name"))
        _ = tools(root).call("ack", arguments: ["consumer": .string("crystalline"), "upTo": .int(3)])
        let older = tools(root).call("ack", arguments: ["consumer": .string("crystalline"), "upTo": .int(1)])
        #expect(!older.isError)
        #expect(older.text == "Bookmark crystalline stays at 3. upTo 1 is not newer.")
    }

    @Test func getItemReturnsMarkdownThenPaths() async throws {
        let root = try await library()
        defer { TempDir.remove(root) }
        let key = try #require(ChangeLogFile.readEvents(root: root).first { $0.kind == .meeting }?.key)
        let output = tools(root).call("get_item", arguments: ["key": .string(key)])
        #expect(!output.isError)
        #expect(output.text.hasPrefix("---\ntype: source"))
        #expect(output.text.contains("\n\n==== roger paths ====\npaths[3]: /"))
    }

    @Test(arguments: ["../x", "/etc/passwd", "Logs/../../etc/passwd", "~/secret"])
    func getItemRejectsPaths(key: String) async throws {
        let root = try await library()
        defer { TempDir.remove(root) }
        let output = tools(root).call("get_item", arguments: ["key": .string(key)])
        #expect(output.isError)
        #expect(output.text.contains("not a valid key"))
    }

    @Test func listItemsHonorsKindSinceAndLimit() async throws {
        let root = try await library()
        defer { TempDir.remove(root) }
        let all = tools(root).call("list_items", arguments: [:]).text
        #expect(all.hasPrefix("items[3]{key,kind,title,date,duration,paths}:\n  Meetings/"))
        #expect(tools(root).call("list_items", arguments: ["limit": .int(1)]).text.hasPrefix("items[1]"))
        #expect(tools(root).call("list_items", arguments: ["kind": .string("log")]).text.hasPrefix("items[2]"))
        #expect(!tools(root).call("list_items", arguments: ["limit": .int(9_999)]).isError)
        #expect(tools(root).call("list_items", arguments: ["limit": .int(0)]).isError)
        #expect(tools(root).call("list_items", arguments: ["since": .int(3)]).isError)
        let since = RFC3339.string(KitFixtures.base.addingTimeInterval(90))
        #expect(tools(root).call("list_items", arguments: ["since": .string(since)]).text.hasPrefix("items[1]"))
        #expect(all.contains(" | "))

    @Test func searchNeedsAQuery() async throws {
        let root = try await library()
        defer { TempDir.remove(root) }
        #expect(tools(root).call("search", arguments: [:]).isError)
        let found = tools(root).call("search", arguments: ["query": .string("supplier")]).text
        #expect(found.hasPrefix("results[2]{key,kind,title,date,snippet}:"))
        #expect(tools(root).call("search", arguments: ["query": .string("zebra")]).text == "results: []")
    }

    @Test func unknownToolIsAnError() throws {
        let root = try TempDir.make()
        defer { TempDir.remove(root) }
        #expect(tools(root).call("delete_everything", arguments: [:]) == .error("Unknown tool \"delete_everything\"."))
    }

    @Test func followsALibraryRootChangeWithoutRestart() async throws {
        let a = try await library()
        let b = try await library()
        defer {
            TempDir.remove(a)
            TempDir.remove(b)
        }
        let box = PreferencesBox(RogerPreferences(libraryRoot: a.path, extraScanFolders: []))
        let tools = RogerTools(
            locator: LibraryLocator(argument: nil, preferences: { box.get() }),
            registry: ExternalTranscriptRegistry(fileURL: a.appendingPathComponent("none.json"))
        )
        #expect(tools.call("status", arguments: [:]).text.hasPrefix("library: \(a.path)\n"))
        _ = tools.call("ack", arguments: ["consumer": .string("crystalline"), "upTo": .int(3)])
        box.set(RogerPreferences(libraryRoot: b.path, extraScanFolders: []))
        #expect(tools.call("status", arguments: [:]).text.hasPrefix("library: \(b.path)\n"))
        // The bookmark belongs to library A; in B the consumer starts over.
        #expect(tools.call("get_changes", arguments: ["consumer": .string("crystalline"), "summary": .bool(true)]).text.contains("count: 3"))
    }

    @Test func deniedAccessExplainsFilesAndFolders() async throws {
        let root = try await library()
        let roger = ChangeLogFile.folder(root: root)
        defer {
            try? FileManager.default.setAttributes([.posixPermissions: 0o755], ofItemAtPath: roger.path)
            TempDir.remove(root)
        }
        try FileManager.default.setAttributes([.posixPermissions: 0o000], ofItemAtPath: roger.path)
        let status = tools(root).call("status", arguments: [:]).text
        #expect(status.contains("Files and Folders"))
        #expect(!status.contains("Start Roger once"))
        for name in ["get_changes", "ack", "get_item", "list_items", "search"] {
            let output = tools(root).call(name, arguments: ["consumer": .string("x"), "upTo": .int(1), "key": .string("Logs/x"), "query": .string("x")])
            #expect(output.isError)
            #expect(output.text.contains("Files and Folders"))
        }
    }

    @Test func onlyBookmarksAreWritten() async throws {
        let root = try await library()
        defer { TempDir.remove(root) }
        func snapshot() throws -> [String: Date] {
            var result: [String: Date] = [:]
            let keys: Set<URLResourceKey> = [.contentModificationDateKey, .isRegularFileKey]
            let enumerator = FileManager.default.enumerator(at: root, includingPropertiesForKeys: Array(keys))
            while let url = enumerator?.nextObject() as? URL {
                let values = try url.resourceValues(forKeys: keys)
                // Folders change when consumers/ appears; only files count.
                guard values.isRegularFile == true, !url.path.contains("/.roger/consumers/") else { continue }
                result[url.path] = values.contentModificationDate
            }
            return result
        }
        let before = try snapshot()
        let key = try #require(ChangeLogFile.readEvents(root: root).first?.key)
        for (name, arguments) in [
            ("status", [String: ToolValue]()),
            ("get_changes", ["consumer": .string("crystalline")]),
            ("ack", ["consumer": .string("crystalline"), "upTo": .int(3)]),
            ("get_item", ["key": .string(key)]),
            ("list_items", [:]),
            ("search", ["query": .string("supplier")]),
        ] {
            #expect(!tools(root).call(name, arguments: arguments).isError)
        }
        #expect(try snapshot() == before)
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `ToolValue`**

`Roger/RogerKit/ToolValue.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

/// A JSON value from a tool call, without depending on the MCP SDK.
public enum ToolValue: Equatable, Sendable {
    case string(String)
    case int(Int)
    case double(Double)
    case bool(Bool)
    case array([ToolValue])
    case object([String: ToolValue])
    case null
}

/// What a tool answers: text, and whether it is an error the agent should
/// read (MCP `isError`).
public struct ToolOutput: Equatable, Sendable {
    public let text: String
    public let isError: Bool

    public static func ok(_ text: String) -> ToolOutput { ToolOutput(text: text, isError: false) }
    public static func error(_ text: String) -> ToolOutput { ToolOutput(text: text, isError: true) }
}

/// A problem with the arguments or the library, answered as `isError`.
struct ToolFailure: Error {
    let message: String
    init(_ message: String) { self.message = message }
}

/// Typed access to the arguments. Agents send numbers as integers, whole
/// doubles or digit strings; all three are accepted.
struct ToolArguments {
    let values: [String: ToolValue]

    func has(_ name: String) -> Bool {
        guard let value = values[name] else { return false }
        return value != .null
    }

    func string(_ name: String) throws -> String? {
        switch values[name] {
        case nil, .null?: return nil
        case .string(let text)?: return text
        default: throw ToolFailure("\(name) must be a string.")
        }
    }

    func int(_ name: String) throws -> Int? {
        switch values[name] {
        case nil, .null?: return nil
        case .int(let number)?: return number
        case .double(let number)? where number.rounded() == number && abs(number) < 1e15: return Int(number)
        case .string(let text)? where Int(text) != nil: return Int(text)
        default: throw ToolFailure("\(name) must be a whole number.")
        }
    }

    func bool(_ name: String) throws -> Bool? {
        switch values[name] {
        case nil, .null?: return nil
        case .bool(let flag)?: return flag
        case .string("true")?: return true
        case .string("false")?: return false
        default: throw ToolFailure("\(name) must be true or false.")
        }
    }

    func strings(_ name: String) throws -> [String]? {
        switch values[name] {
        case nil, .null?: return nil
        case .string(let text)?: return [text]
        case .array(let items)?:
            return try items.map {
                guard case .string(let text) = $0 else { throw ToolFailure("\(name) must be a list of strings.") }
                return text
            }
        default: throw ToolFailure("\(name) must be a list of strings.")
        }
    }

    /// `since` as text: integers become their digits.
    func sinceText(_ name: String) throws -> String? {
        if case .string(let text)? = values[name] { return text }
        return try int(name).map(String.init)
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `LibraryLocator`**

`Roger/RogerKit/LibraryLocator.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

/// The two Roger settings `roger-mcp` reads.
public struct RogerPreferences: Equatable, Sendable {
    public var libraryRoot: String?
    public var extraScanFolders: [String]

    public init(libraryRoot: String?, extraScanFolders: [String]) {
        self.libraryRoot = libraryRoot
        self.extraScanFolders = extraScanFolders
    }
}

public enum LibraryAccess: Equatable, Sendable {
    case ready
    /// The folder or `.roger/changes.jsonl` is missing.
    case notSetUp
    /// macOS refused (TCC Files and Folders, or plain permissions).
    case notReadable
}

public struct LibraryLocation: Equatable, Sendable {
    public let root: URL
    public let extraFolders: [URL]
    /// False with `--library`: then Roger's extra folders and external
    /// transcripts belong to another library and are left out.
    public let usesRogerSettings: Bool
    public let access: LibraryAccess
}

/// Finds the library on every call: `--library`, then Roger's
/// `libraryRoot` setting, then `~/Documents/Roger/`. Reading the setting
/// each time means a folder change in Roger reaches a running server.
public struct LibraryLocator: Sendable {
    public static let bundleID = "com.jordiboehme.roger"

    private let argument: String?
    private let preferences: @Sendable () -> RogerPreferences
    private let defaultRoot: URL

    public init(
        argument: String?,
        preferences: @escaping @Sendable () -> RogerPreferences = LibraryLocator.readPreferences,
        defaultRoot: URL = LibraryLayout.defaultRoot
    ) {
        self.argument = argument
        self.preferences = preferences
        self.defaultRoot = defaultRoot
    }

    public func locate() -> LibraryLocation {
        if let argument, !argument.isEmpty {
            let root = URL(fileURLWithPath: (argument as NSString).expandingTildeInPath, isDirectory: true)
            return LibraryLocation(root: root, extraFolders: [], usesRogerSettings: false, access: Self.probe(root: root))
        }
        let settings = preferences()
        let root = settings.libraryRoot.flatMap { $0.isEmpty ? nil : URL(fileURLWithPath: $0, isDirectory: true) } ?? defaultRoot
        return LibraryLocation(
            root: root,
            extraFolders: settings.extraScanFolders.map { URL(fileURLWithPath: $0, isDirectory: true) },
            usesRogerSettings: true,
            access: Self.probe(root: root)
        )
    }

    /// Roger's settings, fresh from disk.
    public static func readPreferences() -> RogerPreferences {
        let app = bundleID as CFString
        CFPreferencesAppSynchronize(app)
        let root = CFPreferencesCopyAppValue("libraryRoot" as CFString, app) as? String
        let extras = CFPreferencesCopyAppValue("extraScanFolders" as CFString, app) as? [String] ?? []
        return RogerPreferences(libraryRoot: root, extraScanFolders: extras)
    }

    /// Opens `changes.jsonl` for reading: `ENOENT` (or anything else that
    /// is not a permission error) means not set up; `EPERM` and `EACCES`
    /// mean macOS or the file system refused.
    public static func probe(root: URL) -> LibraryAccess {
        let fd = open(ChangeLogFile.url(root: root).path, O_RDONLY | O_CLOEXEC)
        if fd >= 0 {
            close(fd)
            return .ready
        }
        return (errno == EPERM || errno == EACCES) ? .notReadable : .notSetUp
    }

    /// The text for a library that is not ready; nil when it is.
    public static func accessMessage(_ access: LibraryAccess, root: URL) -> String? {
        switch access {
        case .ready:
            return nil
        case .notSetUp:
            return "No Roger library at \(root.path) yet. Start Roger once to set up the library."
        case .notReadable:
            return "Roger cannot read the library at \(root.path). macOS blocks this folder for the app that started roger-mcp. Allow it in System Settings > Privacy & Security > Files and Folders (for example for Terminal or Claude), then try again."
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write `RogerTools`**

`Roger/RogerKit/RogerTools.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

/// The six tools. Every call finds the library again (see
/// `LibraryLocator`). Only `ack` writes, and only a bookmark file.
public struct RogerTools: Sendable {
    public static let names = ["status", "get_changes", "ack", "get_item", "list_items", "search"]

    private let locator: LibraryLocator
    private let registry: ExternalTranscriptRegistry
    private let now: @Sendable () -> Date

    public init(locator: LibraryLocator, registry: ExternalTranscriptRegistry, now: @escaping @Sendable () -> Date = { Date() }) {
        self.locator = locator
        self.registry = registry
        self.now = now
    }

    public func call(_ name: String, arguments: [String: ToolValue]) -> ToolOutput {
        let location = locator.locate()
        let args = ToolArguments(values: arguments)
        do {
            switch name {
            case "status":
                return .ok(status(location))
            case "get_changes":
                try requireReady(location)
                return .ok(try getChanges(args, location))
            case "ack":
                try requireReady(location)
                return .ok(try ack(args, location))
            case "get_item":
                try requireReady(location)
                return .ok(try getItem(args, location))
            case "list_items":
                try requireReady(location)
                return .ok(try listItems(args, location))
            case "search":
                try requireReady(location)
                return .ok(try search(args, location))
            default:
                return .error("Unknown tool \"\(name)\".")
            }
        } catch let failure as ToolFailure {
            return .error(failure.message)
        } catch {
            return .error(error.localizedDescription)
        }
    }

    // MARK: - Tools

    private func status(_ location: LibraryLocation) -> String {
        var lines = [TOON.field("library", .string(location.root.path))]
        if let message = LibraryLocator.accessMessage(location.access, root: location.root) {
            lines.append(TOON.field("state", .string(location.access == .notSetUp ? "not set up" : "not readable")))
            lines.append(TOON.field("message", .string(message)))
            return lines.joined(separator: "\n")
        }
        let events = (try? ChangeLogFile.readEvents(root: location.root)) ?? []
        let lastSeq = events.map(\.seq).max() ?? 0
        lines.append(TOON.field("state", .string("ready")))
        lines.append(TOON.field("lastSeq", .int(lastSeq)))
        lines.append(TOON.field("lastEventAt", events.last.map { .string(RFC3339.string($0.at)) } ?? .null))
        let items = currentItems(location)
        lines.append(TOON.object("items"))
        for kind in LibraryItemKind.allCases {
            lines.append(TOON.field(kind.rawValue, .int(items.filter { $0.kind == kind }.count), depth: 1))
        }
        let rows: [[TOONValue]] = ConsumerBookmarks(root: location.root).all().map { named in
            [
                .string(named.name),
                .int(named.bookmark.seq),
                .string(RFC3339.string(named.bookmark.updatedAt)),
                .string(Self.age(from: named.bookmark.updatedAt, to: now())),
                .int(events.filter { $0.seq > named.bookmark.seq }.count),
            ]
        }
        lines.append(TOON.table("consumers", fields: ["name", "seq", "updatedAt", "age", "open"], rows: rows))
        return lines.joined(separator: "\n")
    }

    private func getChanges(_ args: ToolArguments, _ location: LibraryLocation) throws -> String {
        let consumer = try args.string("consumer")
        let hasSince = args.has("since")
        let from = try args.int("from")
        let upTo = try args.int("upTo")
        if (from == nil) != (upTo == nil) {
            throw ToolFailure("Pass from and upTo together.")
        }
        let forms = [consumer != nil, hasSince, from != nil].filter { $0 }.count
        guard forms == 1 else {
            throw ToolFailure("Pass exactly one start: consumer, since or from with upTo.")
        }
        let kinds: Set<LibraryItemKind>? = try args.strings("kinds").map { names in
            Set(try names.map { name in
                guard let kind = LibraryItemKind(rawValue: name) else {
                    throw ToolFailure("kinds takes log, meeting and transcript.")
                }
                return kind
            })
        }
        let summary = try args.bool("summary") ?? false
        let events = try ChangeLogFile.readEvents(root: location.root)
        let lastSeq = events.map(\.seq).max() ?? 0

        let start: ChangeStart
        if let consumer {
            let bookmark = try ConsumerBookmarks(root: location.root).bookmark(consumer)
            start = .afterSeq(bookmark?.seq ?? 0)
        } else if hasSince {
            guard let raw = try args.sinceText("since"), let parsed = SinceParser.parse(raw, now: now()) else {
                throw ToolFailure("since takes a seq, a date (YYYY-MM-DD), an RFC 3339 time or a duration like 30m, 12h or 3d.")
            }
            start = parsed
        } else if let from, let upTo {
            guard from >= 1, from <= upTo, upTo <= lastSeq else {
                throw ToolFailure("from and upTo must satisfy 1 <= from <= upTo <= \(lastSeq).")
            }
            start = .range(from: from, upTo: upTo)
        } else {
            throw ToolFailure("Pass exactly one start: consumer, since or from with upTo.")
        }
        return ChangeQuery.run(events: events, start: start, kinds: kinds, summary: summary, root: location.root).render()
    }

    private func ack(_ args: ToolArguments, _ location: LibraryLocation) throws -> String {
        guard let consumer = try args.string("consumer"), let upTo = try args.int("upTo") else {
            throw ToolFailure("ack needs consumer and upTo.")
        }
        let lastSeq = try ChangeLogFile.readEvents(root: location.root).map(\.seq).max() ?? 0
        guard upTo >= 0 else { throw ToolFailure("upTo must be 0 or more.") }
        guard upTo <= lastSeq else {
            throw ToolFailure("upTo \(upTo) is after the last change (\(lastSeq)).")
        }
        let result = try ConsumerBookmarks(root: location.root).ack(consumer, upTo: upTo, now: now())
        if result.current == upTo, result.previous < upTo {
            return "Bookmark \(consumer) is now \(result.current) (was \(result.previous))."
        }
        return "Bookmark \(consumer) stays at \(result.current). upTo \(upTo) is not newer."
    }

    private func getItem(_ args: ToolArguments, _ location: LibraryLocation) throws -> String {
        guard let key = try args.string("key") else { throw ToolFailure("get_item needs a key.") }
        let events = try ChangeLogFile.readEvents(root: location.root)
        let subject = try ItemResolver.subject(forKey: key, root: location.root, events: events)
        guard let item = ItemResolver.item(path: subject.path, kind: subject.kind, root: location.root),
              var markdown = ItemResolver.markdown(of: item)
        else { throw ItemKeyError.notFound(key) }
        while markdown.hasSuffix("\n") { markdown.removeLast() }
        let paths = ItemResolver.paths(of: item).map { TOONValue.string($0) }
        return markdown + "\n\n==== roger paths ====\n" + TOON.inlineArray("paths", paths)
    }

    private func listItems(_ args: ToolArguments, _ location: LibraryLocation) throws -> String {
        let kind = try kindArgument(args)
        var since: Date?
        if let raw = try args.sinceText("since") {
            guard case .atOrAfter(let date)? = SinceParser.parse(raw, now: now()) else {
                throw ToolFailure("since takes a date (YYYY-MM-DD), an RFC 3339 time or a duration like 30m, 12h or 3d here.")
            }
            since = date
        }
        let limit = try limitArgument(args, default: 50, max: 500)
        let items = currentItems(location)
            .filter { kind == nil || $0.kind == kind }
            .filter { since == nil || $0.date >= since! }
            .sorted { $0.date > $1.date }
            .prefix(limit)
        let rows: [[TOONValue]] = items.compactMap { item in
            guard let subject = ChangeKey.subject(for: item, root: location.root) else { return nil }
            return [
                .string(subject.key),
                .string(item.kind.rawValue),
                .string(item.title),
                .string(RFC3339.string(item.date)),
                item.durationSeconds.map { .int($0) } ?? .null,
                .string(ItemResolver.paths(of: item).joined(separator: " | ")),
            ]
        }
        return TOON.table("items", fields: ["key", "kind", "title", "date", "duration", "paths"], rows: rows)
    }

    private func search(_ args: ToolArguments, _ location: LibraryLocation) throws -> String {
        guard let query = try args.string("query"), !query.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces).isEmpty else {
            throw ToolFailure("search needs a query.")
        }
        let kind = try kindArgument(args)
        let limit = try limitArgument(args, default: 20, max: 100)
        let rows: [[TOONValue]] = ItemSearch.search(query, in: currentItems(location), kind: kind, limit: limit).compactMap { hit in
            guard let subject = ChangeKey.subject(for: hit.item, root: location.root) else { return nil }
            return [
                .string(subject.key),
                .string(hit.item.kind.rawValue),
                .string(hit.item.title),
                .string(RFC3339.string(hit.item.date)),
                .string(hit.snippet),
            ]
        }
        return TOON.table("results", fields: ["key", "kind", "title", "date", "snippet"], rows: rows)
    }

    // MARK: - Helpers

    private func requireReady(_ location: LibraryLocation) throws {
        if let message = LibraryLocator.accessMessage(location.access, root: location.root) {
            throw ToolFailure(message)
        }
    }

    /// Every item with Markdown, from the library, the extra folders and
    /// (with Roger's settings) the external transcripts.
    private func currentItems(_ location: LibraryLocation) -> [LibraryItem] {
        LibraryScanner.scanAll(
            scope: LibraryScanScope(layout: LibraryLayout(root: location.root), extraFolders: location.extraFolders),
            externalFiles: location.usesRogerSettings ? registry.listedFiles() : []
        ).filter(\.hasMarkdown)
    }

    private func kindArgument(_ args: ToolArguments) throws -> LibraryItemKind? {
        guard let raw = try args.string("kind") else { return nil }
        guard let kind = LibraryItemKind(rawValue: raw) else {
            throw ToolFailure("kind takes log, meeting or transcript.")
        }
        return kind
    }

    private func limitArgument(_ args: ToolArguments, default fallback: Int, max maximum: Int) throws -> Int {
        guard let limit = try args.int("limit") else { return fallback }
        guard limit >= 1 else { throw ToolFailure("limit must be 1 or more.") }
        return min(limit, maximum)
    }

    /// `45m`, `3h`, `2d`.
    static func age(from date: Date, to now: Date) -> String {
        let minutes = max(0, Int(now.timeIntervalSince(date) / 60))
        if minutes < 60 { return "\(minutes)m" }
        if minutes < 48 * 60 { return "\(minutes / 60)h" }
        return "\(minutes / (24 * 60))d"
    }
}
```

`statusReportsCountsAndConsumers` expects `0m`: the test's clock and the ack use the same `now`.

`Roger/RogerTests/RogerMCPEndToEndTests.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation
import Testing
@testable import Roger

/// A running roger-mcp: newline-delimited JSON-RPC on stdin and stdout.
/// Lines on stdout that are not JSON-RPC 2.0 are kept in `badLines`.
final class MCPProcess: @unchecked Sendable {
    private let process = Process()
    private let input = Pipe()
    private let output = Pipe()
    private let lock = NSLock()
    private var buffer = Data()
    private var messages: [[String: Any]] = []
    private var bad: [String] = []

    init(binary: URL, arguments: [String]) throws {
        process.executableURL = binary
        process.arguments = arguments
        process.standardInput = input
        process.standardOutput = output
        process.standardError = FileHandle.nullDevice
        output.fileHandleForReading.readabilityHandler = { [weak self] handle in
            self?.consume(handle.availableData)
        }
        try process.run()
    }

    func send(_ json: String) {
        input.fileHandleForWriting.write(Data((json + "\n").utf8))
    }

    func closeInput() {
        try? input.fileHandleForWriting.close()
    }

    var badLines: [String] { lock.withLock { bad } }

    func response(id: Int) -> [String: Any]? {
        lock.withLock { messages.first { ($0["id"] as? Int) == id } }
    }

    /// Responses may arrive in any order; wait until all ids are there.
    func waitForResponses(ids: [Int], timeout: TimeInterval) async throws -> Bool {
        let deadline = Date().addingTimeInterval(timeout)
        while Date() < deadline {
            if ids.allSatisfy({ response(id: $0) != nil }) { return true }
            try await Task.sleep(for: .milliseconds(50))
        }
        return false
    }

    func waitForExit(timeout: TimeInterval) async throws -> Int32? {
        let deadline = Date().addingTimeInterval(timeout)
        while process.isRunning, Date() < deadline {
            try await Task.sleep(for: .milliseconds(50))
        }
        if process.isRunning {
            process.terminate()
            return nil
        }
        return process.terminationStatus
    }

    private func consume(_ data: Data) {
        lock.withLock {
            buffer.append(data)
            while let newline = buffer.firstIndex(of: 0x0A) {
                let line = Data(buffer[buffer.startIndex..<newline])
                buffer.removeSubrange(buffer.startIndex...newline)
                if let object = try? JSONSerialization.jsonObject(with: line) as? [String: Any],
                   object["jsonrpc"] as? String == "2.0" {
                    messages.append(object)
                } else {
                    bad.append(String(decoding: line, as: UTF8.self))
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

struct RogerMCPEndToEndTests {
    /// The helper embedded in the Roger.app that hosts these tests.
    private var binary: URL {
        Bundle.main.bundleURL.appendingPathComponent("Contents/MacOS/roger-mcp")
    }

    @Test func helperIsEmbeddedAndPrintsTheAppVersion() throws {
        #expect(FileManager.default.isExecutableFile(atPath: binary.path))
        let process = Process()
        process.executableURL = binary
        process.arguments = ["--version"]
        let out = Pipe()
        process.standardOutput = out
        try process.run()
        process.waitUntilExit()
        let text = String(decoding: out.fileHandleForReading.readDataToEndOfFile(), as: UTF8.self)
        let version = try #require(Bundle.main.infoDictionary?["CFBundleShortVersionString"] as? String)
        #expect(text == "roger-mcp \(version)\n")
        #expect(process.terminationStatus == 0)
    }

    @Test func servesInitializeToolsListAndGetChanges() async throws {
        let root = try TempFolder.make()
        defer { TempFolder.remove(root) }
        let date = Date(timeIntervalSince1970: 1_790_000_000)
        let entry = LogEntry(text: "Called the supplier", startedAt: date, endedAt: date.addingTimeInterval(5), presetName: "Log", language: nil)
        let md = try await LogEntryWriter.write(entry, layout: LibraryLayout(root: root), audioSamples: nil)
        try await ChangeLog(root: root).record(.written, ChangeKey.subject(markdownURL: md, kind: .log, root: root))

        let server = try MCPProcess(binary: binary, arguments: ["--library", root.path])
        server.send(#"{"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":1,"method":"initialize","params":{"protocolVersion":"2025-06-18","capabilities":{},"clientInfo":{"name":"roger-tests","version":"1"}}}"#)
        #expect(try await server.waitForResponses(ids: [1], timeout: 15))

        server.closeInput()
        #expect(try await server.waitForExit(timeout: 10) == 0)
        #expect(server.badLines.isEmpty)
```
