- [ ] **Step 3: Write `RogerMCPOptions`**

`Roger/RogerKit/RogerMCPOptions.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

public struct RogerMCPOptionsError: Error, Equatable {
    public let message: String
}

/// `roger-mcp [--library <folder>] [--verbose]`, `--version`, `--help`.
public struct RogerMCPOptions: Equatable, Sendable {
    public enum Mode: Equatable, Sendable {
        case serve
        case version
        case help
    }

    public var mode: Mode
    public var library: String?
    public var verbose: Bool

    public init(mode: Mode = .serve, library: String? = nil, verbose: Bool = false) {
        self.mode = mode
        self.library = library
        self.verbose = verbose
    }

    public static let usage = """
    Usage: roger-mcp [--library <folder>] [--verbose]
           roger-mcp --version
    Serves Roger's library to MCP clients over stdin and stdout.
    Without --library it uses the library folder set in Roger.
    """

    public static func parse(_ arguments: [String]) throws -> RogerMCPOptions {
        var options = RogerMCPOptions()
        var rest = arguments[...]
        while let argument = rest.popFirst() {
            switch argument {
            case "--library":
                guard let folder = rest.popFirst(), !folder.hasPrefix("--") else {
                    throw RogerMCPOptionsError(message: "--library needs a folder.")
                }
                options.library = folder
            case "--verbose":
                options.verbose = true
            case "--version":
                options.mode = .version
            case "--help", "-h":
                options.mode = .help
            default:
                throw RogerMCPOptionsError(message: "Unknown argument: \(argument)")
            }
        }
        return options
    }
}

public enum RogerVersion {
    /// `CFBundleShortVersionString` of the app whose `Contents/MacOS/` holds
    /// `executable` (symlinks resolved, so a Homebrew link works). Nil when
    /// the binary is not inside an app.
    public static func enclosingApp(executable: URL) -> String? {
        let resolved = executable.resolvingSymlinksInPath()
        let macOS = resolved.deletingLastPathComponent()
        let contents = macOS.deletingLastPathComponent()
        guard macOS.lastPathComponent == "MacOS", contents.lastPathComponent == "Contents",
              let data = try? Data(contentsOf: contents.appendingPathComponent("Info.plist")),
              let plist = try? PropertyListSerialization.propertyList(from: data, format: nil) as? [String: Any]
        else { return nil }
        return plist["CFBundleShortVersionString"] as? String
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Add the package and the target to `Roger/project.yml`**

Under `packages:` add:

```yaml
  MCP:
    url: https://github.com/modelcontextprotocol/swift-sdk.git
    exactVersion: "0.12.1"
```

Under `targets:` add:

```yaml
  roger-mcp:
    type: tool
    platform: macOS
    sources:
      - path: RogerMCP
    dependencies:
      - target: RogerKit
      - package: MCP
        product: MCP
    settings:
      base:
        PRODUCT_NAME: roger-mcp
        PRODUCT_BUNDLE_IDENTIFIER: com.jordiboehme.roger.mcp
        SWIFT_VERSION: "6.0"
        MACOSX_DEPLOYMENT_TARGET: "15.0"
        ENABLE_HARDENED_RUNTIME: YES
        SKIP_INSTALL: YES
        CODE_SIGN_STYLE: Automatic
        DEVELOPMENT_TEAM: 77PA95K95V
```

In the `Roger` target's `dependencies`, after `- target: RogerKit`, add:

```yaml
      - target: roger-mcp
        embed: true
        codeSign: true
        copy:
          destination: executables
```

`destination: executables` is the Copy Files phase for `Contents/MacOS`; `codeSign: true` sets "Code Sign On Copy", so the copy is signed again with the app's identity. `SKIP_INSTALL: YES` keeps the tool out of the archive's `Products/usr/local/bin`, so `xcodebuild archive` still produces an app archive. (Checked with a probe project on Xcode 27.0 and XcodeGen 2.46.0: the embedded tool lands in `Contents/MacOS` and builds against swift-sdk 0.12.1.)

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the helper**

`Roger/RogerMCP/ValueBridge.swift`:

```swift
import MCP
import RogerKit

/// MCP `Value` to RogerKit's `ToolValue`, so RogerKit never imports MCP.
enum ValueBridge {
    static func arguments(_ values: [String: Value]?) -> [String: ToolValue] {
        (values ?? [:]).mapValues(toolValue)
    }

    static func toolValue(_ value: Value) -> ToolValue {
        switch value {
        case .null: return .null
        case .bool(let flag): return .bool(flag)
        case .int(let number): return .int(number)
        case .double(let number): return .double(number)
        case .string(let text): return .string(text)
        case .data(_, let data): return .string(data.base64EncodedString())
        case .array(let items): return .array(items.map(toolValue))
        case .object(let fields): return .object(fields.mapValues(toolValue))
        }
    }
}
```

`Roger/RogerMCP/ToolCatalog.swift`:

```swift
import MCP

/// Tool definitions and the server instructions. Text agents read: plain
/// sentences, no Oxford comma, no long dashes.
enum ToolCatalog {
    static let instructions = """
    Roger is a speech-to-text app on this Mac. Its library holds log entries, meeting transcripts and file transcripts as Markdown files.
    To ingest what is new, call get_changes with your consumer name, ingest each entry, then call ack with that consumer and the upTo from the answer. Repeat while more is true.
    Entries come one per item with its latest state. On updated read the item again as a whole. On deleted remove what you made from it.
    Answers are text. Lists are TOON: a header like items[3]{key,kind,title}: and then one comma-separated row per record. Values with commas, colons or quotes are in double quotes.
    A get_changes answer starts with from, upTo, more and count. Each entry then starts with the line ==== roger entry <seq> ====, lists its fields and has its Markdown after a blank line.
    Items are named by keys. No tool takes a file path and no tool deletes anything.
    """

    private static let readOnly = Tool.Annotations(readOnlyHint: true, destructiveHint: false, idempotentHint: true, openWorldHint: false)

    private static func schema(_ properties: [String: Value], required: [String] = []) -> Value {
        var object: [String: Value] = [
            "type": .string("object"),
            "properties": .object(properties),
            "additionalProperties": .bool(false),
        ]
        if !required.isEmpty { object["required"] = .array(required.map { .string($0) }) }
        return .object(object)
    }

    private static func property(_ type: String, _ description: String) -> Value {
        .object(["type": .string(type), "description": .string(description)])
    }

    private static let kindValues: Value = .array([.string("log"), .string("meeting"), .string("transcript")])

    static let tools: [Tool] = [
        Tool(
            name: "status",
            title: "Library status",
            description: "Shows the library folder, item counts per kind, the last change seq and every consumer with its bookmark, age and number of open changes.",
            inputSchema: schema([:]),
            annotations: readOnly
        ),
        Tool(
            name: "get_changes",
            title: "Get changes",
            description: "Returns what changed in the library, one entry per item with its latest state. Pass exactly one start: consumer (after that consumer's bookmark, unknown names start at 0), since (a seq, a date YYYY-MM-DD, an RFC 3339 time or a duration like 30m, 12h or 3d) or from with upTo (a fixed range). Ingest loop: call get_changes with your consumer, ingest every entry, then call ack with the same consumer and the answer's upTo. Repeat while more is true. With since, pass the previous upTo as since. On updated read the entry again as a whole. On deleted remove what you made from it. summary: true leaves out the Markdown, so you can plan first and then fetch groups with from and upTo (optionally with kinds). Only ack the upTo of a call without kinds.",
            inputSchema: schema([
                "consumer": property("string", "Your consumer name: a-z, 0-9 and -, at most 64 characters."),
                "since": .object([
                    "type": .array([.string("integer"), .string("string")]),
                    "description": .string("A seq, a date YYYY-MM-DD, an RFC 3339 time or a duration like 30m, 12h or 3d."),
                ]),
                "from": .object(["type": .string("integer"), "minimum": .int(1)]),
                "upTo": .object(["type": .string("integer"), "minimum": .int(1)]),
                "kinds": .object(["type": .string("array"), "items": .object(["type": .string("string"), "enum": kindValues])]),
                "summary": property("boolean", "Leave out the Markdown. Default false."),
            ]),
            annotations: readOnly
        ),
        Tool(
            name: "ack",
            title: "Confirm ingested changes",
            description: "Moves a consumer's bookmark to upTo after you ingested everything up to it. Never moves it backwards: an older upTo leaves the bookmark as it is. upTo must not be after the last change.",
            inputSchema: schema([
                "consumer": property("string", "Your consumer name."),
                "upTo": .object(["type": .string("integer"), "minimum": .int(0)]),
            ], required: ["consumer", "upTo"]),
            annotations: Tool.Annotations(readOnlyHint: false, destructiveHint: false, idempotentHint: true, openWorldHint: false)
        ),
        Tool(
            name: "get_item",
            title: "Get item",
            description: "Returns one item by its key: the whole Markdown file, then its absolute file paths (Markdown, audio and meeting screenshots). Keys come from get_changes, list_items or search.",
            inputSchema: schema(["key": property("string", "An item key such as Logs/2026/10/2026-10-13 14-32-05.")], required: ["key"]),
            annotations: readOnly
        ),
        Tool(
            name: "list_items",
            title: "List items",
            description: "Lists current items, newest first, with key, kind, title, date, duration in seconds and paths (several absolute paths separated by \" | \"). Optional kind, since (a date, an RFC 3339 time or a duration) and limit (default 50, at most 500).",
            inputSchema: schema([
                "kind": .object(["type": .string("string"), "enum": kindValues]),
                "since": property("string", "A date YYYY-MM-DD, an RFC 3339 time or a duration like 3d."),
                "limit": .object(["type": .string("integer"), "minimum": .int(1), "maximum": .int(500)]),
            ]),
            annotations: readOnly
        ),
        Tool(
            name: "search",
            title: "Search",
            description: "Finds items whose title or text contains every word of the query, case-insensitive, newest first. Optional kind and limit (default 20, at most 100). Returns key, kind, title, date and a snippet.",
            inputSchema: schema([
                "query": property("string", "Words that must all appear."),
                "kind": .object(["type": .string("string"), "enum": kindValues]),
                "limit": .object(["type": .string("integer"), "minimum": .int(1), "maximum": .int(100)]),
            ], required: ["query"]),
            annotations: readOnly
        ),
    ]
}
```

`Roger/RogerMCP/RogerMCP.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation
import MCP
import os
import RogerKit

private let logger = Logger(subsystem: "com.jordiboehme.roger", category: "roger-mcp")

/// Notes go to the unified log, and to stderr with `--verbose`. Never to
/// stdout: stdout carries MCP only.
struct Diagnostics: Sendable {
    let verbose: Bool

    func note(_ message: String) {
        logger.info("\(message, privacy: .public)")
        if verbose {
            FileHandle.standardError.write(Data(("roger-mcp: " + message + "\n").utf8))
        }
    }
}

/// Counts running tool calls, so the process does not exit in the middle
/// of one when stdin closes.
actor InFlight {
    private var count = 0

    func begin() { count += 1 }
    func end() { count -= 1 }

    func waitUntilIdle(timeout: Duration) async {
        let clock = ContinuousClock()
        let deadline = clock.now + timeout
        while count > 0, clock.now < deadline {
            try? await Task.sleep(for: .milliseconds(20))
        }
    }
}

@main
struct RogerMCP {
    static func main() async {
        // A client that goes away must not kill us with SIGPIPE mid-write.
        signal(SIGPIPE, SIG_IGN)

        let options: RogerMCPOptions
        do {
            options = try RogerMCPOptions.parse(Array(CommandLine.arguments.dropFirst()))
        } catch {
            let message = (error as? RogerMCPOptionsError)?.message ?? error.localizedDescription
            FileHandle.standardError.write(Data((message + "\n\n" + RogerMCPOptions.usage + "\n").utf8))
            exit(64)
        }

        let version = Bundle.main.executableURL.flatMap(RogerVersion.enclosingApp(executable:)) ?? "dev"
        switch options.mode {
        case .version:
            FileHandle.standardOutput.write(Data("roger-mcp \(version)\n".utf8))
            exit(0)
        case .help:
            FileHandle.standardOutput.write(Data((RogerMCPOptions.usage + "\n").utf8))
            exit(0)
        case .serve:
            break
        }

        let diagnostics = Diagnostics(verbose: options.verbose)
        let tools = RogerTools(locator: LibraryLocator(argument: options.library), registry: .standard)
        let inFlight = InFlight()
        let server = Server(
            name: "roger",
            version: version,
            instructions: ToolCatalog.instructions,
            capabilities: .init(tools: .init(listChanged: false))
        )

        await server.withMethodHandler(ListTools.self) { _ in
            .init(tools: ToolCatalog.tools)
        }
        await server.withMethodHandler(CallTool.self) { params in
            await inFlight.begin()
            let name = params.name
            let arguments = ValueBridge.arguments(params.arguments)
            // File work runs off the server actor.
            let output = await Task.detached { tools.call(name, arguments: arguments) }.value
            await inFlight.end()
            diagnostics.note("\(name): \(output.isError ? "error" : "ok")")
            return .init(content: [.text(text: output.text, annotations: nil, _meta: nil)], isError: output.isError)
        }

        do {
            try await server.start(transport: StdioTransport())
            diagnostics.note("serving \(version)")
        } catch {
            diagnostics.note("could not start: \(error.localizedDescription)")
            exit(1)
        }
        // Returns when stdin closes.
        await server.waitUntilCompleted()
        await inFlight.waitUntilIdle(timeout: .seconds(2))
        // Let the last response leave the transport.
        try? await Task.sleep(for: .milliseconds(200))
        diagnostics.note("stdin closed, exiting")
        exit(0)
    }
}
```

`Roger/Services/Agents/AgentsStatus.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

struct AgentsConsumerRow: Equatable, Identifiable {
    let name: String
    let seq: Int
    let updatedAt: Date
    /// Events after the bookmark (not grouped by item).
    let openEvents: Int

    var id: String { name }
}

/// What the Agents tab shows about the change log.
struct AgentsLibraryStatus: Equatable {
    let libraryPath: String
    let isSetUp: Bool
    let eventCount: Int
    let lastEventAt: Date?
    let consumers: [AgentsConsumerRow]

    static func read(root: URL) -> AgentsLibraryStatus {
        guard let events = try? ChangeLogFile.readEvents(root: root) else {
            return AgentsLibraryStatus(libraryPath: root.path, isSetUp: false, eventCount: 0, lastEventAt: nil, consumers: [])
        }
        let consumers = ConsumerBookmarks(root: root).all().map { named in
            AgentsConsumerRow(
                name: named.name,
                seq: named.bookmark.seq,
                updatedAt: named.bookmark.updatedAt,
                openEvents: events.filter { $0.seq > named.bookmark.seq }.count
            )
        }
        return AgentsLibraryStatus(
            libraryPath: root.path,
            isSetUp: true,
            eventCount: events.count,
            lastEventAt: events.max { $0.seq < $1.seq }?.at,
            consumers: consumers
        )
    }
}
```

`Roger/Services/Agents/RogerMCPLocation.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

/// Which `roger-mcp` path Roger hands to agents: a Homebrew link when it
/// points into this very app, else the binary inside the running app.
/// Always absolute: agents started from the Dock may have a short `PATH`.
enum RogerMCPLocation {
    static let homebrewLinks = ["/opt/homebrew/bin/roger-mcp", "/usr/local/bin/roger-mcp"]

    enum Choice: Equatable {
        case usable(String)
        /// Why no path can be handed out (the app runs from a disk image or
        /// a translocated copy, which goes away).
        case unusable(String)
    }

    static func bundled(in appBundle: URL) -> URL {
        appBundle.appendingPathComponent("Contents/MacOS/roger-mcp")
    }

    static func choose(appBundle: URL, links: [String] = homebrewLinks) -> Choice {
        let path = appBundle.standardizedFileURL.path
        if path.contains("/AppTranslocation/") || path.hasPrefix("/Volumes/") {
            return .unusable("Roger runs from a temporary place. Move Roger to the Applications folder, open it from there and try again.")
        }
        let binary = bundled(in: appBundle)
        let target = binary.resolvingSymlinksInPath().path
        for link in links where FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: link) {
            if URL(fileURLWithPath: link).resolvingSymlinksInPath().path == target {
                return .usable(link)
            }
        }
        return .usable(binary.path)
    }
}
```

`Roger/Services/Agents/McpCommandFormatter.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

/// Commands and config snippets the Agents tab offers to copy.
enum McpCommandFormatter {
    /// As is when it only has safe characters, else in single quotes.
    static func shellQuoted(_ text: String) -> String {
        let safe = CharacterSet(charactersIn: "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ0123456789/._-+=:@")
        if !text.isEmpty, text.unicodeScalars.allSatisfy({ safe.contains($0) }) { return text }
        return "'" + text.replacingOccurrences(of: "'", with: "'\\''") + "'"
    }

    static func claudeAdd(executable: String) -> String {
        "claude mcp add --scope user roger -- " + shellQuoted(executable)
    }

    /// `{"mcpServers": {"roger": {"command": ..., "args": []}}}` for
    /// `.mcp.json`, Cursor, Copilot CLI, Kiro and other clients.
    static func jsonSnippet(executable: String) -> String {
        let object: [String: Any] = ["mcpServers": ["roger": ["command": executable, "args": [String]()]]]
        guard let data = try? JSONSerialization.data(withJSONObject: object, options: [.prettyPrinted, .sortedKeys, .withoutEscapingSlashes]),
              let text = String(data: data, encoding: .utf8)
        else { return "" }
        return text
    }
}
```

`jsonSnippetHasTheServerEntry` pins the exact `JSONSerialization` pretty-print output (two-space indent, ` : `, an empty array printed over two lines). If a future SDK changes that layout, update the expected string, not the code.

`Roger/UI/AgentsSettingsView.swift`:

```swift
import AppKit
import SwiftUI

/// Settings > Agents: what agents need to read Roger's library.
struct AgentsSettingsView: View {
    @Environment(AppCoordinator.self) private var coordinator
    @State private var status: AgentsLibraryStatus?
    @State private var consumerAction: ConsumerAction?
    @State private var actionError: String?

    enum ConsumerAction: Identifiable, Equatable {
        case startOver(String)
        case forget(String)

        var id: String {
            switch self {
            case .startOver(let name): return "start-over-" + name
            case .forget(let name): return "forget-" + name
            }
        }

        var name: String {
            switch self {
            case .startOver(let name), .forget(let name): return name
            }
        }
    }

    static let documentsHint = "The first time an agent reads the library, macOS may ask whether Terminal (or the app that runs the agent) may access your Documents folder. Allow it, or Roger's tools cannot read the library. You can change this later in System Settings > Privacy & Security > Files and Folders."

    private var root: URL { coordinator.appState.libraryLayout.root }

    private var executableChoice: RogerMCPLocation.Choice {
        RogerMCPLocation.choose(appBundle: Bundle.main.bundleURL)
    }

    var body: some View {
        ScrollView {
            VStack(spacing: 16) {
                libraryCard
                manualSetupCard
                consumersCard
                Text(Self.documentsHint)
                    .font(.caption)
                    .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
                    .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
            }
            .padding()
        }
        .task { reload() }
        .confirmationDialog(
            consumerAction.map(confirmationTitle) ?? "",
            isPresented: Binding(get: { consumerAction != nil }, set: { if !$0 { consumerAction = nil } }),
            presenting: consumerAction
        ) { action in
            Button(confirmationButton(action), role: .destructive) { perform(action) }
            Button("Cancel", role: .cancel) {}
        } message: { action in
            Text(confirmationMessage(action))
        }
        .alert("Could not change the consumer", isPresented: Binding(get: { actionError != nil }, set: { if !$0 { actionError = nil } })) {
            Button("OK") {}
        } message: {
            Text(actionError ?? "")
        }
    }

    // MARK: - Cards

    private var libraryCard: some View {
        settingsCard(icon: "books.vertical", title: "Library") {
            Text(root.path)
                .font(.system(size: 12, design: .monospaced))
                .textSelection(.enabled)
            if let status, status.isSetUp {
                if let last = status.lastEventAt {
                    Text("\(status.eventCount) changes recorded, the last one \(last.formatted(.relative(presentation: .named))).")
                        .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
                } else {
                    Text("No changes recorded yet.")
                        .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
                }
            } else {
                Text("The change list is not set up yet. Roger sets it up when it starts.")
                    .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
            }
        }
    }

    private var manualSetupCard: some View {
        settingsCard(icon: "wrench.and.screwdriver", title: "Manual setup") {
            DisclosureGroup("Commands to add Roger to an agent yourself") {
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 10) {
                    switch executableChoice {
                    case .usable(let executable):
                        Text("Claude Code:")
                        copyableBlock(McpCommandFormatter.claudeAdd(executable: executable))
                        Text("Other clients (.mcp.json, Cursor, Copilot CLI, Kiro):")
                        copyableBlock(McpCommandFormatter.jsonSnippet(executable: executable))
                    case .unusable(let reason):
                        Text(reason).foregroundStyle(.secondary)
                    }
                }
                .padding(.top, 6)
            }
        }
    }

    private var consumersCard: some View {
        settingsCard(icon: "person.2.badge.gearshape", title: "Consumers") {
            let consumers = status?.consumers ?? []
            if consumers.isEmpty {
                Text("No agent has read the library yet. Each agent keeps a bookmark here under its consumer name.")
                    .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
            }
            ForEach(consumers) { consumer in
                HStack {
                    VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 2) {
                        Text(consumer.name).font(.body.weight(.medium))
                        Text("At change \(consumer.seq), \(consumer.openEvents) open, \(consumer.updatedAt.formatted(.relative(presentation: .named)))")
                            .font(.caption)
                            .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
                    }
                    Spacer()
                    Button("Start Over") { consumerAction = .startOver(consumer.name) }
                    Button("Forget") { consumerAction = .forget(consumer.name) }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // MARK: - Actions

    private func reload() {
        let root = self.root
        Task {
            let fresh = await Task.detached { AgentsLibraryStatus.read(root: root) }.value
            status = fresh
        }
    }

    private func perform(_ action: ConsumerAction) {
        let bookmarks = ConsumerBookmarks(root: root)
        do {
            switch action {
            case .startOver(let name): try bookmarks.reset(name)
            case .forget(let name): try bookmarks.forget(name)
            }
        } catch {
            actionError = error.localizedDescription
        }
        reload()
    }

    private func confirmationTitle(_ action: ConsumerAction) -> String {
        switch action {
        case .startOver(let name): return "Start over for \(name)?"
        case .forget(let name): return "Forget \(name)?"
        }
    }

    private func confirmationButton(_ action: ConsumerAction) -> String {
        switch action {
        case .startOver: return "Start Over"
        case .forget: return "Forget"
        }
    }

    private func confirmationMessage(_ action: ConsumerAction) -> String {
        switch action {
        case .startOver:
            return "The next time \(action.name) asks for changes, it gets the whole library again."
        case .forget:
            return "Roger deletes the bookmark of \(action.name). If it asks again, it starts with the whole library."
        }
    }

    // MARK: - Helpers

    private func copyableBlock(_ text: String) -> some View {
        HStack(alignment: .top) {
            Text(text)
                .font(.system(size: 11, design: .monospaced))
                .textSelection(.enabled)
                .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
            Button("Copy") {
                NSPasteboard.general.clearContents()
                NSPasteboard.general.setString(text, forType: .string)
            }
        }
        .padding(8)
        .background(.quaternary.opacity(0.4))
        .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous))
    }

    private func settingsCard(icon: String, title: String, @ViewBuilder content: () -> some View) -> some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 12) {
            HStack(spacing: 8) {
                Image(systemName: icon)
                    .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
                    .frame(width: 20)
                Text(title)
                    .font(.headline)
            }
            content()
        }
        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
        .padding(14)
        .background(.quaternary.opacity(0.3))
        .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 10, style: .continuous))
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the skill**

`skills/ingest-roger/SKILL.md`:

````markdown
---
name: ingest-roger
description: Ingest new Roger log entries, meeting transcripts and file transcripts into Crystalline. Use when the user asks to ingest, import or sync Roger, or runs /ingest-roger <domain>.
---

# Ingest Roger

Roger is a speech-to-text app on this Mac. Its MCP server `roger` serves log entries, meeting transcripts and file transcripts. This skill brings new and changed items into Crystalline and then confirms them, so nothing is read twice and nothing is missed.

Your client may show the tools with a prefix (for example `mcp__roger__get_changes` or `mcp__crystalline__write_engram`). Use whatever name it gives them.

## Target domain

- The Crystalline domain is the first argument: `/ingest-roger <domain>`.
- Without an argument, ask the user which domain to use. Never guess a domain.
- When nobody can answer (an unattended run), stop with `RESULT: failed: no domain given`.

## Before you start

1. Call `roger` `status`. If the library is not ready, stop with `RESULT: failed: <the message from status>`.
2. Read the domain's conventions in Crystalline (its MANIFEST and `vocabulary`). Reuse its tags, folders and categories. Search before you write anything.

## The loop

The consumer name is always `crystalline`.

1. Call `get_changes(consumer: "crystalline", summary: true)`. Note `from`, `upTo`, `more` and the entries (key, event, kind, title, date).
2. Plan groups:
   - log entries of the same day and the same topic go together,
   - every meeting is its own group,
   - every file transcript is its own group.
3. For each group, call `get_changes(from: <from>, upTo: <upTo>, kinds: [<kind>])` with `from` and `upTo` from step 1 and read the entries of that group. If that answer has `more: true`, call again with `from` set to its `upTo + 1` and the same `upTo` until `more` is false. For meetings, open the screenshots through their paths when they show something that matters.
4. Ingest the group (see "What to write").
5. Only when every group of the range succeeded, call `ack(consumer: "crystalline", upTo: <upTo from step 1>)`. Never ack the `upTo` of a call with `kinds`.
6. If `more` in step 1 was true, start again at step 1. Do at most 5 rounds in one run.

## What to write

- **Log entries** become observations (`[decision]`, `[task]`, `[fact]`, `[idea]`). Entries of one day on one topic share one engram. Keep the speaker's words where they matter. Name people, dates and amounts.
- **A meeting** becomes one engram: who spoke (the speaker labels as they are), what was decided, action items with owner and due date when said, open questions and a short summary. Mention what the screenshots show where it helps.
- **A file transcript** becomes one source summary engram: what the recording is, the main points and anything worth keeping.

## Never twice

- Every engram you write lists `roger://<key>` in `sources` for each Roger item it covers.
- Before you write, search with `metadata_filters: {"sources": {"$contains_any": ["roger://<key>", ...]}}`. If an engram already covers the item, edit it instead of writing a new one.
- `updated`: read the whole item again and update the engram.
- `deleted`: when the engram covers only deleted items, set its status to `archived`. Otherwise remove that item's observations and its `roger://<key>` source.
- `missing: true`: skip the entry and mention it in your summary.

## Result

End with exactly one of these lines as the last line:

- `RESULT: ok` when nothing is left (`more` was false),
- `RESULT: ok more` when you stopped after 5 rounds and `more` was still true,
- `RESULT: failed: <reason>` when something went wrong. Do not ack the range in that case, so the next run repeats it.
````

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the script**

`scripts/ingest-roger.sh`:

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Runs the ingest-roger skill with Claude Code until Roger has nothing new.
#
# Usage: scripts/ingest-roger.sh <crystalline-domain> [max-runs]
#
# Environment:
#   CLAUDE_BIN              Claude Code binary (default: claude)
#   ROGER_MCP_SERVER        name of the Roger MCP server (default: roger)
#   CRYSTALLINE_MCP_SERVER  name of the Crystalline MCP server (default: crystalline)
#
# Only the tools of these two servers are allowed. Exits 0 when everything
# was ingested, 1 when a run failed (the next run repeats the same range).
# Installs nothing.
set -euo pipefail

domain="${1:-}"
runs="${2:-10}"
if [ -z "$domain" ]; then
  echo "Usage: $0 <crystalline-domain> [max-runs]" >&2
  exit 2
fi

claude_bin="${CLAUDE_BIN:-claude}"
allowed="mcp__${ROGER_MCP_SERVER:-roger},mcp__${CRYSTALLINE_MCP_SERVER:-crystalline}"

for ((run = 1; run <= runs; run++)); do
  if ! output=$("$claude_bin" --allowedTools "$allowed" -p "/ingest-roger $domain" 2>&1); then
    printf '%s\n' "$output"
    echo "ingest-roger: claude failed in run $run" >&2
    exit 1
  fi
  printf '%s\n' "$output"
  result=$(printf '%s\n' "$output" | grep -E '^RESULT: ' | tail -n 1 || true)
  case "$result" in
    "RESULT: ok")
      exit 0
      ;;
    "RESULT: ok more")
      continue
      ;;
    *)
      echo "ingest-roger: ${result:-no RESULT line} in run $run" >&2
      exit 1
      ;;
  esac
done

echo "ingest-roger: there is still more after $runs runs. Run it again later." >&2
exit 0
```


A folder reference keeps the structure, so the file lands at `Contents/Resources/skills/ingest-roger/SKILL.md`.

`Roger/Services/Agents/BundledSkill.swift`:

```swift
import CryptoKit
import Foundation

enum SkillHash {
    /// Lowercase hex SHA-256.
    static func sha256(_ data: Data) -> String {
        SHA256.hash(data: data).map { String(format: "%02x", $0) }.joined()
    }
}

/// The ingest skill shipped inside Roger.app. Its version is the app
/// version; its hash decides whether an installed copy changed.
struct BundledSkill: Equatable, Sendable {
    static let name = "ingest-roger"

    let content: Data
    let version: String

    init(content: Data, version: String) {
        self.content = content
        self.version = version
    }

    var sha256: String { SkillHash.sha256(content) }

    static func load(from bundle: Bundle = .main) -> BundledSkill? {
        guard let url = bundle.url(forResource: "SKILL", withExtension: "md", subdirectory: "skills/\(name)"),
              let data = try? Data(contentsOf: url)
        else { return nil }
        let version = bundle.infoDictionary?["CFBundleShortVersionString"] as? String ?? "0"
        return BundledSkill(content: data, version: version)
    }
}
```

In `README.md`, add this bullet to "Features" after the "Drop files to transcribe" bullet:

```markdown
- **Feeds your agents** - a small MCP server inside Roger tells Claude Code, Codex, Gemini CLI, Cursor, Copilot CLI, Kiro and Claude Desktop what is new in your library: log entries, meetings and transcripts. Your agent picks them up, files them into its knowledge base and confirms. Nothing is read twice, nothing is missed and Roger does not even have to run
```

Add this section under "How It Works", after "### Log entries":

````markdown
### Agents (MCP)

Roger ships a small MCP server, `roger-mcp`, inside the app. An agent starts it and reads your library through six tools: `status`, `get_changes`, `ack`, `get_item`, `list_items` and `search`. Roger keeps a list of every change in `.roger/changes.jsonl` inside the library folder. Each agent has a bookmark there, so it gets exactly what is new since its last visit and confirms with `ack`. The server only reads your files. The only thing it writes is that bookmark. Roger does not have to run for this.

The easy way: open Settings › Agents. Roger finds Claude Code, Codex, Gemini CLI, Cursor, Copilot CLI and Kiro on your Mac. Install puts the ingest skill in place and adds the server through the agent's own command line tool. Roger never edits another app's config file. Where that is not possible, the row shows the command or JSON to copy. For Claude Desktop, "Add to Claude Desktop" builds an extension and hands it over, and "Save skill for Claude Desktop" writes a zip you upload in Claude under Settings › Capabilities › Skills.

By hand, with Homebrew (the cask links `roger-mcp` into your `PATH`):

```bash
claude mcp add --scope user roger -- /opt/homebrew/bin/roger-mcp
codex mcp add roger -- /opt/homebrew/bin/roger-mcp
gemini mcp add --scope user roger /opt/homebrew/bin/roger-mcp
copilot mcp add roger -- /opt/homebrew/bin/roger-mcp
kiro-cli mcp add --name roger --command /opt/homebrew/bin/roger-mcp --scope global
```

Without Homebrew, use `/Applications/Roger.app/Contents/MacOS/roger-mcp`. For Cursor and other clients, add this to their MCP config (for example `~/.cursor/mcp.json`):

```json
{ "mcpServers": { "roger": { "command": "/opt/homebrew/bin/roger-mcp", "args": [] } } }
```

The first time an agent reads the library, macOS may ask whether Terminal (or the app that runs the agent) may access your Documents folder. Allow it. You can change it later in System Settings › Privacy & Security › Files and Folders.

The skill `skills/ingest-roger/SKILL.md` files everything into [Crystalline](https://github.com/jordiboehme/crystalline): `/ingest-roger <domain>`. Log entries become observations, a meeting becomes one engram with decisions and action items and a file transcript becomes a source summary. To run it on a schedule, use `scripts/ingest-roger.sh <domain>`. It runs Claude Code with only the Roger and Crystalline tools allowed and stops with exit code 1 when something fails, so the next run tries the same range again. Example LaunchAgent, saved as `~/Library/LaunchAgents/com.example.ingest-roger.plist` and loaded with `launchctl bootstrap gui/$(id -u) ~/Library/LaunchAgents/com.example.ingest-roger.plist`:

```xml
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<!DOCTYPE plist PUBLIC "-//Apple//DTD PLIST 1.0//EN" "http://www.apple.com/DTDs/PropertyList-1.0.dtd">
<plist version="1.0">
<dict>
  <key>Label</key>
  <string>com.example.ingest-roger</string>
  <key>ProgramArguments</key>
  <array>
    <string>/bin/bash</string>
    <string>/Users/you/git/roger/scripts/ingest-roger.sh</string>
    <string>work</string>
  </array>
  <key>EnvironmentVariables</key>
  <dict>
    <key>PATH</key>
    <string>/opt/homebrew/bin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/bin:/bin</string>
  </dict>
  <key>StartCalendarInterval</key>
  <dict>
    <key>Hour</key>
    <integer>18</integer>
    <key>Minute</key>
    <integer>30</integer>
  </dict>
  <key>StandardOutPath</key>
  <string>/tmp/ingest-roger.log</string>
  <key>StandardErrorPath</key>
  <string>/tmp/ingest-roger.log</string>
</dict>
</plist>
```
````

### Task 12: Harness detection, install plan, receipt and skill installer

Plain types that decide which harnesses exist, where the skill goes, what state each copy is in and how install, update and uninstall touch the disk. No UI and no CLI yet.

**Files:**
- Create: `Roger/Services/Agents/Harness.swift`
- Create: `Roger/Services/Agents/SkillInstaller.swift`
- Test: `Roger/RogerTests/SkillInstallerTests.swift`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `BundledSkill`, `SkillHash` (Task 11).
- Produces:
  - `enum HarnessID: String, CaseIterable, Codable, Sendable { case claudeCode, codex, gemini, cursor, copilot, kiro }`
  - `struct HarnessSpec: Equatable, Sendable { let id: HarnessID; let name: String; let homeFolder: String; let readsSkillFolders: [String]; let writesSkillFolder: String; let cli: String?; let configFiles: [String] }` (folders and files relative to the home folder)
  - `enum HarnessCatalog { static let all: [HarnessSpec]; static func spec(_ id: HarnessID) -> HarnessSpec; static var knownSkillFolders: [String]; static func detected(home: URL) -> [HarnessSpec] }`
  - `struct HarnessInstallPlan: Equatable, Sendable { let harness: HarnessSpec; let skillFolder: URL; let coveredBy: HarnessID? }`
  - `enum SkillInstallPlanner { static func plan(for harnesses: [HarnessSpec], home: URL) -> [HarnessInstallPlan] }`
  - `enum SkillRowState: Equatable, Sendable { case notInstalled, upToDate, outdated, changedByYou }`
  - `struct SkillReceipt: Codable, Equatable, Sendable { struct Entry: Codable, Equatable, Sendable { var appVersion: String; var sha256: String; var installedAt: Date }; var version: Int; var installs: [String: Entry] }`
  - `enum SkillInstallOutcome: Equatable, Sendable { case installed, updated(backup: URL?) }`, `enum SkillUninstallOutcome: Equatable, Sendable { case notInstalled, removed, needsConfirmation, movedToTrash }`, `enum SkillInstallError: Error, Equatable, LocalizedError { case unknownFolder(String), symbolicLink(String) }`
  - `struct SkillInstaller: Sendable { init(home: URL, receiptURL: URL, skill: BundledSkill, trash: @escaping @Sendable (URL) throws -> Void, now: @escaping @Sendable () -> Date = { Date() }); static func live(skill: BundledSkill) -> SkillInstaller; var knownFolders: [URL]; func skillFile(in folder: URL) -> URL; func state(of folder: URL) -> SkillRowState; func install(into folder: URL) throws -> SkillInstallOutcome; func uninstall(from folder: URL, confirmChanged: Bool) throws -> SkillUninstallOutcome; func anyOutdated() -> Bool; func loadReceipt() -> SkillReceipt }`

`Roger/RogerTests/SkillInstallerTests.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation
import Testing
@testable import Roger

/// Records what went to the "Trash" and moves it into a temp folder.
final class TrashSpy: @unchecked Sendable {
    private let lock = NSLock()
    private var moved: [URL] = []
    let folder: URL

    init(folder: URL) { self.folder = folder }

    var urls: [URL] { lock.withLock { moved } }

    func trash(_ url: URL) throws {
        try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: folder, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        try FileManager.default.moveItem(at: url, to: folder.appendingPathComponent(UUID().uuidString))
        lock.withLock { moved.append(url) }
    }
}

struct SkillInstallerTests {
    private let v1 = BundledSkill(content: Data("---\nname: ingest-roger\n---\nversion one\n".utf8), version: "0.25.0")
    private let v2 = BundledSkill(content: Data("---\nname: ingest-roger\n---\nversion two\n".utf8), version: "0.26.0")

    private func installer(_ home: URL, _ skill: BundledSkill, trash: TrashSpy? = nil) -> SkillInstaller {
        let spy = trash ?? TrashSpy(folder: home.appendingPathComponent("Trash"))
        return SkillInstaller(
            home: home,
            receiptURL: home.appendingPathComponent("Library/Application Support/Roger/skill-installs.json"),
            skill: skill,
            trash: { try spy.trash($0) },
            now: { Date(timeIntervalSince1970: 1_790_000_000) }
        )
    }

    private func makeHome(_ folders: [String]) throws -> URL {
        let home = try TempFolder.make()
        for folder in folders {
            try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: home.appendingPathComponent(folder), withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        }
        return home
    }

    private func claudeSkills(_ home: URL) -> URL { home.appendingPathComponent(".claude/skills", isDirectory: true) }

    @Test func detectsHarnessesByHomeFolder() throws {
        let home = try makeHome([".claude", ".gemini", ".kiro"])
        defer { TempFolder.remove(home) }
        try Data().write(to: home.appendingPathComponent(".codex"))
        #expect(HarnessCatalog.detected(home: home).map(\.id) == [.claudeCode, .gemini, .kiro])
    }

    @Test func sharedFoldersAreWrittenOnce() throws {
        let home = try makeHome([".claude", ".codex", ".gemini", ".cursor", ".copilot", ".kiro"])
        defer { TempFolder.remove(home) }
        let plan = SkillInstallPlanner.plan(for: HarnessCatalog.detected(home: home), home: home)
        let byID = Dictionary(uniqueKeysWithValues: plan.map { ($0.harness.id, $0) })
        #expect(byID[.claudeCode]?.coveredBy == nil)
        #expect(byID[.claudeCode]?.skillFolder.path == home.appendingPathComponent(".claude/skills").path)
        #expect(byID[.codex]?.coveredBy == nil)
        #expect(byID[.codex]?.skillFolder.path == home.appendingPathComponent(".agents/skills").path)
        #expect(byID[.gemini]?.coveredBy == .codex)
        #expect(byID[.copilot]?.coveredBy == .codex)
        #expect(byID[.cursor]?.coveredBy == .claudeCode)
        #expect(byID[.kiro]?.coveredBy == nil)
        #expect(byID[.kiro]?.skillFolder.path == home.appendingPathComponent(".kiro/skills").path)
    }

    @Test func cursorAloneWritesTheAgentsFolder() throws {
        let home = try makeHome([".cursor"])
        defer { TempFolder.remove(home) }
        let plan = SkillInstallPlanner.plan(for: HarnessCatalog.detected(home: home), home: home)
        #expect(plan.map(\.coveredBy) == [nil])
        #expect(plan.first?.skillFolder.path == home.appendingPathComponent(".agents/skills").path)
    }

    @Test func statesFollowContentAndReceipt() throws {
        let home = try makeHome([".claude"])
        defer { TempFolder.remove(home) }
        let folder = claudeSkills(home)
        #expect(installer(home, v1).state(of: folder) == .notInstalled)
        #expect(try installer(home, v1).install(into: folder) == .installed)
        #expect(installer(home, v1).state(of: folder) == .upToDate)
        #expect(installer(home, v2).state(of: folder) == .outdated)
        #expect(installer(home, v2).anyOutdated())
        #expect(try Data(contentsOf: installer(home, v1).skillFile(in: folder)) == v1.content)
    }

    @Test func changedByYouWinsOverOutdated() throws {
        let home = try makeHome([".claude"])
        defer { TempFolder.remove(home) }
        let folder = claudeSkills(home)
        try installer(home, v1).install(into: folder)
        try Data("my own notes\n".utf8).write(to: installer(home, v1).skillFile(in: folder))
        #expect(installer(home, v1).state(of: folder) == .changedByYou)
        #expect(installer(home, v2).state(of: folder) == .changedByYou)
    }

    @Test func aMatchingFileWithoutReceiptIsAdopted() throws {
        let home = try makeHome([".claude"])
        defer { TempFolder.remove(home) }
        let file = installer(home, v1).skillFile(in: claudeSkills(home))
        try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: file.deletingLastPathComponent(), withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        try v1.content.write(to: file)
        #expect(installer(home, v1).state(of: claudeSkills(home)) == .upToDate)
        try Data("other".utf8).write(to: file)
        #expect(installer(home, v1).state(of: claudeSkills(home)) == .changedByYou)
    }

    @Test func secondUpdateKeepsTheFirstBackup() throws {
        let home = try makeHome([".claude"])
        defer { TempFolder.remove(home) }
        let folder = claudeSkills(home)
        let file = installer(home, v1).skillFile(in: folder)
        try installer(home, v1).install(into: folder)
        try Data("first edit\n".utf8).write(to: file)
        #expect(try installer(home, v2).install(into: folder) == .updated(backup: file.deletingLastPathComponent().appendingPathComponent("SKILL.md.bak")))
        try Data("second edit\n".utf8).write(to: file)
        #expect(try installer(home, v2).install(into: folder) == .updated(backup: file.deletingLastPathComponent().appendingPathComponent("SKILL.md.bak 1")))
        let skillFolder = file.deletingLastPathComponent()
        #expect(try String(contentsOf: skillFolder.appendingPathComponent("SKILL.md.bak"), encoding: .utf8) == "first edit\n")
        #expect(try String(contentsOf: skillFolder.appendingPathComponent("SKILL.md.bak 1"), encoding: .utf8) == "second edit\n")
        #expect(try Data(contentsOf: file) == v2.content)
        #expect(installer(home, v2).state(of: folder) == .upToDate)
    }

    @Test func plainUpdateMakesNoBackup() throws {
        let home = try makeHome([".claude"])
        defer { TempFolder.remove(home) }
        let folder = claudeSkills(home)
        try installer(home, v1).install(into: folder)
        #expect(try installer(home, v2).install(into: folder) == .updated(backup: nil))
        let names = try FileManager.default.contentsOfDirectory(atPath: installer(home, v2).skillFile(in: folder).deletingLastPathComponent().path)
        #expect(names == ["SKILL.md"])
    }

    @Test func uninstallAsksForAChangedFileAndThenUsesTheTrash() throws {
        let home = try makeHome([".claude"])
        defer { TempFolder.remove(home) }
        let spy = TrashSpy(folder: home.appendingPathComponent("Trash"))
        let folder = claudeSkills(home)
        try installer(home, v1, trash: spy).install(into: folder)
        let file = installer(home, v1).skillFile(in: folder)
        try Data("mine\n".utf8).write(to: file)
        #expect(try installer(home, v1, trash: spy).uninstall(from: folder, confirmChanged: false) == .needsConfirmation)
        #expect(FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: file.path))
        #expect(try installer(home, v1, trash: spy).uninstall(from: folder, confirmChanged: true) == .movedToTrash)
        #expect(spy.urls.map(\.lastPathComponent) == ["SKILL.md"])
        #expect(!FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: file.deletingLastPathComponent().path))
    }

    @Test func uninstallKeepsOtherFilesTheUserAdded() throws {
        let home = try makeHome([".claude"])
        defer { TempFolder.remove(home) }
        let folder = claudeSkills(home)
        try installer(home, v1).install(into: folder)
        let skillFolder = installer(home, v1).skillFile(in: folder).deletingLastPathComponent()
        try Data("x".utf8).write(to: skillFolder.appendingPathComponent("notes.txt"))
        #expect(try installer(home, v1).uninstall(from: folder, confirmChanged: false) == .removed)
        #expect(try FileManager.default.contentsOfDirectory(atPath: skillFolder.path) == ["notes.txt"])
        #expect(installer(home, v1).loadReceipt().installs.isEmpty)
    }

    @Test func receiptEntriesOutsideKnownFoldersAreIgnored() throws {
        let home = try makeHome([".claude"])
        defer { TempFolder.remove(home) }
        let evil = home.appendingPathComponent("Documents/important", isDirectory: true)
        let evilFile = evil.appendingPathComponent("ingest-roger/SKILL.md")
        try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: evilFile.deletingLastPathComponent(), withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        try v1.content.write(to: evilFile)
        let receiptURL = home.appendingPathComponent("Library/Application Support/Roger/skill-installs.json")
        try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: receiptURL.deletingLastPathComponent(), withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        let receipt = SkillReceipt(version: 1, installs: [evil.path: .init(appVersion: "0.25.0", sha256: v1.sha256, installedAt: Date())])
        let encoder = JSONEncoder()
        encoder.dateEncodingStrategy = .iso8601
        try encoder.encode(receipt).write(to: receiptURL)

        #expect(installer(home, v1).loadReceipt().installs.isEmpty)
        #expect(throws: SkillInstallError.unknownFolder(evil.path)) {
            try installer(home, v1).uninstall(from: evil, confirmChanged: true)
        }
        #expect(throws: SkillInstallError.unknownFolder(evil.path)) {
            try installer(home, v1).install(into: evil)
        }
        try installer(home, v1).install(into: claudeSkills(home))
        let decoder = JSONDecoder()
        decoder.dateDecodingStrategy = .iso8601
        let saved = try decoder.decode(SkillReceipt.self, from: Data(contentsOf: receiptURL))
        #expect(Array(saved.installs.keys) == [claudeSkills(home).standardizedFileURL.path])
        #expect(FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: evilFile.path))

    @Test func aLinkedSkillFolderIsRefused() throws {
        let home = try makeHome([".claude/skills"])
        defer { TempFolder.remove(home) }
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the harness catalog and the planner**

`Roger/Services/Agents/Harness.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

enum HarnessID: String, CaseIterable, Codable, Sendable {
    case claudeCode
    case codex
    case gemini
    case cursor
    case copilot
    case kiro
}

/// One coding agent Roger knows. Paths are relative to the home folder.
/// Folders and CLIs were checked against each tool's docs on 2026-10-14
/// (see the plan's Verified facts).
struct HarnessSpec: Equatable, Sendable {
    let id: HarnessID
    let name: String
    /// Detected when this folder exists.
    let homeFolder: String
    /// Every user-level skills folder the harness reads.
    let readsSkillFolders: [String]
    /// The folder Roger writes for it when no other row covers it.
    let writesSkillFolder: String
    /// Executable that registers MCP servers, nil when there is none.
    let cli: String?
    /// Config files Roger reads (never writes) to show the server state.
    let configFiles: [String]
}

enum HarnessCatalog {
    /// In this order: the first harness that writes a folder owns it.
    static let all: [HarnessSpec] = [
        HarnessSpec(id: .claudeCode, name: "Claude Code", homeFolder: ".claude",
                    readsSkillFolders: [".claude/skills"], writesSkillFolder: ".claude/skills",
                    cli: "claude", configFiles: [".claude.json"]),
        HarnessSpec(id: .codex, name: "Codex", homeFolder: ".codex",
                    readsSkillFolders: [".agents/skills", ".codex/skills"], writesSkillFolder: ".agents/skills",
                    cli: "codex", configFiles: [".codex/config.toml"]),
        HarnessSpec(id: .gemini, name: "Gemini CLI", homeFolder: ".gemini",
                    readsSkillFolders: [".agents/skills", ".gemini/skills"], writesSkillFolder: ".agents/skills",
                    cli: "gemini", configFiles: [".gemini/settings.json"]),
        HarnessSpec(id: .cursor, name: "Cursor", homeFolder: ".cursor",
                    readsSkillFolders: [".agents/skills", ".cursor/skills", ".claude/skills", ".codex/skills"], writesSkillFolder: ".agents/skills",
                    cli: nil, configFiles: [".cursor/mcp.json"]),
        HarnessSpec(id: .copilot, name: "Copilot CLI", homeFolder: ".copilot",
                    readsSkillFolders: [".agents/skills", ".copilot/skills"], writesSkillFolder: ".agents/skills",
                    cli: "copilot", configFiles: [".copilot/mcp-config.json"]),
        HarnessSpec(id: .kiro, name: "Kiro", homeFolder: ".kiro",
                    readsSkillFolders: [".kiro/skills"], writesSkillFolder: ".kiro/skills",
                    cli: "kiro-cli", configFiles: [".kiro/settings/mcp.json", ".kiro/mcp.json"]),
    ]

    static func spec(_ id: HarnessID) -> HarnessSpec {
        all.first { $0.id == id }!
    }

    /// The only folders Roger ever writes `ingest-roger/` into.
    static var knownSkillFolders: [String] {
        Array(Set(all.map(\.writesSkillFolder))).sorted()
    }

    /// Harnesses whose home folder exists (a folder, not a file).
    static func detected(home: URL) -> [HarnessSpec] {
        all.filter { spec in
            var isDirectory: ObjCBool = false
            return FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: home.appendingPathComponent(spec.homeFolder).path, isDirectory: &isDirectory)
                && isDirectory.boolValue
        }
    }
}

struct HarnessInstallPlan: Equatable, Sendable {
    let harness: HarnessSpec
    /// The skills folder that holds (or will hold) `ingest-roger/`.
    let skillFolder: URL
    /// Another row writes a folder this harness reads; this row writes nothing.
    let coveredBy: HarnessID?
}

enum SkillInstallPlanner {
    /// In catalog order: a harness that reads a folder an earlier row writes
    /// is covered by that row; otherwise it writes its own folder.
    static func plan(for harnesses: [HarnessSpec], home: URL) -> [HarnessInstallPlan] {
        var written: [(folder: String, by: HarnessID)] = []
        var plans: [HarnessInstallPlan] = []
        for harness in harnesses {
            if let cover = written.first(where: { harness.readsSkillFolders.contains($0.folder) }) {
                plans.append(HarnessInstallPlan(harness: harness, skillFolder: home.appendingPathComponent(cover.folder, isDirectory: true), coveredBy: cover.by))
            } else {
                written.append((harness.writesSkillFolder, harness.id))
                plans.append(HarnessInstallPlan(harness: harness, skillFolder: home.appendingPathComponent(harness.writesSkillFolder, isDirectory: true), coveredBy: nil))
            }
        }
        return plans
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the installer**

`Roger/Services/Agents/SkillInstaller.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

enum SkillRowState: Equatable, Sendable {
    case notInstalled
    case upToDate
    case outdated
    case changedByYou
}

/// `~/Library/Application Support/Roger/skill-installs.json`. Untrusted:
/// only entries for the known skills folders are used or kept.
struct SkillReceipt: Codable, Equatable, Sendable {
    struct Entry: Codable, Equatable, Sendable {
        var appVersion: String
        var sha256: String
        var installedAt: Date
    }

    var version: Int = 1
    /// Key: standardized absolute path of the skills folder.
    var installs: [String: Entry] = [:]
}

enum SkillInstallOutcome: Equatable, Sendable {
    case installed
    /// `backup` is where a file you changed was saved first.
    case updated(backup: URL?)
}

enum SkillUninstallOutcome: Equatable, Sendable {
    case notInstalled
    case removed
    /// The file was changed by the user; ask, then call again with `confirmChanged`.
    case needsConfirmation
    case movedToTrash
}

enum SkillInstallError: Error, Equatable, LocalizedError {
    case unknownFolder(String)
    case symbolicLink(String)

    var errorDescription: String? {
        switch self {
        case .unknownFolder(let path):
            return "Roger only installs its skill in the skills folders of known agents, not in \(path)."
        case .symbolicLink(let path):
            return "\(path) is a link. Roger does not write through links. Remove the link and try again."
        }
    }
}

/// Copies (never links) `ingest-roger/SKILL.md` into a skills folder with a
/// temp file plus rename, remembers what it wrote in the receipt and never
/// destroys a file the user changed.
struct SkillInstaller: Sendable {
    static let fileName = "SKILL.md"

    let home: URL
    let receiptURL: URL
    let skill: BundledSkill
    private let trash: @Sendable (URL) throws -> Void
    private let now: @Sendable () -> Date

    init(home: URL, receiptURL: URL, skill: BundledSkill, trash: @escaping @Sendable (URL) throws -> Void, now: @escaping @Sendable () -> Date = { Date() }) {
        self.home = home
        self.receiptURL = receiptURL
        self.skill = skill
        self.trash = trash
        self.now = now
    }

    /// The installer for this Mac's real home folder and receipt.
    static func live(skill: BundledSkill) -> SkillInstaller {
        let support = FileManager.default.urls(for: .applicationSupportDirectory, in: .userDomainMask).first
            ?? URL(fileURLWithPath: NSHomeDirectory()).appendingPathComponent("Library/Application Support")
        return SkillInstaller(
            home: FileManager.default.homeDirectoryForCurrentUser,
            receiptURL: support.appendingPathComponent("Roger/skill-installs.json"),
            skill: skill,
            trash: { try FileManager.default.trashItem(at: $0, resultingItemURL: nil) }
        )
    }

    var knownFolders: [URL] {
        HarnessCatalog.knownSkillFolders.map { home.appendingPathComponent($0, isDirectory: true).standardizedFileURL }
    }

    func skillFile(in folder: URL) -> URL {
        folder.appendingPathComponent(BundledSkill.name, isDirectory: true).appendingPathComponent(Self.fileName)
    }

    func state(of folder: URL) -> SkillRowState {
        guard isKnown(folder), let data = regularFileData(skillFile(in: folder)) else { return .notInstalled }
        let hash = SkillHash.sha256(data)
        if let entry = loadReceipt().installs[key(folder)] {
            if hash != entry.sha256 { return .changedByYou }
            return hash == skill.sha256 ? .upToDate : .outdated
        }
        return hash == skill.sha256 ? .upToDate : .changedByYou
    }

    func anyOutdated() -> Bool {
        knownFolders.contains { state(of: $0) == .outdated }
    }

    @discardableResult
    func install(into folder: URL) throws -> SkillInstallOutcome {
        try requireKnown(folder)
        let skillFolder = skillFile(in: folder).deletingLastPathComponent()
        try refuseLink(skillFolder)
        let file = skillFile(in: folder)
        try refuseLink(file)
        try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: skillFolder, withIntermediateDirectories: true)

        let existed = FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: file.path)
        var backup: URL?
        if existed, state(of: folder) == .changedByYou {
            let target = freeBackupURL(in: skillFolder)
            try FileManager.default.moveItem(at: file, to: target)
            backup = target
        }
        let temp = skillFolder.appendingPathComponent(".\(Self.fileName).\(UUID().uuidString).tmp")
        try skill.content.write(to: temp)
        guard rename(temp.path, file.path) == 0 else {
            let error = POSIXError(POSIXErrorCode(rawValue: errno) ?? .EIO)
            try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: temp)
            throw error
        }
        var receipt = loadReceipt()
        receipt.installs[key(folder)] = .init(appVersion: skill.version, sha256: skill.sha256, installedAt: now())
        try save(receipt)
        return existed ? .updated(backup: backup) : .installed
    }

    func uninstall(from folder: URL, confirmChanged: Bool) throws -> SkillUninstallOutcome {
        try requireKnown(folder)
        let skillFolder = skillFile(in: folder).deletingLastPathComponent()
        try refuseLink(skillFolder)
        let file = skillFile(in: folder)
        try refuseLink(file)
        var receipt = loadReceipt()
        guard FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: file.path) else {
            receipt.installs[key(folder)] = nil
            try save(receipt)
            return .notInstalled
        }
        let outcome: SkillUninstallOutcome
        if state(of: folder) == .changedByYou {
            guard confirmChanged else { return .needsConfirmation }
            try trash(file)
            outcome = .movedToTrash
        } else {
            try FileManager.default.removeItem(at: file)
            outcome = .removed
        }
        if let rest = try? FileManager.default.contentsOfDirectory(atPath: skillFolder.path), rest.isEmpty {
            try FileManager.default.removeItem(at: skillFolder)
        }
        receipt.installs[key(folder)] = nil
        try save(receipt)
        return outcome
    }

    /// The receipt with every entry outside the known folders dropped.
    /// Missing or broken: empty.
    func loadReceipt() -> SkillReceipt {
        let decoder = JSONDecoder()
        decoder.dateDecodingStrategy = .iso8601
        guard let data = try? Data(contentsOf: receiptURL),
              var receipt = try? decoder.decode(SkillReceipt.self, from: data)
        else { return SkillReceipt() }
        let allowed = Set(knownFolders.map(\.path))
        receipt.installs = receipt.installs.filter { allowed.contains($0.key) }
        return receipt
    }

    // MARK: - Helpers

    private func key(_ folder: URL) -> String {
        folder.standardizedFileURL.path
    }

    private func isKnown(_ folder: URL) -> Bool {
        knownFolders.map(\.path).contains(key(folder))
    }

    private func requireKnown(_ folder: URL) throws {
        guard isKnown(folder) else { throw SkillInstallError.unknownFolder(folder.path) }
    }

    private func refuseLink(_ url: URL) throws {
        let type = (try? FileManager.default.attributesOfItem(atPath: url.path))?[.type] as? FileAttributeType
        if type == .typeSymbolicLink { throw SkillInstallError.symbolicLink(url.path) }
    }

    private func regularFileData(_ url: URL) -> Data? {
        let type = (try? FileManager.default.attributesOfItem(atPath: url.path))?[.type] as? FileAttributeType
        guard type == .typeRegular else { return nil }
        return try? Data(contentsOf: url)
    }

    /// `SKILL.md.bak`, then `SKILL.md.bak 1`, `SKILL.md.bak 2` … Never an existing name.
    private func freeBackupURL(in folder: URL) -> URL {
        let base = Self.fileName + ".bak"
        var candidate = folder.appendingPathComponent(base)
        var counter = 1
        while FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: candidate.path) {
            candidate = folder.appendingPathComponent("\(base) \(counter)")
            counter += 1
        }
        return candidate
    }

    private func save(_ receipt: SkillReceipt) throws {
        try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: receiptURL.deletingLastPathComponent(), withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        let encoder = JSONEncoder()
        encoder.outputFormatting = [.prettyPrinted, .sortedKeys]
        encoder.dateEncodingStrategy = .iso8601
        try encoder.encode(receipt).write(to: receiptURL, options: .atomic)
    }
}
```

Receipt dates are ISO 8601 on both sides (`save` and `loadReceipt`).

### Task 13: CLI runner, MCP registration, harness rows and the popover hint

Install in a harness row installs the skill and registers the server through the harness's own CLI, which may hang, prompt or be missing. The runner always comes back; the row then shows the command to copy.

**Files:**
- Create: `Roger/Services/Agents/CommandRunner.swift`
- Create: `Roger/Services/Agents/HarnessRegistration.swift`
- Create: `Roger/Services/Agents/AgentsController.swift`
- Modify: `Roger/UI/AgentsSettingsView.swift` (harness card)
- Modify: `Roger/App/AppState.swift` (`agentSkillHintDismissedVersion`)
- Modify: `Roger/App/AppCoordinator.swift` (`agentSkillOutdated`, `checkAgentSkills()`)
- Modify: `Roger/App/RogerApp.swift` (check at launch)
- Modify: `Roger/UI/MenuBarView.swift` (hint)
- Test: `Roger/RogerTests/CommandRunnerTests.swift`
- Test: `Roger/RogerTests/HarnessRegistrationTests.swift`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `HarnessSpec`, `HarnessCatalog`, `HarnessInstallPlan`, `SkillInstallPlanner`, `SkillInstaller`, `SkillRowState`, `SkillInstallOutcome`, `SkillUninstallOutcome` (Task 12); `BundledSkill` (Task 11); `RogerMCPLocation`, `McpCommandFormatter` (Task 10).
- Produces:
  - `struct CommandResult: Equatable, Sendable { enum Status: Equatable, Sendable { case exited(Int32), timedOut, failedToStart(String) }; let status: Status; let stdout: String; let stderr: String; var succeeded: Bool }`
  - `protocol CommandRunner: Sendable { func run(_ executable: URL, _ arguments: [String], environment: [String: String], currentDirectory: URL, timeout: Duration) async -> CommandResult }`, `struct ProcessCommandRunner: CommandRunner`
  - `enum CLILocator { static let extraDirectories: [String]; static func searchPath(loginShellPATH: String?) -> [String]; static func find(_ name: String, in directories: [String]) -> URL?; static func loginShellPATH(runner: any CommandRunner, home: URL) async -> String? }`
  - `struct RegistrationCommand: Equatable, Sendable { let cli: String; let arguments: [String]; var display: String }`
  - `enum ConfigLookup: Equatable, Sendable { case command(String), absent, unreadable }`, `enum ServerConnection: Equatable, Sendable { case connected, otherCommand(String), notConnected, unknown }`
  - `enum HarnessRegistration { static func add(_ id: HarnessID, executable: String) -> RegistrationCommand?; static func remove(_ id: HarnessID) -> RegistrationCommand?; static func snippet(_ spec: HarnessSpec, executable: String) -> String; static func connection(_ spec: HarnessSpec, home: URL, executable: String) -> ServerConnection; static func jsonCommand(_ data: Data) -> ConfigLookup; static func tomlCommand(_ text: String) -> ConfigLookup }`
  - `struct RegistrationOutcome: Equatable, Sendable { enum Kind: Equatable, Sendable { case registered, alreadyRegistered, removed, manual }; let kind: Kind; let message: String?; let command: String? }`
  - `struct HarnessRegistrar: Sendable { init(home: URL, runner: any CommandRunner, searchPath: [String], timeout: Duration = .seconds(20)); func register(_ spec: HarnessSpec, executable: String) async -> RegistrationOutcome; func unregister(_ spec: HarnessSpec, executable: String) async -> RegistrationOutcome }`
  - `enum AgentsHint { static func shouldShow(outdated: Bool, dismissedVersion: String?, appVersion: String) -> Bool; static var appVersion: String }`
  - `@MainActor @Observable final class AgentsController { struct Row: Identifiable, Equatable; private(set) var rows: [Row]; let executableChoice: RogerMCPLocation.Choice; init(home: URL, installer: SkillInstaller, runner: any CommandRunner, appBundle: URL, searchPath: [String]? = nil, links: [String] = RogerMCPLocation.homebrewLinks); static func live() -> AgentsController?; func refresh(); func install(_ id: HarnessID) async; func uninstall(_ id: HarnessID, confirmChanged: Bool) async -> Bool }`
  - `AppState.agentSkillHintDismissedVersion: String?`, `AppCoordinator.agentSkillOutdated: Bool`, `AppCoordinator.checkAgentSkills()`

```swift
import Foundation
import Testing
@testable import Roger

struct CommandRunnerTests {
    private let runner = ProcessCommandRunner()
    private let tmp = FileManager.default.temporaryDirectory

    @Test func hangingCommandTimesOut() async {
        let start = Date()
        let result = await runner.run(URL(fileURLWithPath: "/bin/sleep"), ["30"], environment: [:], currentDirectory: tmp, timeout: .milliseconds(500))
        #expect(result.status == .timedOut)
        #expect(Date().timeIntervalSince(start) < 5)
    }

    @Test func stdinIsClosed() async {
        // cat waits for input forever unless stdin is at end of file.
        let result = await runner.run(URL(fileURLWithPath: "/bin/cat"), [], environment: [:], currentDirectory: tmp, timeout: .seconds(5))
        #expect(result.status == .exited(0))
        #expect(result.stdout.isEmpty)
    }

    @Test func passesTheEnvironmentAndFolder() async {
        let result = await runner.run(
            URL(fileURLWithPath: "/bin/sh"), ["-c", "echo \"$PATH\"; pwd -P"],
            environment: ["PATH": "/custom/bin:/usr/bin"], currentDirectory: URL(fileURLWithPath: "/usr"), timeout: .seconds(5)
        )
        #expect(result.stdout == "/custom/bin:/usr/bin\n/usr\n")
    }

    @Test func reportsExitCodeAndStderr() async {
        let result = await runner.run(URL(fileURLWithPath: "/bin/sh"), ["-c", "echo oops >&2; exit 3"], environment: [:], currentDirectory: tmp, timeout: .seconds(5))
        #expect(result.status == .exited(3))
        #expect(result.stderr == "oops\n")
        #expect(!result.succeeded)
    }

    @Test func missingExecutableFailsToStart() async {
        let result = await runner.run(URL(fileURLWithPath: "/nonexistent/claude"), [], environment: [:], currentDirectory: tmp, timeout: .seconds(5))
        guard case .failedToStart = result.status else {
            Issue.record("expected failedToStart, got \(result.status)")
            return
        }
    }

    @Test func searchPathKeepsAbsoluteEntriesAndAddsHomebrew() {

        #expect(CLILocator.searchPath(loginShellPATH: nil) == ["/opt/homebrew/bin", "/usr/local/bin"])
    }
}
```

```swift
import Foundation
import Testing
@testable import Roger

/// Records calls and answers with canned results (the last one repeats).
final class FakeRunner: CommandRunner, @unchecked Sendable {
    private let lock = NSLock()
    private var recorded: [[String]] = []
    private var results: [CommandResult]

    init(_ results: [CommandResult]) { self.results = results }

    var calls: [[String]] { lock.withLock { recorded } }

    func run(_ executable: URL, _ arguments: [String], environment: [String: String], currentDirectory: URL, timeout: Duration) async -> CommandResult {
        lock.withLock {
            recorded.append([executable.lastPathComponent] + arguments)
            return results.count > 1 ? results.removeFirst() : results[0]
        }
    }
}
```
