- [ ] **Step 4: Write the runner**

`Roger/Services/Agents/CommandRunner.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

struct CommandResult: Equatable, Sendable {
    enum Status: Equatable, Sendable {
        case exited(Int32)
        case timedOut
        case failedToStart(String)
    }

    let status: Status
    let stdout: String
    let stderr: String

    var succeeded: Bool { status == .exited(0) }
}

protocol CommandRunner: Sendable {
    func run(_ executable: URL, _ arguments: [String], environment: [String: String], currentDirectory: URL, timeout: Duration) async -> CommandResult
}

/// Collects a pipe's output from its readability handler.
private final class OutputBuffer: @unchecked Sendable {
    private let lock = NSLock()
    private var data = Data()

    func append(_ chunk: Data) { lock.withLock { data.append(chunk) } }
    var text: String { lock.withLock { String(decoding: data, as: UTF8.self) } }
}

/// Runs a harness CLI so that it can never block Roger: stdin is
/// `/dev/null` (a prompt reads end of file), only the given environment is
/// passed, and after `timeout` the process gets SIGTERM, then SIGKILL.
struct ProcessCommandRunner: CommandRunner {
    func run(_ executable: URL, _ arguments: [String], environment: [String: String], currentDirectory: URL, timeout: Duration) async -> CommandResult {
        let process = Process()
        process.executableURL = executable
        process.arguments = arguments
        process.environment = environment
        process.currentDirectoryURL = currentDirectory
        process.standardInput = FileHandle.nullDevice
        let outPipe = Pipe()
        let errPipe = Pipe()
        process.standardOutput = outPipe
        process.standardError = errPipe
        let out = OutputBuffer()
        let err = OutputBuffer()
        outPipe.fileHandleForReading.readabilityHandler = { out.append($0.availableData) }
        errPipe.fileHandleForReading.readabilityHandler = { err.append($0.availableData) }

        let (exits, exitSink) = AsyncStream.makeStream(of: Void.self, bufferingPolicy: .bufferingNewest(1))
        process.terminationHandler = { _ in
            exitSink.yield()
            exitSink.finish()
        }
        do {
            try process.run()
        } catch {
            outPipe.fileHandleForReading.readabilityHandler = nil
            errPipe.fileHandleForReading.readabilityHandler = nil
            return CommandResult(status: .failedToStart(error.localizedDescription), stdout: "", stderr: "")
        }

        let finishedInTime = await Self.wait(for: exits, timeout: timeout)
        if !finishedInTime {
            process.terminate()
            if await Self.wait(for: exits, timeout: .seconds(1)) == false {
                kill(process.processIdentifier, SIGKILL)
                _ = await Self.wait(for: exits, timeout: .seconds(1))
            }
        }
        // Give the handlers a moment for the last bytes, then stop reading.
        // A grandchild may hold the pipe open, so never read to end of file.
        try? await Task.sleep(for: .milliseconds(100))
        outPipe.fileHandleForReading.readabilityHandler = nil
        errPipe.fileHandleForReading.readabilityHandler = nil
        let status: CommandResult.Status = finishedInTime ? .exited(process.terminationStatus) : .timedOut
        return CommandResult(status: status, stdout: out.text, stderr: err.text)
    }

    /// True when the process ended within `timeout`.
    private static func wait(for exits: AsyncStream<Void>, timeout: Duration) async -> Bool {
        await withTaskGroup(of: Bool.self) { group in
            group.addTask {
                for await _ in exits { return true }
                return true
            }
            group.addTask {
                try? await Task.sleep(for: timeout)
                return false
            }
            let first = await group.next() ?? false
            group.cancelAll()
            return first
        }
    }
}

/// Where harness CLIs are looked for: the login shell's `PATH` (a GUI app
/// gets a short one) plus Homebrew's folders.
enum CLILocator {
    static let extraDirectories = ["/opt/homebrew/bin", "/usr/local/bin"]

    static func searchPath(loginShellPATH: String?) -> [String] {
        let fromShell = (loginShellPATH ?? "")
            .split(separator: ":")
            .map(String.init)
            .filter { $0.hasPrefix("/") }
        var seen = Set<String>()
        return (fromShell + extraDirectories).filter { seen.insert($0).inserted }
    }

    static func find(_ name: String, in directories: [String]) -> URL? {
        directories
            .map { URL(fileURLWithPath: $0).appendingPathComponent(name) }
            .first { FileManager.default.isExecutableFile(atPath: $0.path) }
    }

    /// `PATH` as the user's login shell sets it. Nil when the shell fails.
    static func loginShellPATH(runner: any CommandRunner, home: URL) async -> String? {
        let shell = getpwuid(getuid()).flatMap { $0.pointee.pw_shell.map { String(cString: $0) } } ?? "/bin/zsh"
        let result = await runner.run(
            URL(fileURLWithPath: shell),
            ["-l", "-c", "printf '%s' \"$PATH\""],
            environment: ["HOME": home.path, "USER": NSUserName()],
            currentDirectory: home,
            timeout: .seconds(5)
        )
        guard result.succeeded else { return nil }
        // Profile scripts may print first; PATH is the last line.
        return result.stdout.split(separator: "\n").last.map(String.init)
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write registration**

`Roger/Services/Agents/HarnessRegistration.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

struct RegistrationCommand: Equatable, Sendable {
    let cli: String
    let arguments: [String]

    /// The command as a user would type it.
    var display: String {
        ([cli] + arguments).map(McpCommandFormatter.shellQuoted).joined(separator: " ")
    }
}

enum ConfigLookup: Equatable, Sendable {
    case command(String)
    case absent
    case unreadable
}

enum ServerConnection: Equatable, Sendable {
    case connected
    /// Registered, but with another `roger-mcp` path (Roger moved, old copy).
    case otherCommand(String)
    case notConnected
    /// A config file exists but could not be read.
    case unknown
}

/// The CLI commands per harness (checked 2026-10-14, see Verified facts)
/// and the read-only config lookups for the "server connected" state.
enum HarnessRegistration {
    static func add(_ id: HarnessID, executable: String) -> RegistrationCommand? {
        switch id {
        case .claudeCode: return RegistrationCommand(cli: "claude", arguments: ["mcp", "add", "--scope", "user", "roger", "--", executable])
        case .codex: return RegistrationCommand(cli: "codex", arguments: ["mcp", "add", "roger", "--", executable])
        case .gemini: return RegistrationCommand(cli: "gemini", arguments: ["mcp", "add", "--scope", "user", "roger", executable])
        case .copilot: return RegistrationCommand(cli: "copilot", arguments: ["mcp", "add", "roger", "--", executable])
        case .kiro:
            // --command is one string to Kiro; a space would split it.
            guard !executable.contains(" ") else { return nil }
            return RegistrationCommand(cli: "kiro-cli", arguments: ["mcp", "add", "--name", "roger", "--command", executable, "--scope", "global"])
        case .cursor: return nil
        }
    }

    static func remove(_ id: HarnessID) -> RegistrationCommand? {
        switch id {
        case .claudeCode: return RegistrationCommand(cli: "claude", arguments: ["mcp", "remove", "--scope", "user", "roger"])
        case .codex: return RegistrationCommand(cli: "codex", arguments: ["mcp", "remove", "roger"])
        case .gemini: return RegistrationCommand(cli: "gemini", arguments: ["mcp", "remove", "--scope", "user", "roger"])
        case .copilot: return RegistrationCommand(cli: "copilot", arguments: ["mcp", "remove", "roger"])
        case .kiro: return RegistrationCommand(cli: "kiro-cli", arguments: ["mcp", "remove", "--name", "roger", "--scope", "global"])
        case .cursor: return nil
        }
    }

    /// What to paste into the harness's config file by hand.
    static func snippet(_ spec: HarnessSpec, executable: String) -> String {
        if spec.id == .codex {
            return "[mcp_servers.roger]\ncommand = \"\(executable.replacingOccurrences(of: "\\", with: "\\\\").replacingOccurrences(of: "\"", with: "\\\""))\"\n"
        }
        return McpCommandFormatter.jsonSnippet(executable: executable)
    }

    /// Reads (never writes) the harness's config files in order.
    static func connection(_ spec: HarnessSpec, home: URL, executable: String) -> ServerConnection {
        var sawUnreadable = false
        for file in spec.configFiles {
            guard let data = try? Data(contentsOf: home.appendingPathComponent(file)) else { continue }
            let lookup = spec.id == .codex ? tomlCommand(String(decoding: data, as: UTF8.self)) : jsonCommand(data)
            switch lookup {
            case .command(let command):
                return sameExecutable(command, executable) ? .connected : .otherCommand(command)
            case .absent:
                continue
            case .unreadable:
                sawUnreadable = true
            }
        }
        return sawUnreadable ? .unknown : .notConnected
    }

    /// `mcpServers.roger.command` at the top level (user scope).
    static func jsonCommand(_ data: Data) -> ConfigLookup {
        guard let object = try? JSONSerialization.jsonObject(with: data) as? [String: Any] else { return .unreadable }
        guard let servers = object["mcpServers"] as? [String: Any],
              let roger = servers["roger"] as? [String: Any],
              let command = roger["command"] as? String
        else { return .absent }
        return .command(command)
    }

    /// `command = "..."` inside `[mcp_servers.roger]`.
    static func tomlCommand(_ text: String) -> ConfigLookup {
        var inSection = false
        for raw in text.split(separator: "\n", omittingEmptySubsequences: false) {
            let line = raw.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
            if line.hasPrefix("[") {
                inSection = line == "[mcp_servers.roger]"
                continue
            }
            guard inSection else { continue }
            let parts = line.split(separator: "=", maxSplits: 1).map { $0.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces) }
            guard parts.count == 2, parts[0] == "command" else { continue }
            let value = parts[1]
            guard value.count >= 2, let quote = value.first, quote == "\"" || quote == "'", value.last == quote else { return .unreadable }
            let inner = String(value.dropFirst().dropLast())
            return .command(quote == "\"" ? inner.replacingOccurrences(of: "\\\"", with: "\"").replacingOccurrences(of: "\\\\", with: "\\") : inner)
        }
        return .absent
    }

    private static func sameExecutable(_ a: String, _ b: String) -> Bool {
        a == b || URL(fileURLWithPath: a).resolvingSymlinksInPath().path == URL(fileURLWithPath: b).resolvingSymlinksInPath().path
    }
}

struct RegistrationOutcome: Equatable, Sendable {
    enum Kind: Equatable, Sendable {
        case registered
        case alreadyRegistered
        case removed
        /// The user has to do it: `message` says why, `command` is what to copy.
        case manual
    }

    let kind: Kind
    let message: String?
    let command: String?
}

/// Registers and removes the server through the harness CLI. Never edits a
/// config file. Every failure ends in `.manual` with the command to copy.
struct HarnessRegistrar: Sendable {
    let home: URL
    let runner: any CommandRunner
    let searchPath: [String]
    let timeout: Duration

    init(home: URL, runner: any CommandRunner, searchPath: [String], timeout: Duration = .seconds(20)) {
        self.home = home
        self.runner = runner
        self.searchPath = searchPath
        self.timeout = timeout
    }

    func register(_ spec: HarnessSpec, executable: String) async -> RegistrationOutcome {
        let current = HarnessRegistration.connection(spec, home: home, executable: executable)
        if current == .connected {
            return RegistrationOutcome(kind: .alreadyRegistered, message: nil, command: nil)
        }
        guard let add = HarnessRegistration.add(spec.id, executable: executable) else {
            let file = "~/" + (spec.configFiles.first ?? "")
            return RegistrationOutcome(kind: .manual, message: "Add Roger to \(file) yourself:", command: HarnessRegistration.snippet(spec, executable: executable))
        }
        guard let cli = CLILocator.find(add.cli, in: searchPath) else {
            return RegistrationOutcome(kind: .manual, message: "\(add.cli) was not found. Run this in Terminal:", command: add.display)
        }
        if case .otherCommand = current, let remove = HarnessRegistration.remove(spec.id) {
            let removed = await run(cli, remove)
            guard removed.succeeded else {
                return RegistrationOutcome(kind: .manual, message: reason(removed, cli: add.cli), command: remove.display + " && " + add.display)
            }
        }
        let added = await run(cli, add)
        guard added.succeeded else {
            return RegistrationOutcome(kind: .manual, message: reason(added, cli: add.cli), command: add.display)
        }
        return RegistrationOutcome(kind: .registered, message: nil, command: nil)
    }

    func unregister(_ spec: HarnessSpec, executable: String) async -> RegistrationOutcome {
        if HarnessRegistration.connection(spec, home: home, executable: executable) == .notConnected {
            return RegistrationOutcome(kind: .removed, message: nil, command: nil)
        }
        guard let remove = HarnessRegistration.remove(spec.id) else {
            let file = "~/" + (spec.configFiles.first ?? "")
            return RegistrationOutcome(kind: .manual, message: "Remove the roger entry from \(file) yourself.", command: nil)
        }
        guard let cli = CLILocator.find(remove.cli, in: searchPath) else {
            return RegistrationOutcome(kind: .manual, message: "\(remove.cli) was not found. Run this in Terminal:", command: remove.display)
        }
        let removed = await run(cli, remove)
        guard removed.succeeded else {
            return RegistrationOutcome(kind: .manual, message: reason(removed, cli: remove.cli), command: remove.display)
        }
        return RegistrationOutcome(kind: .removed, message: nil, command: nil)
    }

    private func run(_ cli: URL, _ command: RegistrationCommand) async -> CommandResult {
        await runner.run(
            cli,
            command.arguments,
            environment: ["PATH": searchPath.joined(separator: ":"), "HOME": home.path, "USER": NSUserName()],
            currentDirectory: home,
            timeout: timeout
        )
    }

    private func reason(_ result: CommandResult, cli: String) -> String {
        switch result.status {
        case .timedOut:
            return "\(cli) did not answer within \(Int(timeout.components.seconds)) seconds. It may wait for a login or a question. Run this in Terminal:"
        case .failedToStart(let message):
            return "\(cli) could not start (\(message)). Run this in Terminal:"
        case .exited(let code):
            let detail = result.stderr.split(separator: "\n").first.map { ": " + $0 } ?? ""
            return "\(cli) failed with exit code \(code)\(detail). Run this in Terminal:"
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write the controller and the hint rule**

`Roger/Services/Agents/AgentsController.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation
import Observation

enum AgentsHint {
    static var appVersion: String {
        Bundle.main.infoDictionary?["CFBundleShortVersionString"] as? String ?? "0"
    }

    /// One quiet line in the popover while a skill is outdated, until the
    /// user closes it for this app version.
    static func shouldShow(outdated: Bool, dismissedVersion: String?, appVersion: String) -> Bool {
        outdated && dismissedVersion != appVersion
    }
}

/// State and actions of the harness rows in Settings > Agents.
@MainActor
@Observable
final class AgentsController {
    struct Row: Identifiable, Equatable {
        let spec: HarnessSpec
        var plan: HarnessInstallPlan
        var skillState: SkillRowState
        var connection: ServerConnection
        var message: String?
        var manualCommand: String?
        var isBusy = false

        var id: HarnessID { spec.id }
    }

    private(set) var rows: [Row] = []
    let executableChoice: RogerMCPLocation.Choice

    @ObservationIgnored private let home: URL
    @ObservationIgnored let installer: SkillInstaller
    @ObservationIgnored private let runner: any CommandRunner
    @ObservationIgnored private var searchPath: [String]?

    init(home: URL, installer: SkillInstaller, runner: any CommandRunner, appBundle: URL, searchPath: [String]? = nil, links: [String] = RogerMCPLocation.homebrewLinks) {
        self.home = home
        self.installer = installer
        self.runner = runner
        self.searchPath = searchPath
        self.executableChoice = RogerMCPLocation.choose(appBundle: appBundle, links: links)
    }

    /// Nil when this copy of Roger has no bundled skill.
    static func live() -> AgentsController? {
        guard let skill = BundledSkill.load() else { return nil }
        return AgentsController(
            home: FileManager.default.homeDirectoryForCurrentUser,
            installer: .live(skill: skill),
            runner: ProcessCommandRunner(),
            appBundle: Bundle.main.bundleURL
        )
    }

    private var executable: String? {
        if case .usable(let path) = executableChoice { return path }
        return nil
    }

    /// Reads detection, skill states and config files again. Messages stay.
    func refresh() {
        let plans = SkillInstallPlanner.plan(for: HarnessCatalog.detected(home: home), home: home)
        let previous = Dictionary(uniqueKeysWithValues: rows.map { ($0.id, $0) })
        rows = plans.map { plan in
            Row(
                spec: plan.harness,
                plan: plan,
                skillState: installer.state(of: plan.skillFolder),
                connection: executable.map { HarnessRegistration.connection(plan.harness, home: home, executable: $0) } ?? .unknown,
                message: previous[plan.harness.id]?.message,
                manualCommand: previous[plan.harness.id]?.manualCommand
            )
        }
    }

    /// Installs or updates the skill (unless another row covers it), then
    /// registers the server.
    func install(_ id: HarnessID) async {
        guard let row = rows.first(where: { $0.id == id }) else { return }
        setBusy(id, true)
        var notes: [String] = []
        var command: String?
        if row.plan.coveredBy == nil {
            do {
                if case .updated(let backup?) = try installer.install(into: row.plan.skillFolder) {
                    notes.append("Your changed skill was saved as \(backup.lastPathComponent).")
                }
            } catch {
                notes.append(error.localizedDescription)
            }
        }
        if let executable {
            let outcome = await registrar().register(row.spec, executable: executable)
            if outcome.kind == .manual {
                notes.append(outcome.message ?? "")
                command = outcome.command
            }
        } else if case .unusable(let reason) = executableChoice {
            notes.append(reason)
        }
        finish(id, message: notes.isEmpty ? nil : notes.joined(separator: " "), command: command)
    }

    /// False when a changed skill needs a confirmation first; call again
    /// with `confirmChanged: true` after the user agreed.
    func uninstall(_ id: HarnessID, confirmChanged: Bool) async -> Bool {
        guard let row = rows.first(where: { $0.id == id }) else { return true }
        var notes: [String] = []
        var command: String?
        if row.plan.coveredBy == nil {
            do {
                if try installer.uninstall(from: row.plan.skillFolder, confirmChanged: confirmChanged) == .needsConfirmation {
                    return false
                }
            } catch {
                notes.append(error.localizedDescription)
            }
        }
        setBusy(id, true)
        if let executable {
            let outcome = await registrar().unregister(row.spec, executable: executable)
            if outcome.kind == .manual {
                notes.append(outcome.message ?? "")
                command = outcome.command
            }
        }
        finish(id, message: notes.isEmpty ? nil : notes.joined(separator: " "), command: command)
        return true
    }

    // MARK: - Helpers

    private func registrar() async -> HarnessRegistrar {
        if searchPath == nil {
            let path = await CLILocator.loginShellPATH(runner: runner, home: home)
            searchPath = CLILocator.searchPath(loginShellPATH: path)
        }
        return HarnessRegistrar(home: home, runner: runner, searchPath: searchPath ?? CLILocator.extraDirectories)
    }

    private func setBusy(_ id: HarnessID, _ busy: Bool) {
        if let index = rows.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) { rows[index].isBusy = busy }
    }

    private func finish(_ id: HarnessID, message: String?, command: String?) {
        refresh()
        if let index = rows.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) {
            rows[index].message = message
            rows[index].manualCommand = command
            rows[index].isBusy = false
        }
    }
}
```

```swift
    private var harnessesCard: some View {
        settingsCard(icon: "terminal", title: "Coding agents") {
            if let agents {
                if case .unusable(let reason) = agents.executableChoice {
                    Text(reason).foregroundStyle(.orange)
                }
                if agents.rows.isEmpty {
                    Text("No coding agent found. Roger looks for Claude Code, Codex, Gemini CLI, Cursor, Copilot CLI and Kiro in your home folder.")
                        .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
                }
                ForEach(agents.rows) { row in
                    harnessRow(row, agents: agents)
                    if row.id != agents.rows.last?.id { Divider() }
                }
            } else {
                Text("This copy of Roger has no ingest skill.")
                    .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
            }
        }
    }

    private func harnessRow(_ row: AgentsController.Row, agents: AgentsController) -> some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 6) {
            HStack(alignment: .firstTextBaseline) {
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 2) {
                    Text(row.spec.name).font(.body.weight(.medium))
                    Text(skillText(row) + " · " + serverText(row.connection))
                        .font(.caption)
                        .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
                }
                Spacer()
                if row.isBusy {
                    ProgressView().controlSize(.small)
                } else {
                    if let label = primaryAction(row) {
                        Button(label) {
                            Task {
                                await agents.install(row.id)
                                coordinator.checkAgentSkills()
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    if row.skillState != .notInstalled || row.connection != .notConnected {
                        Button("Uninstall") {
                            Task {
                                if await agents.uninstall(row.id, confirmChanged: false) == false {
                                    confirmUninstall = row.id
                                }
                                coordinator.checkAgentSkills()
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            if let message = row.message {
                Text(message).font(.caption).foregroundStyle(.orange)
            }
            if let command = row.manualCommand {
                copyableBlock(command)
            }
        }
    }

    private func skillText(_ row: AgentsController.Row) -> String {
        if let cover = row.plan.coveredBy {
            return "Skill covered by \(HarnessCatalog.spec(cover).name)"
        }
        switch row.skillState {
        case .notInstalled: return "Skill not installed"
        case .upToDate: return "Skill up to date"
        case .outdated: return "Skill outdated"
        case .changedByYou: return "Skill changed by you"
        }
    }

    private func serverText(_ connection: ServerConnection) -> String {
        switch connection {
        case .connected: return "Server connected"
        case .otherCommand: return "Server points to another roger-mcp"
        case .notConnected: return "Server not connected"
        case .unknown: return "Server state unknown"
        }
    }

    /// "Install" for a fresh row, "Update" when something is out of date,
    /// nothing when all is well.
    private func primaryAction(_ row: AgentsController.Row) -> String? {
        let skillOK = row.plan.coveredBy != nil || row.skillState == .upToDate
        if row.skillState == .notInstalled, row.plan.coveredBy == nil, row.connection == .notConnected { return "Install" }
        if !skillOK || row.connection != .connected { return "Update" }
        return nil
    }
```

### Task 14: Claude Desktop extension and skill zip

Roger builds `roger.mcpb` at runtime and hands it to Claude Desktop, and writes `ingest-roger.zip` for the skill upload. Roger cannot read Desktop's state, so the row shows when each step was last done and warns when Roger.app moved since.

**Files:**
- Create: `Roger/Services/Agents/DesktopExtensionBuilder.swift`
- Modify: `Roger/Services/Agents/AgentsController.swift` (Desktop state and actions)
- Modify: `Roger/UI/AgentsSettingsView.swift` (Claude Desktop card)
- Test: `Roger/RogerTests/DesktopExtensionBuilderTests.swift`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `BundledSkill` (Task 11); `RogerMCPLocation`, `McpCommandFormatter` (Task 10); `AgentsController` (Task 13).
- Produces:
  - `struct MCPBManifest: Codable, Equatable` (fields below, encoded with MCPB 0.3 key names)
  - `protocol Archiver: Sendable { func zip(_ source: URL, keepParent: Bool, to destination: URL) throws }`, `struct DittoArchiver: Archiver`
  - `enum DesktopExtensionBuilder { static let manifestVersion = "0.3"; static let entryPoint = "server/roger-mcp"; static func manifest(executable: String, version: String) -> MCPBManifest; static func launcher(executable: String) -> String; static func buildExtension(executable: String, version: String, iconPNG: Data, in folder: URL, archiver: any Archiver) throws -> URL; static func buildSkillZip(_ skill: BundledSkill, in folder: URL, archiver: any Archiver) throws -> URL; static func iconPNG(for appBundle: URL, size: Int = 256) -> Data? }`
  - `struct DesktopRecord: Codable, Equatable, Sendable { var extensionCommand: String?; var extensionAddedAt: Date?; var skillSavedAt: Date?; func appMoved(currentExecutable: String) -> Bool }`, `struct DesktopRecordStore: Sendable { let fileURL: URL; func load() -> DesktopRecord; func save(_ record: DesktopRecord) throws; static var live: DesktopRecordStore }`
  - `AgentsController`: `var desktopAvailable: Bool`, `private(set) var desktopRecord: DesktopRecord`, `var desktopMessage: String?`, `func addToClaudeDesktop() async`, `func saveSkillForClaudeDesktop()`

```swift
import AppKit
import Foundation
import Testing
@testable import Roger

struct DesktopExtensionBuilderTests {
    private let exe = "/Applications/Roger.app/Contents/MacOS/roger-mcp"

    private func zipEntries(_ zip: URL) throws -> [String] {
        try run("/usr/bin/zipinfo", ["-1", zip.path]).split(separator: "\n").map(String.init).sorted()
    }

    private func run(_ tool: String, _ arguments: [String]) throws -> String {
        let process = Process()
        process.executableURL = URL(fileURLWithPath: tool)
        process.arguments = arguments
        let out = Pipe()
        process.standardOutput = out
        try process.run()
        let data = out.fileHandleForReading.readDataToEndOfFile()
        process.waitUntilExit()
        return String(decoding: data, as: UTF8.self)
    }

    private func pngIcon() throws -> Data {
        try #require(DesktopExtensionBuilder.iconPNG(for: Bundle.main.bundleURL))
    }

    @Test func manifestFollowsMCPB() throws {
        let data = try JSONEncoder().encode(DesktopExtensionBuilder.manifest(executable: exe, version: "0.25.0"))
        let json = try #require(try JSONSerialization.jsonObject(with: data) as? [String: Any])
        #expect(json["manifest_version"] as? String == "0.3")
        #expect(json["name"] as? String == "roger")
        #expect(json["version"] as? String == "0.25.0")
        #expect(json["icon"] as? String == "icon.png")
        #expect((json["author"] as? [String: Any])?["name"] as? String == "Jordi Böhme")
        let server = try #require(json["server"] as? [String: Any])
        #expect(server["type"] as? String == "binary")
        #expect(server["entry_point"] as? String == "server/roger-mcp")
        let config = try #require(server["mcp_config"] as? [String: Any])
        #expect(config["command"] as? String == exe)
        #expect((config["args"] as? [String])?.isEmpty == true)
        #expect((json["compatibility"] as? [String: Any])?["platforms"] as? [String] == ["darwin"])
        #expect((json["tools"] as? [[String: Any]])?.compactMap { $0["name"] as? String }.sorted() == ["ack", "get_changes", "get_item", "list_items", "search", "status"])
        let text = String(decoding: data, as: UTF8.self)
        #expect(!text.contains("\u{2014}") && !text.contains("\u{2013}"))
    }

    @Test func launcherExecsTheQuotedPath() {
        #expect(DesktopExtensionBuilder.launcher(executable: "/Users/x/My Apps/Roger.app/Contents/MacOS/roger-mcp")
                == "#!/bin/sh\nexec '/Users/x/My Apps/Roger.app/Contents/MacOS/roger-mcp' \"$@\"\n")
    }

    @Test func extensionZipHasTheBundleLayout() throws {
        let folder = try TempFolder.make()
        defer { TempFolder.remove(folder) }
        let mcpb = try DesktopExtensionBuilder.buildExtension(executable: exe, version: "0.25.0", iconPNG: pngIcon(), in: folder, archiver: DittoArchiver())
        #expect(mcpb.lastPathComponent == "roger.mcpb")
        #expect(try zipEntries(mcpb) == ["icon.png", "manifest.json", "server/", "server/roger-mcp"])
        let listing = try run("/usr/bin/zipinfo", [mcpb.path, "server/roger-mcp"])
        #expect(listing.hasPrefix("-rwxr-xr-x"))
        let iconProcess = Process()
        iconProcess.executableURL = URL(fileURLWithPath: "/usr/bin/unzip")
        iconProcess.arguments = ["-p", mcpb.path, "icon.png"]
        let iconPipe = Pipe()
        iconProcess.standardOutput = iconPipe
        try iconProcess.run()
        let iconData = iconPipe.fileHandleForReading.readDataToEndOfFile()
        iconProcess.waitUntilExit()
        #expect(Array(iconData.prefix(4)) == [0x89, 0x50, 0x4E, 0x47])
        // Building again replaces Roger's own temp bundle.
        _ = try DesktopExtensionBuilder.buildExtension(executable: exe, version: "0.25.1", iconPNG: pngIcon(), in: folder, archiver: DittoArchiver())
        #expect(try run("/usr/bin/unzip", ["-p", mcpb.path, "manifest.json"]).contains("0.25.1"))
    }

    @Test func skillZipHoldsTheSkillFolder() throws {
        let folder = try TempFolder.make()
        defer { TempFolder.remove(folder) }
        let skill = BundledSkill(content: Data("---\nname: ingest-roger\n---\n".utf8), version: "0.25.0")
        let zip = try DesktopExtensionBuilder.buildSkillZip(skill, in: folder, archiver: DittoArchiver())
        #expect(zip.lastPathComponent == "ingest-roger.zip")
        #expect(try zipEntries(zip) == ["ingest-roger/", "ingest-roger/SKILL.md"])
        #expect(try run("/usr/bin/unzip", ["-p", zip.path, "ingest-roger/SKILL.md"]) == "---\nname: ingest-roger\n---\n")
        // A second save never overwrites the first file in Downloads.
        let second = try DesktopExtensionBuilder.buildSkillZip(skill, in: folder, archiver: DittoArchiver())
        #expect(second.lastPathComponent == "ingest-roger 1.zip")

    @Test func movedAppShowsWarning() {
        var record = DesktopRecord()
        #expect(!record.appMoved(currentExecutable: exe))
        record.extensionCommand = exe
        #expect(!record.appMoved(currentExecutable: exe))
        #expect(record.appMoved(currentExecutable: "/Users/x/Applications/Roger.app/Contents/MacOS/roger-mcp"))

    @Test func recordRoundTrips() throws {
        let folder = try TempFolder.make()
        defer { TempFolder.remove(folder) }
        let store = DesktopRecordStore(fileURL: folder.appendingPathComponent("claude-desktop.json"))
        #expect(store.load() == DesktopRecord())

        try store.save(record)
        #expect(store.load() == record)
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the builder**

`Roger/Services/Agents/DesktopExtensionBuilder.swift`:

```swift
import AppKit
import Foundation

/// MCPB manifest, version 0.3 (github.com/modelcontextprotocol/mcpb,
/// `@anthropic-ai/mcpb` 2.1.2). The schema is strict: only these keys.
struct MCPBManifest: Codable, Equatable {
    struct Author: Codable, Equatable {
        let name: String
        let url: String
    }

    struct Config: Codable, Equatable {
        let command: String
        let args: [String]
        let env: [String: String]
    }

    struct Server: Codable, Equatable {
        let type: String
        let entryPoint: String
        let mcpConfig: Config

        enum CodingKeys: String, CodingKey {
            case type
            case entryPoint = "entry_point"
            case mcpConfig = "mcp_config"
        }
    }

    struct Compatibility: Codable, Equatable {
        let platforms: [String]
    }

    struct ToolEntry: Codable, Equatable {
        let name: String
        let description: String
    }

    let manifestVersion: String
    let name: String
    let displayName: String
    let version: String
    let description: String
    let author: Author
    let homepage: String
    let license: String
    let icon: String
    let server: Server
    let tools: [ToolEntry]
    let compatibility: Compatibility

    enum CodingKeys: String, CodingKey {
        case manifestVersion = "manifest_version"
        case name
        case displayName = "display_name"
        case version
        case description
        case author
        case homepage
        case license
        case icon
        case server
        case tools
        case compatibility
    }
}

protocol Archiver: Sendable {
    /// Zips `source` into `destination`. `keepParent` puts the folder itself
    /// in the zip; otherwise its contents are at the root.
    func zip(_ source: URL, keepParent: Bool, to destination: URL) throws
}

struct ArchiverError: Error, LocalizedError {
    let status: Int32
    var errorDescription: String? { "Could not create the zip file (ditto exit code \(status))." }
}

/// `/usr/bin/ditto`, which keeps the executable bit and adds no resource
/// forks or extended attributes.
struct DittoArchiver: Archiver {
    func zip(_ source: URL, keepParent: Bool, to destination: URL) throws {
        let process = Process()
        process.executableURL = URL(fileURLWithPath: "/usr/bin/ditto")
        process.arguments = ["-c", "-k", "--norsrc", "--noextattr", "--noqtn", "--noacl"]
            + (keepParent ? ["--keepParent"] : [])
            + [source.path, destination.path]
        process.standardInput = FileHandle.nullDevice
        try process.run()
        process.waitUntilExit()
        guard process.terminationStatus == 0 else { throw ArchiverError(status: process.terminationStatus) }
    }
}

enum DesktopExtensionBuilder {
    static let manifestVersion = "0.3"
    static let entryPoint = "server/roger-mcp"

    static func manifest(executable: String, version: String) -> MCPBManifest {
        MCPBManifest(
            manifestVersion: manifestVersion,
            name: "roger",
            displayName: "Roger",
            version: version,
            description: "Log entries, meetings and file transcripts from Roger, with a change list so nothing is read twice.",
            author: .init(name: "Jordi Böhme", url: "https://github.com/jordiboehme"),
            homepage: "https://github.com/jordiboehme/roger",
            license: "MIT",
            icon: "icon.png",
            server: .init(
                type: "binary",
                entryPoint: entryPoint,
                mcpConfig: .init(command: executable, args: [], env: [:])
            ),
            tools: [
                .init(name: "status", description: "Library folder, item counts and consumers."),
                .init(name: "get_changes", description: "What changed since a consumer's bookmark, a time or a seq."),
                .init(name: "ack", description: "Moves a consumer's bookmark forward."),
                .init(name: "get_item", description: "One item as Markdown with its file paths."),
                .init(name: "list_items", description: "Current items, newest first."),
                .init(name: "search", description: "Items that contain every word of a query."),
            ],
            compatibility: .init(platforms: ["darwin"])
        )
    }

    /// The bundle's entry point: runs the `roger-mcp` inside Roger.app. The
    /// MCPB validator wants an executable file at `entry_point`.
    static func launcher(executable: String) -> String {
        "#!/bin/sh\nexec \(McpCommandFormatter.shellQuoted(executable)) \"$@\"\n"
    }

    /// `<folder>/roger.mcpb`, replaced when it exists (it is Roger's own temp file).
    static func buildExtension(executable: String, version: String, iconPNG: Data, in folder: URL, archiver: any Archiver) throws -> URL {
        let fm = FileManager.default
        let stage = folder.appendingPathComponent("roger-mcpb-\(UUID().uuidString)", isDirectory: true)
        defer { try? fm.removeItem(at: stage) }
        let server = stage.appendingPathComponent("server", isDirectory: true)
        try fm.createDirectory(at: server, withIntermediateDirectories: true)

        let encoder = JSONEncoder()
        encoder.outputFormatting = [.prettyPrinted, .sortedKeys, .withoutEscapingSlashes]
        try encoder.encode(manifest(executable: executable, version: version)).write(to: stage.appendingPathComponent("manifest.json"))
        try iconPNG.write(to: stage.appendingPathComponent("icon.png"))
        let launcherURL = server.appendingPathComponent("roger-mcp")
        try Data(launcher(executable: executable).utf8).write(to: launcherURL)
        try fm.setAttributes([.posixPermissions: 0o755], ofItemAtPath: launcherURL.path)

        let output = folder.appendingPathComponent("roger.mcpb")
        if fm.fileExists(atPath: output.path) { try fm.removeItem(at: output) }
        try archiver.zip(stage, keepParent: false, to: output)
        return output
    }

    /// `<folder>/ingest-roger.zip` holding `ingest-roger/SKILL.md`. Never
    /// overwrites: `ingest-roger 1.zip`, `ingest-roger 2.zip` …
    static func buildSkillZip(_ skill: BundledSkill, in folder: URL, archiver: any Archiver) throws -> URL {
        let fm = FileManager.default
        let stage = fm.temporaryDirectory.appendingPathComponent("roger-skill-\(UUID().uuidString)", isDirectory: true)
        defer { try? fm.removeItem(at: stage) }
        let skillFolder = stage.appendingPathComponent(BundledSkill.name, isDirectory: true)
        try fm.createDirectory(at: skillFolder, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        try skill.content.write(to: skillFolder.appendingPathComponent("SKILL.md"))

        var output = folder.appendingPathComponent("\(BundledSkill.name).zip")
        var counter = 1
        while fm.fileExists(atPath: output.path) {
            output = folder.appendingPathComponent("\(BundledSkill.name) \(counter).zip")
            counter += 1
        }
        try archiver.zip(skillFolder, keepParent: true, to: output)
        return output
    }

    /// The app icon as a `size` x `size` PNG.
    static func iconPNG(for appBundle: URL, size: Int = 256) -> Data? {
        let image = NSWorkspace.shared.icon(forFile: appBundle.path)
        guard let rep = NSBitmapImageRep(
            bitmapDataPlanes: nil, pixelsWide: size, pixelsHigh: size, bitsPerSample: 8,
            samplesPerPixel: 4, hasAlpha: true, isPlanar: false, colorSpaceName: .deviceRGB,
            bytesPerRow: 0, bitsPerPixel: 0
        ) else { return nil }
        rep.size = NSSize(width: size, height: size)
        NSGraphicsContext.saveGraphicsState()
        NSGraphicsContext.current = NSGraphicsContext(bitmapImageRep: rep)
        image.draw(in: NSRect(x: 0, y: 0, width: size, height: size))
        NSGraphicsContext.restoreGraphicsState()
        return rep.representation(using: .png, properties: [:])
    }
}

/// What Roger knows about Claude Desktop: only what it did itself.
struct DesktopRecord: Codable, Equatable, Sendable {
    var extensionCommand: String?
    var extensionAddedAt: Date?
    var skillSavedAt: Date?

    /// The extension points at another `roger-mcp` than the running app's.
    func appMoved(currentExecutable: String) -> Bool {
        guard let extensionCommand else { return false }
        return extensionCommand != currentExecutable
    }
}

/// `~/Library/Application Support/Roger/claude-desktop.json`.
struct DesktopRecordStore: Sendable {
    let fileURL: URL

    static var live: DesktopRecordStore {
        let support = FileManager.default.urls(for: .applicationSupportDirectory, in: .userDomainMask).first
            ?? URL(fileURLWithPath: NSHomeDirectory()).appendingPathComponent("Library/Application Support")
        return DesktopRecordStore(fileURL: support.appendingPathComponent("Roger/claude-desktop.json"))
    }

    func load() -> DesktopRecord {
        let decoder = JSONDecoder()
        decoder.dateDecodingStrategy = .iso8601
        guard let data = try? Data(contentsOf: fileURL), let record = try? decoder.decode(DesktopRecord.self, from: data) else {
            return DesktopRecord()
        }
        return record
    }

    func save(_ record: DesktopRecord) throws {
        try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: fileURL.deletingLastPathComponent(), withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        let encoder = JSONEncoder()
        encoder.dateEncodingStrategy = .iso8601
        encoder.outputFormatting = [.prettyPrinted, .sortedKeys]
        try encoder.encode(record).write(to: fileURL, options: .atomic)
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Desktop actions in the controller**

Add to `AgentsController` (`Roger/Services/Agents/AgentsController.swift`), with `import AppKit` at the top:

```swift
    /// Where Claude Desktop lives; the row shows only when it exists.
    @ObservationIgnored var claudeAppURL = URL(fileURLWithPath: "/Applications/Claude.app")
    @ObservationIgnored var desktopStore = DesktopRecordStore.live
    @ObservationIgnored var downloadsFolder = FileManager.default.urls(for: .downloadsDirectory, in: .userDomainMask).first
        ?? FileManager.default.homeDirectoryForCurrentUser.appendingPathComponent("Downloads")
    @ObservationIgnored var appBundle = Bundle.main.bundleURL

    private(set) var desktopRecord = DesktopRecord()
    var desktopMessage: String?

    var desktopAvailable: Bool {
        FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: claudeAppURL.path)
    }

    /// Desktop gets the bundle's own binary, never a Homebrew link.
    private var desktopExecutable: String? {
        if case .unusable = executableChoice { return nil }
        return RogerMCPLocation.bundled(in: appBundle).path
    }

    func refreshDesktop() {
        desktopRecord = desktopStore.load()
    }

    func desktopAppMoved() -> Bool {
        guard let desktopExecutable else { return false }
        return desktopRecord.appMoved(currentExecutable: desktopExecutable)
    }

    /// Builds `roger.mcpb` in a temp folder and opens it with Claude
    /// Desktop, which shows its install dialog. Same name means an update.
    func addToClaudeDesktop() async {
        guard let executable = desktopExecutable else {
            if case .unusable(let reason) = executableChoice { desktopMessage = reason }
            return
        }
        do {
            let folder = FileManager.default.temporaryDirectory.appendingPathComponent("Roger-mcpb", isDirectory: true)
            try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: folder, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
            guard let icon = DesktopExtensionBuilder.iconPNG(for: appBundle) else {
                desktopMessage = "Roger could not draw its icon for the extension."
                return
            }
            let mcpb = try DesktopExtensionBuilder.buildExtension(
                executable: executable, version: AgentsHint.appVersion, iconPNG: icon, in: folder, archiver: DittoArchiver()
            )
            _ = try await NSWorkspace.shared.open([mcpb], withApplicationAt: claudeAppURL, configuration: NSWorkspace.OpenConfiguration())
            var record = desktopStore.load()
            record.extensionCommand = executable
            record.extensionAddedAt = Date()
            try desktopStore.save(record)
            desktopMessage = "Claude Desktop shows its install dialog. Confirm it there."
        } catch {
            desktopMessage = error.localizedDescription
        }
        refreshDesktop()
    }

    /// Writes `ingest-roger.zip` to Downloads and shows it in Finder.
    func saveSkillForClaudeDesktop() {
        do {
            let zip = try DesktopExtensionBuilder.buildSkillZip(installer.skill, in: downloadsFolder, archiver: DittoArchiver())
            NSWorkspace.shared.activateFileViewerSelecting([zip])
            var record = desktopStore.load()
            record.skillSavedAt = Date()
            try desktopStore.save(record)
            desktopMessage = "Upload \(zip.lastPathComponent) in Claude > Settings > Capabilities > Skills."
        } catch {
            desktopMessage = error.localizedDescription
        }
        refreshDesktop()
    }
```

In `init(home:installer:runner:appBundle:searchPath:links:)`, add `self.appBundle = appBundle` as the last line, so tests and the Desktop actions use the same app.

Changelog notes:

- **swift-sdk 0.11.0 to 0.12.1** (new for Roger, so nothing breaks): 0.12.0 brought the 2025-11-25 protocol review fixes, client authorization (SEP-990, SEP-1046) and a NetworkTransport race fix; 0.12.1 auth improvements and general fixes. Nothing touches `StdioTransport` servers. Useful later: `Tool.icons`, `outputSchema` and `structuredContent` (not used in 0.25.0, all output is text by spec). Supported protocol versions: 2025-11-25, 2025-06-18, 2025-03-26, 2024-11-05 (`Sources/MCP/Base/Versioning.swift`). Note: its `Package.swift` (tools 6.1) depends on `swift-docc-plugin` at `branch: main`; resolving 0.12.1 as a version dependency still works (checked with `swift build` in a probe package and with an XcodeGen probe project). It also pulls swift-system, swift-log, eventsource and swift-nio, linked into `roger-mcp` only.
- **mcpb 2.1.x**: `LATEST_MANIFEST_VERSION = "0.4"`, `DEFAULT_MANIFEST_VERSION = "0.3"` (`src/shared/constants.ts`); 0.4 differs from 0.3 only by the `uv` server type (`diff src/schemas/0.3.ts src/schemas/0.4.ts`). Schemas are strict objects (no extra keys). The validator requires `server.entry_point` to exist in the bundle and, for `binary`, to be executable (`src/node/validate.ts`); `mcp_config.command` is any string.
- **FluidAudio and KeyboardShortcuts**: already at their latest releases, so there is no changelog range to read and no bump task. Recommendation for Jordi: none for 0.25.0.

### swift-sdk 0.12.1 API (read from the tagged sources and built)

- Product `MCP`, package identity `swift-sdk` (`Package.swift`).
- `Server.init(name:version:title:instructions:capabilities:configuration:)`; `instructions` is sent in the initialize result (`Server.swift:187`, `:953`).
- `server.withMethodHandler(ListTools.self) { _ in .init(tools: [...]) }`, `server.withMethodHandler(CallTool.self) { params in .init(content: [...], isError: ...) }`; `params.name`, `params.arguments: [String: Value]?` (`Tools.swift`).
- `Tool(name:title:description:inputSchema:annotations:outputSchema:icons:_meta:)`; `Tool.Annotations(title:readOnlyHint:destructiveHint:idempotentHint:openWorldHint:)`.
- `Tool.Content.text(text:annotations:_meta:)` plus the helper `.text(_:metadata:)`.
- `StdioTransport(input:output:logger:)` with a no-op logger by default (nothing on stdout but MCP); `server.start(transport:)`, `server.waitUntilCompleted()` (returns when stdin closes), `server.stop()`.
- Probe server (`swift build` against 0.12.1, then JSON-RPC over a pipe): initialize returned `serverInfo`, `instructions` and protocol 2025-06-18; `tools/list` returned the annotations; responses came back out of order (id 3 before id 2), so tests match by id; the process exited 0 after stdin closed.

### XcodeGen 2.46.0 and Xcode 27 (probe project)

- `type: library.static` target, `type: tool` target, `type: bundle.unit-test` without a host (no `TEST_HOST` is generated when the dependency is not an application); a top-level `schemes:` entry runs it with `xcodebuild test -scheme <name>`.
- The app embeds the tool with `dependencies: - target: roger-mcp, embed: true, codeSign: true, copy: { destination: executables }`; it lands in `Contents/MacOS`.
- `@_exported import RogerKit` in one app file makes RogerKit types visible to a hosted test target through `@testable import <App>` (probe test passed).
- `Bundle.main.executableURL` is the symlink path when started through a link; `resolvingSymlinksInPath()` gives the bundle path.
- Ruling, not probed: `SKIP_INSTALL: YES` on the tool keeps `xcodebuild archive` an app archive (standard Xcode behavior for nested tools). The release workflow's verify step fails loudly if the nested binary is missing or unsigned.

`claude --help` (2.1.296): `--allowedTools, --allowed-tools <tools...>` takes a comma or space separated list; `-p/--print` for non-interactive runs.

### Claude Desktop

- MCPB README: "open the file with Claude for macOS and Windows to show an installation dialog". Roger opens `roger.mcpb` with `/Applications/Claude.app`. Same `name` means an update (Crystalline engram, from the Crystalline 0.25.2 release).
- A Roger-shaped manifest (absolute `mcp_config.command`, `server.type` `binary`) passed `npx -y @anthropic-ai/mcpb@2.1.2 validate`.
- `/usr/bin/ditto -c -k --norsrc --noextattr --noqtn --noacl` keeps `-rwxr-xr-x` on the launcher; without `--keepParent` the manifest is at the zip root, with `--keepParent` the skill zip holds `ingest-roger/SKILL.md` (checked with `zipinfo`).
- Skill upload: a zip that contains the skill folder (not the files at the root), uploaded under Settings > Capabilities (support.claude.com article 12512180, claude.com/docs/skills/how-to). One Anthropic page calls the menu Settings > Features; Roger's hint says Capabilities as the spec does.

### Not verifiable from here (decided by a ruling or a manual step)

- Whether Claude Desktop installs and starts a binary extension whose `command` points outside the bundle: manual step in Task 14 with a fallback (Ruling 30).
- Which app macOS asks for Documents access when Claude Desktop starts `roger-mcp`: documented as "the app that runs the agent"; the error text covers any parent app.
- Kiro's global MCP file name: both read (Ruling 19, Task 13 `connectionComparesTheCommand`).
- Codex, Gemini CLI, Copilot CLI and Kiro are not installed on this Mac, so their CLIs were not run; their syntax comes from their sources and docs above. The registration flow is tested with a fake runner.
